// The standard library runtime. This file joins the generated program's main
// package, which defines the list type L, the option, result and step types
// and the failure helpers used here. Enumerables arrive as iterators: lists,
// maps, sets, ranges and streams all look the same to these functions.

type pair[A, B any] = struct {
	E0 A
	E1 B
}

type triple[A, B, C any] = struct {
	E0 A
	E1 B
	E2 C
}

// ---- Sources ----

func seqList[T any](l L[T]) iter.Seq[T] {
	return func(yield func(T) bool) {
		for i := len(l.S) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
			if !yield(l.S[i]) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func seqMap[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], less func(K, K) bool) iter.Seq[pair[K, V]] {
	return func(yield func(pair[K, V]) bool) {
		keys, values := actor.MapSorted(m, less)
		for i, key := range keys {
			if !yield(pair[K, V]{key, values[i]}) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func seqSet[K comparable](m actor.Map[K, struct{}], less func(K, K) bool) iter.Seq[K] {
	return func(yield func(K) bool) {
		for _, key := range sortedKeys(m, less) {
			if !yield(key) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

// collect drains an iterator into a slice in iteration order.
func collect[T any](s iter.Seq[T]) []T {
	var out []T
	s(func(x T) bool { out = append(out, x); return true })
	return out
}

func toList[T any](s iter.Seq[T]) L[T] { return mk(collect(s)) }

// rangeSeq is first..last: ascending, or descending when first > last.
func rangeSeq(ctx *actor.Context, first, last int64) iter.Seq[int64] {
	step := int64(1)
	if first > last {
		step = -1
	}
	return rangeBy(first, last, step)
}

// rangeStep is first..last//step; it is empty when step points away from last.
func rangeStep(ctx *actor.Context, first, last, step int64, where string) iter.Seq[int64] {
	if step == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "range step cannot be zero")
	}
	return rangeBy(first, last, step)
}

// rangeOf makes the Range first..last//step; a step of 0 means 1, or -1
// when last is below first.
func rangeOf(ctx *actor.Context, first, last, step int64, where string) TRange {
	if step == 0 {
		step = 1
		if first > last {
			step = -1
		}
	}
	return TRange{first, last, step}
}

func seqRange(r TRange) iter.Seq[int64] { return rangeBy(r.F0, r.F1, r.F2) }

func rangeNew(ctx *actor.Context, first, last int64, where string) TRange {
	return TRange{first, last, 1}
}

func rangeNewStep(ctx *actor.Context, first, last, step int64, where string) TRange {
	if step == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "a range's step cannot be 0")
	}
	return TRange{first, last, step}
}

// rangeSize is how many integers a range holds.
func rangeSize(r TRange) int64 {
	if r.F2 > 0 && r.F1 < r.F0 || r.F2 < 0 && r.F1 > r.F0 {
		return 0
	}
	return (r.F1-r.F0)/r.F2 + 1
}

// rangeMembers is the first and last integers a range holds, low first.
func rangeBounds(r TRange) (int64, int64, bool) {
	if rangeSize(r) == 0 {
		return 0, 0, false
	}
	last := r.F0 + (rangeSize(r)-1)*r.F2
	return min(r.F0, last), max(r.F0, last), true
}

func rangeMember(r TRange, n int64) bool {
	low, high, ok := rangeBounds(r)
	return ok && n >= low && n <= high && (n-r.F0)%r.F2 == 0
}

// rangeDisjoint reports whether two ranges hold no integer in common.
func rangeDisjoint(a, b TRange) bool {
	if rangeSize(a) > rangeSize(b) {
		a, b = b, a
	}
	if rangeSize(a) > 1<<16 {
		lowA, highA, _ := rangeBounds(a)
		lowB, highB, _ := rangeBounds(b)
		if highA < lowB || highB < lowA {
			return true
		}
		if (a.F2 == 1 || a.F2 == -1) && (b.F2 == 1 || b.F2 == -1) {
			return false
		}
	}
	for n := range rangeBy(a.F0, a.F1, a.F2) {
		if rangeMember(b, n) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// rangeShift moves a range by a number of its steps.
func rangeShift(r TRange, steps int64) TRange {
	return TRange{r.F0 + steps*r.F2, r.F1 + steps*r.F2, r.F2}
}

func rangeSplit(r TRange, n int64) pair[TRange, TRange] {
	size := rangeSize(r)
	if n < 0 {
		n = max(size+n, 0)
	}
	n = min(n, size)
	cut := r.F0 + n*r.F2
	return pair[TRange, TRange]{TRange{r.F0, cut - r.F2, r.F2}, TRange{cut, r.F1, r.F2}}
}

func rangeToList(r TRange) L[int64] { return toList(seqRange(r)) }

// sliceIndexes turns a range of indexes into a list's, counting negative
// ones from the end, as Enum.slice and String.slice do.
func sliceIndexes(r TRange, size int64) (start, count, step int64) {
	first, last := r.F0, r.F1
	if first < 0 {
		first += size
	}
	if last < 0 {
		last += size
	}
	step = r.F2
	if step == -1 {
		// first..last with last below first, such as 1..-2, slices forwards
		// once its negative indexes count from the end, as in Elixir.
		step = 1
	}
	if step < 0 || first < 0 || first >= size || last < first {
		return 0, 0, 1
	}
	last = min(last, size-1)
	return first, last - first + 1, step
}

func enumSliceRange[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], r TRange, where string) L[A] {
	items := collect(s)
	start, count, step := sliceIndexes(r, int64(len(items)))
	var out []A
	for i := int64(0); i < count; i += step {
		out = append(out, items[start+i])
	}
	return mk(out)
}

func strSliceRange(ctx *actor.Context, s string, r TRange, where string) string {
	clusters := graphemes(s)
	start, count, step := sliceIndexes(r, int64(len(clusters)))
	var out strings.Builder
	for i := int64(0); i < count; i += step {
		out.WriteString(clusters[start+i])
	}
	return out.String()
}

func rangeBy(first, last, step int64) iter.Seq[int64] {
	return func(yield func(int64) bool) {
		for i := first; (step > 0 && i <= last) || (step < 0 && i >= last); {
			if !yield(i) {
				return
			}
			next := i + step
			if (step > 0 && next < i) || (step < 0 && next > i) {
				return // the next value would overflow
			}
			i = next
		}
	}
}

func raiseOf[T any](ctx *actor.Context, where, message string) T {
	failAt(ctx, where, message)
	panic("unreachable")
}

func raiseException[T, E any](ctx *actor.Context, where string, raised E, message func(E) string) T {
	actor.Raise(ctx, where, raised, message(raised))
	panic("unreachable")
}

func throwOf[T any](ctx *actor.Context, where string, value any) T {
	actor.Throw(ctx, where, value)
	panic("unreachable")
}

func intPow(ctx *actor.Context, base, exponent int64, where string) int64 {
	if exponent < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "integer power needs a nonnegative exponent")
	}
	result := int64(1)
	for ; exponent > 0; exponent >>= 1 {
		if exponent&1 == 1 {
			result = mul(ctx, result, base, where)
		}
		if exponent > 1 {
			base = mul(ctx, base, base, where)
		}
	}
	return result
}

func listSubtract[T any](a, b L[T], eq func(T, T) bool) L[T] {
	remove := fwd(b)
	if len(remove) == 0 {
		return a
	}
	// Integers and strings are counted in a hash table instead of compared
	// pairwise, which matters once the right-hand side is long.
	if len(remove) > 8 {
		switch left := any(fwd(a)).(type) {
		case []int64:
			return mk(any(subtractCounted(left, any(remove).([]int64))).([]T))
		case []string:
			return mk(any(subtractCounted(left, any(remove).([]string))).([]T))
		}
	}
	// gone marks the right-hand elements already used; first skips the
	// used prefix so that removing a run costs one comparison each.
	gone := make([]bool, len(remove))
	first := 0
	var out []T
	for _, x := range fwd(a) {
		found := false
		for i := first; i < len(remove); i++ {
			if !gone[i] && eq(x, remove[i]) {
				gone[i], found = true, true
				for first < len(remove) && gone[first] {
					first++
				}
				break
			}
		}
		if !found {
			out = append(out, x)
		}
	}
	return mk(out)
}

func subtractCounted[K comparable](left, remove []K) []K {
	counts := make(map[K]int, len(remove))
	for _, y := range remove {
		counts[y]++
	}
	var out []K
	for _, x := range left {
		if n := counts[x]; n > 0 {
			counts[x] = n - 1
			continue
		}
		out = append(out, x)
	}
	return out
}

func listLess[T any](a, b L[T], less func(T, T) bool) bool {
	for i := 0; i < len(a.S) && i < len(b.S); i++ {
		x, y := lat(a, i), lat(b, i)
		if less(x, y) {
			return true
		}
		if less(y, x) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return len(a.S) < len(b.S)
}

// The random source is seeded from ALCHEM_SEED when set, so a run that used
// random choices can be repeated exactly.
var random = func() *rand.Rand {
	seed := uint64(time.Now().UnixNano())
	if text := os.Getenv("ALCHEM_SEED"); text != "" {
		var parsed uint64
		for _, r := range text {
			if r < '0' || r > '9' {
				parsed = 0
				for _, b := range []byte(text) {
					parsed = parsed*131 + uint64(b)
				}
				break
			}
			parsed = parsed*10 + uint64(r-'0')
		}
		seed = parsed
	}
	return rand.New(rand.NewPCG(seed, seed^0x9e3779b97f4a7c15))
}()
var randomLock = make(chan struct{}, 1)

func randomInt(n int) int {
	randomLock <- struct{}{}
	defer func() { <-randomLock }()
	return random.IntN(n)
}

// ---- Enum: eager functions over any enumerable ----

func enumToList[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) L[A] { return toList(s) }

func enumAll1(s iter.Seq[bool]) bool {
	all := true
	s(func(x bool) bool { all = x; return x })
	return all
}

func enumAll[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) bool {
	all := true
	s(func(x A) bool { all = f(ctx, x); return all })
	return all
}

func enumAny1(s iter.Seq[bool]) bool {
	found := false
	s(func(x bool) bool { found = x; return !x })
	return found
}

func enumAny[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) bool {
	found := false
	s(func(x A) bool { found = f(ctx, x); return !found })
	return found
}

// seqAt finds the element at an index; negative indexes count from the end.
func seqAt[A any](s iter.Seq[A], index int64) (A, bool) {
	var zero A
	if index < 0 {
		all := collect(s)
		if position := int64(len(all)) + index; position >= 0 {
			return all[position], true
		}
		return zero, false
	}
	found := false
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if index == 0 {
			zero, found = x, true
			return false
		}
		index--
		return true
	})
	return zero, found
}

func enumAt[A any](s iter.Seq[A], index int64) Toption[A] {
	if x, ok := seqAt(s, index); ok {
		return someOf(x)
	}
	return noneOf[A]()
}

func enumAtDefault[A any](s iter.Seq[A], index int64, fallback A) A {
	if x, ok := seqAt(s, index); ok {
		return x
	}
	return fallback
}

func enumFetchBang[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], index int64, where string) A {
	x, ok := seqAt(s, index)
	if !ok {
		failAt(ctx, where, "out of bounds error")
	}
	return x
}

func enumChunkBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, eq func(B, B) bool) L[L[A]] {
	return toList(streamChunkBy(ctx, s, f, eq))
}

func enumChunkEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], count int64, where string) L[L[A]] {
	return toList(streamChunkEveryStep(ctx, s, count, count, where))
}

func enumChunkEveryStep[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], count, step int64, where string) L[L[A]] {
	return toList(streamChunkEveryStep(ctx, s, count, step, where))
}

func enumConcat1[A any](s iter.Seq[L[A]]) L[A] {
	var out []A
	s(func(l L[A]) bool { out = append(out, fwd(l)...); return true })
	return mk(out)
}

func enumConcat[A any](a, b iter.Seq[A]) L[A] { return mk(append(collect(a), collect(b)...)) }

// Lists are stored head last, so the second list's storage comes first.
func enumConcatL[A any](a, b L[A]) L[A] {
	out := make([]A, len(a.S)+len(b.S))
	copy(out, b.S)
	copy(out[len(b.S):], a.S)
	return L[A]{S: out}
}

func enumCount[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) int64 {
	n := int64(0)
	s(func(A) bool { n++; return true })
	return n
}

func enumCountBy[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) int64 {
	n := int64(0)
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if f(ctx, x) {
			n++
		}
		return true
	})
	return n
}

func enumCountUntil[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], limit int64, where string) int64 {
	if limit <= 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "count_until needs a positive limit")
	}
	n := int64(0)
	s(func(A) bool { n++; return n < limit })
	return n
}

func enumCountUntilBy[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool, limit int64, where string) int64 {
	if limit <= 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "count_until needs a positive limit")
	}
	n := int64(0)
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if f(ctx, x) {
			n++
		}
		return n < limit
	})
	return n
}

func enumDedup[A any](s iter.Seq[A], eq func(A, A) bool) L[A] { return toList(streamDedup(s, eq)) }

func enumDedupBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, eq func(B, B) bool) L[A] {
	return toList(streamDedupBy(ctx, s, f, eq))
}

func enumDrop[A any](s iter.Seq[A], count int64) L[A] {
	if count >= 0 {
		return toList(streamDrop(s, count))
	}
	all := collect(s)
	keep := int64(len(all)) + count
	if keep < 0 {
		keep = 0
	}
	return mk(all[:keep])
}

func enumDropEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], nth int64, where string) L[A] {
	return toList(streamDropEvery(ctx, s, nth, where))
}

func enumDropWhile[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) L[A] {
	return toList(streamDropWhile(ctx, s, f))
}

func enumEach[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) {
	s(func(x A) bool { f(ctx, x); return true })
}

func enumEmpty[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) bool {
	empty := true
	s(func(A) bool { empty = false; return false })
	return empty
}

func enumFilter[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) L[A] {
	return toList(streamFilter(ctx, s, f))
}

func find[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) (A, int64, bool) {
	var hit A
	index, found := int64(-1), false
	s(func(x A) bool {
		index++
		if f(ctx, x) {
			hit, found = x, true
		}
		return !found
	})
	return hit, index, found
}

func enumFind[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) Toption[A] {
	if x, _, ok := find(ctx, s, f); ok {
		return someOf(x)
	}
	return noneOf[A]()
}

func enumFindDefault[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], fallback A, f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) A {
	if x, _, ok := find(ctx, s, f); ok {
		return x
	}
	return fallback
}

func enumFindIndex[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) Toption[int64] {
	if _, index, ok := find(ctx, s, f); ok {
		return someOf(index)
	}
	return noneOf[int64]()
}

func enumFindValue[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) Toption[B]) Toption[B] {
	result := noneOf[B]()
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if hit, ok := f(ctx, x).(Toption_c1[B]); ok {
			result = Toption[B](hit)
			return false
		}
		return true
	})
	return result
}

func enumFlatMap[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) L[B]) L[B] {
	var out []B
	s(func(x A) bool {
		part := f(ctx, x)
		for i := len(part.S) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
			out = append(out, part.S[i])
		}
		return true
	})
	return mk(out)
}

func enumFlatMapReduce[A, C, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc C, f func(*actor.Context, A, C) pair[L[B], C]) pair[L[B], C] {
	var out []B
	s(func(x A) bool {
		step := f(ctx, x, acc)
		out, acc = append(out, fwd(step.E0)...), step.E1
		return true
	})
	return pair[L[B], C]{mk(out), acc}
}

func enumFrequencies[A comparable](s iter.Seq[A]) actor.Map[A, int64] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[A, int64]{})
	s(func(x A) bool {
		n, _ := out.Get(x)
		out.Put(x, n+1)
		return true
	})
	return out.Map()
}

func enumFrequenciesBy[A any, K comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) K) actor.Map[K, int64] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, int64]{})
	s(func(x A) bool {
		key := f(ctx, x)
		n, _ := out.Get(key)
		out.Put(key, n+1)
		return true
	})
	return out.Map()
}

func enumGroupByMap[A any, K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], key func(*actor.Context, A) K, value func(*actor.Context, A) V) actor.Map[K, L[V]] {
	groups := map[K][]V{}
	var order []K
	s(func(x A) bool {
		k := key(ctx, x)
		if _, seen := groups[k]; !seen {
			order = append(order, k)
		}
		groups[k] = append(groups[k], value(ctx, x))
		return true
	})
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, L[V]]{})
	for _, k := range order {
		out.Put(k, mk(groups[k]))
	}
	return out.Map()
}

func enumGroupBy[A any, K comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], key func(*actor.Context, A) K) actor.Map[K, L[A]] {
	return enumGroupByMap(ctx, s, key, func(_ *actor.Context, x A) A { return x })
}

func enumIntersperse[A any](s iter.Seq[A], separator A) L[A] {
	return toList(streamIntersperse(s, separator))
}

func enumJoinSep[A any](s iter.Seq[A], separator string, show func(A) string) string {
	var out strings.Builder
	first := true
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if !first {
			out.WriteString(separator)
		}
		first = false
		out.WriteString(show(x))
		return true
	})
	return out.String()
}

func enumJoin[A any](s iter.Seq[A], show func(A) string) string { return enumJoinSep(s, "", show) }

func enumMap[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) L[B] {
	return toList(streamMap(ctx, s, f))
}

func enumMapEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], nth int64, f func(*actor.Context, A) A, where string) L[A] {
	return toList(streamMapEvery(ctx, s, nth, f, where))
}

func enumMapIntersperse[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], separator B, f func(*actor.Context, A) B) L[B] {
	return toList(streamIntersperse(streamMap(ctx, s, f), separator))
}

func enumMapJoinSep[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], separator string, f func(*actor.Context, A) B, show func(B) string) string {
	return enumJoinSep(streamMap(ctx, s, f), separator, show)
}

func enumMapJoin[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, show func(B) string) string {
	return enumJoinSep(streamMap(ctx, s, f), "", show)
}

func enumMapReduce[A, C, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc C, f func(*actor.Context, A, C) pair[B, C]) pair[L[B], C] {
	var out []B
	s(func(x A) bool {
		step := f(ctx, x, acc)
		out, acc = append(out, step.E0), step.E1
		return true
	})
	return pair[L[B], C]{mk(out), acc}
}

// extreme returns the least element under less, the first one among equals.
func extreme[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], less func(A, A) bool, where string) A {
	var best A
	any := false
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if !any || less(x, best) {
			best, any = x, true
		}
		return true
	})
	if !any {
		failAt(ctx, where, "empty error")
	}
	return best
}

func flip[A any](less func(A, A) bool) func(A, A) bool {
	return func(x, y A) bool { return less(y, x) }
}

func enumMin[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], less func(A, A) bool, where string) A {
	return extreme(ctx, s, less, where)
}

func enumMax[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], less func(A, A) bool, where string) A {
	return extreme(ctx, s, flip(less), where)
}

// The Or forms return what the function gives for an empty enumerable.
func enumMaxOr[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], fallback func(*actor.Context) A, less func(A, A) bool) A {
	return extremeOr(ctx, s, fallback, flip(less))
}

func enumMinOr[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], fallback func(*actor.Context) A, less func(A, A) bool) A {
	return extremeOr(ctx, s, fallback, less)
}

func extremeOr[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], fallback func(*actor.Context) A, less func(A, A) bool) A {
	var best A
	any := false
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if !any || less(x, best) {
			best, any = x, true
		}
		return true
	})
	if !any {
		return fallback(ctx)
	}
	return best
}

func enumChunkWhile[A, S, C any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc S, step func(*actor.Context, A, S) pair[Toption[C], S], last func(*actor.Context, S) Toption[C]) L[C] {
	return toList(streamChunkWhile(ctx, s, acc, step, last))
}

// into adds the elements to a list, a set, a map (as {key, value} pairs) or a
// string. The checker has made sure the elements fit the target.
func enumInto[A, C any](s iter.Seq[A], target C) C {
	switch into := any(target).(type) {
	case string:
		var out strings.Builder
		out.WriteString(into)
		s(func(x A) bool { out.WriteString(any(x).(string)); return true })
		return any(out.String()).(C)
	case L[A]:
		return any(enumConcatL(into, toList(s))).(C)
	case interface {
		Collect(func(func(any) bool)) any
	}:
		return into.Collect(func(yield func(any) bool) { s(func(x A) bool { return yield(x) }) }).(C)
	}
	return target
}

func enumMinMax[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], less func(A, A) bool, where string) pair[A, A] {
	all := seqSlice(collect(s))
	return pair[A, A]{extreme(ctx, all, less, where), extreme(ctx, all, flip(less), where)}
}

func byKey[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, f func(*actor.Context, A) B, less func(B, B) bool) func(A, A) bool {
	return func(x, y A) bool { return less(f(ctx, x), f(ctx, y)) }
}

func enumMinBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, less func(B, B) bool, where string) A {
	return extreme(ctx, s, byKey(ctx, f, less), where)
}

func enumMaxBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, less func(B, B) bool, where string) A {
	return extreme(ctx, s, byKey(ctx, f, flip(less)), where)
}

func enumMinMaxBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, less func(B, B) bool, where string) pair[A, A] {
	all := seqSlice(collect(s))
	return pair[A, A]{extreme(ctx, all, byKey(ctx, f, less), where), extreme(ctx, all, byKey(ctx, f, flip(less)), where)}
}

func seqSlice[T any](xs []T) iter.Seq[T] {
	return func(yield func(T) bool) {
		for _, x := range xs {
			if !yield(x) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func enumMember[A any](s iter.Seq[A], wanted A, eq func(A, A) bool) bool {
	found := false
	s(func(x A) bool { found = eq(x, wanted); return !found })
	return found
}

// Sums and products check integer overflow like the arithmetic operators.
func numAdd[A int64 | float64](ctx *actor.Context, x, y A, where string) A {
	if a, ok := any(x).(int64); ok {
		return any(add(ctx, a, any(y).(int64), where)).(A)
	}
	return x + y
}

func numMul[A int64 | float64](ctx *actor.Context, x, y A, where string) A {
	if a, ok := any(x).(int64); ok {
		return any(mul(ctx, a, any(y).(int64), where)).(A)
	}
	return x * y
}

func enumSum[A int64 | float64](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], where string) A {
	var total A
	s(func(x A) bool { total = numAdd(ctx, total, x, where); return true })
	return total
}

func enumProduct[A int64 | float64](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], where string) A {
	total := A(1)
	s(func(x A) bool { total = numMul(ctx, total, x, where); return true })
	return total
}

func enumSumBy[A any, B int64 | float64](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, where string) B {
	return enumSum(ctx, streamMap(ctx, s, f), where)
}

func enumProductBy[A any, B int64 | float64](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, where string) B {
	return enumProduct(ctx, streamMap(ctx, s, f), where)
}

func enumRandom[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], where string) A {
	all := collect(s)
	if len(all) == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "empty error")
	}
	return all[randomInt(len(all))]
}

func shuffled[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) []A {
	all := collect(s)
	for i := len(all) - 1; i > 0; i-- {
		j := randomInt(i + 1)
		all[i], all[j] = all[j], all[i]
	}
	return all
}

func enumShuffle[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) L[A] { return mk(shuffled(s)) }

func enumTakeRandom[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], count int64, where string) L[A] {
	if count < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "take_random needs a nonnegative count")
	}
	all := shuffled(s)
	if count < int64(len(all)) {
		all = all[:count]
	}
	return mk(all)
}

func enumReduce1[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A, A) A, where string) A {
	var acc A
	started := false
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if !started {
			acc, started = x, true
		} else {
			acc = f(ctx, x, acc)
		}
		return true
	})
	if !started {
		failAt(ctx, where, "empty error")
	}
	return acc
}

func enumReduce[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc B, f func(*actor.Context, A, B) B) B {
	s(func(x A) bool { acc = f(ctx, x, acc); return true })
	return acc
}

func enumReduceWhile[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc B, f func(*actor.Context, A, B) Tstep[B]) B {
	s(func(x A) bool {
		switch step := f(ctx, x, acc).(type) {
		case Tstep_c0[B]:
			acc = step.F0
			return true
		case Tstep_c1[B]:
			acc = step.F0
		}
		return false
	})
	return acc
}

func enumReject[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) L[A] {
	return toList(streamReject(ctx, s, f))
}

func reverse[T any](xs []T) []T {
	for i, j := 0, len(xs)-1; i < j; i, j = i+1, j-1 {
		xs[i], xs[j] = xs[j], xs[i]
	}
	return xs
}

func enumReverse[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) L[A] { return mk(reverse(collect(s))) }

func enumReverseTail[A any](s, tail iter.Seq[A]) L[A] {
	return mk(append(reverse(collect(s)), collect(tail)...))
}

func enumReverseSlice[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], start, count int64, where string) L[A] {
	if start < 0 || count < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "reverse_slice needs a nonnegative start and count")
	}
	all := collect(s)
	if start < int64(len(all)) {
		end := min(start+count, int64(len(all)))
		reverse(all[start:end])
	}
	return mk(all)
}

func enumScan1[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A, A) A) L[A] {
	return toList(streamScan1(ctx, s, f))
}

func enumScan[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc B, f func(*actor.Context, A, B) B) L[B] {
	return toList(streamScan(ctx, s, acc, f))
}

func enumSlice[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], start, count int64, where string) L[A] {
	if count < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "slice needs a nonnegative amount")
	}
	if start < 0 {
		all := collect(s)
		if start += int64(len(all)); start < 0 {
			return L[A]{}
		}
		return mk(all[start:min(start+count, int64(len(all)))])
	}
	return toList(streamTake(streamDrop(s, start), count))
}

func enumSort[A any](s iter.Seq[A], less func(A, A) bool) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	sortStable(all, less)
	return mk(all)
}

// sortStable sorts by less, keeping equal elements in their order. Integers
// and strings that compare equal are indistinguishable, so those are sorted
// directly.
func sortStable[A any](all []A, less func(A, A) bool) {
	switch typed := any(all).(type) {
	case []int64:
		slices.Sort(typed)
	case []string:
		slices.Sort(typed)
	default:
		slices.SortStableFunc(all, func(x, y A) int {
			switch {
			case less(x, y):
				return -1
			case less(y, x):
				return 1
			}
			return 0
		})
	}
}

// A sorter says whether its first argument may come before its second;
// returning true for equal elements keeps the sort stable.
func enumSortWith[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], sorter func(*actor.Context, A, A) bool) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	// The sorter is "may come before": x sorts after y only when it may not
	// come before it.
	slices.SortStableFunc(all, func(x, y A) int {
		if !sorter(ctx, y, x) {
			return -1
		}
		if !sorter(ctx, x, y) {
			return 1
		}
		return 0
	})
	return mk(all)
}

func enumSortBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, less func(B, B) bool) L[A] {
	return sortByKey(ctx, s, f, less, true)
}

// sortByKey sorts by the keys f gives. natural says less is the ordinary
// order of B, which lets integer and string keys be compared directly.
func sortByKey[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, less func(B, B) bool, natural bool) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	keys := make([]B, len(all))
	order := make([]int, len(all))
	for i, x := range all {
		keys[i], order[i] = f(ctx, x), i
	}
	var direct any
	if natural {
		direct = keys
	}
	switch typed := direct.(type) {
	case []int64:
		slices.SortStableFunc(order, func(i, j int) int { return cmp.Compare(typed[i], typed[j]) })
	case []string:
		slices.SortStableFunc(order, func(i, j int) int { return cmp.Compare(typed[i], typed[j]) })
	default:
		slices.SortStableFunc(order, func(i, j int) int {
			switch {
			case less(keys[i], keys[j]):
				return -1
			case less(keys[j], keys[i]):
				return 1
			}
			return 0
		})
	}
	out := make([]A, len(all))
	for i, index := range order {
		out[i] = all[index]
	}
	return mk(out)
}

func enumSortByWith[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, sorter func(*actor.Context, B, B) bool) L[A] {
	return sortByKey(ctx, s, f, func(x, y B) bool { return !sorter(ctx, y, x) }, false)
}

func enumSplit[A any](s iter.Seq[A], count int64) pair[L[A], L[A]] {
	all := collect(s)
	at := count
	if count < 0 {
		at = max(int64(len(all))+count, 0)
	}
	at = min(at, int64(len(all)))
	return pair[L[A], L[A]]{mk(append([]A{}, all[:at]...)), mk(append([]A{}, all[at:]...))}
}

func enumSplitWhile[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) pair[L[A], L[A]] {
	all := collect(s)
	at := 0
	for at < len(all) && f(ctx, all[at]) {
		at++
	}
	return pair[L[A], L[A]]{mk(append([]A{}, all[:at]...)), mk(append([]A{}, all[at:]...))}
}

func enumSplitWith[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) pair[L[A], L[A]] {
	var yes, no []A
	s(func(x A) bool {
		if f(ctx, x) {
			yes = append(yes, x)
		} else {
			no = append(no, x)
		}
		return true
	})
	return pair[L[A], L[A]]{mk(yes), mk(no)}
}

func enumTake[A any](s iter.Seq[A], count int64) L[A] {
	if count >= 0 {
		return toList(streamTake(s, count))
	}
	all := collect(s)
	return mk(all[max(int64(len(all))+count, 0):])
}

func enumTakeEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], nth int64, where string) L[A] {
	return toList(streamTakeEvery(ctx, s, nth, where))
}

func enumTakeWhile[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) L[A] {
	return toList(streamTakeWhile(ctx, s, f))
}

func enumUniq[A any](s iter.Seq[A], eq func(A, A) bool) L[A] { return toList(streamUniq(s, eq)) }
func enumUniqC[A comparable](s iter.Seq[A]) L[A]              { return toList(streamUniqC(s)) }

func enumUniqBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, eq func(B, B) bool) L[A] {
	return toList(streamUniqBy(ctx, s, f, eq))
}

func enumUniqByC[A any, B comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) L[A] {
	return toList(streamUniqByC(ctx, s, f))
}

func enumUnzip[A, B any](s iter.Seq[pair[A, B]]) pair[L[A], L[B]] {
	var left []A
	var right []B
	s(func(x pair[A, B]) bool { left, right = append(left, x.E0), append(right, x.E1); return true })
	return pair[L[A], L[B]]{mk(left), mk(right)}
}

func enumWithIndex[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) L[pair[A, int64]] { return toList(streamWithIndexFrom(s, 0)) }

// enumWithIndexMap passes each element and its index to the function.
func enumWithIndexMap[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A, int64) B) L[B] {
	var out []B
	i := int64(0)
	s(func(x A) bool {
		out = append(out, f(ctx, x, i))
		i++
		return true
	})
	return mk(out)
}

// enumSlide moves the element at index to insertion, shifting those
// between; negative indexes count from the end.
func enumSlide[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], index, insertion int64, where string) L[A] {
	items := collect(s)
	n := int64(len(items))
	at := func(i int64) int64 {
		if i < 0 {
			i += n
		}
		return i
	}
	from, to := at(index), at(insertion)
	if from < 0 || from >= n || to < 0 || to >= n {
		if n == 0 || from < 0 || from >= n {
			return mk(items)
		}
		failAt(ctx, where, "Enum.slide: insertion index out of range")
	}
	moved := items[from]
	rest := append(append([]A{}, items[:from]...), items[from+1:]...)
	out := append(append(append([]A{}, rest[:to]...), moved), rest[to:]...)
	return mk(out)
}

// enumZipAll zips any number of enumerables, as lists, stopping at the
// shortest.
func enumZipAll[A any](lists iter.Seq[L[A]]) L[L[A]] {
	var columns [][]A
	lists(func(l L[A]) bool { columns = append(columns, fwd(l)); return true })
	if len(columns) == 0 {
		return mk([]L[A]{})
	}
	shortest := len(columns[0])
	for _, column := range columns {
		shortest = min(shortest, len(column))
	}
	out := make([]L[A], shortest)
	for i := range out {
		row := make([]A, len(columns))
		for j, column := range columns {
			row[j] = column[i]
		}
		out[i] = mk(row)
	}
	return mk(out)
}

func enumZipWithAll[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, lists iter.Seq[L[A]], f func(*actor.Context, L[A]) B) L[B] {
	var out []B
	for _, row := range fwd(enumZipAll(lists)) {
		out = append(out, f(ctx, row))
	}
	return mk(out)
}

// enumChunkEveryLeftover pads the last chunk from leftover, or drops it
// when it is short and leftover is :discard.
func enumChunkEveryLeftover[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], count, step int64, leftover iter.Seq[A], where string) L[L[A]] {
	chunks := fwd(toList(streamChunkEveryStep(ctx, s, count, step, where)))
	if n := len(chunks); n > 0 && int64(len(chunks[n-1].S)) < count {
		last := fwd(chunks[n-1])
		leftover(func(x A) bool {
			if int64(len(last)) >= count {
				return false
			}
			last = append(last, x)
			return true
		})
		chunks[n-1] = mk(last)
	}
	return mk(chunks)
}

func enumChunkEveryDiscard[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], count, step int64, leftover actor.Atom, where string) L[L[A]] {
	if leftover != "discard" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Enum.chunk_every takes a list to pad the last chunk with, or :discard")
	}
	chunks := fwd(toList(streamChunkEveryStep(ctx, s, count, step, where)))
	if n := len(chunks); n > 0 && int64(len(chunks[n-1].S)) < count {
		chunks = chunks[:n-1]
	}
	return mk(chunks)
}

// direction turns :asc or :desc into an order.
func direction[A any](ctx *actor.Context, order actor.Atom, less func(A, A) bool, where string) func(A, A) bool {
	switch order {
	case "asc":
		return less
	case "desc":
		return flip(less)
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "the sort order is :asc or :desc, not :"+string(order))
	return less
}

// sortWith sorts stably by any order; sortStable assumes the natural order
// for integers and strings.
func sortWith[A any](all []A, less func(A, A) bool) {
	slices.SortStableFunc(all, func(x, y A) int {
		switch {
		case less(x, y):
			return -1
		case less(y, x):
			return 1
		}
		return 0
	})
}

func enumSortDirection[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], order actor.Atom, less func(A, A) bool, where string) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	sortWith(all, direction(ctx, order, less, where))
	return mk(all)
}

func enumSortByDirection[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, order actor.Atom, less func(B, B) bool, where string) L[A] {
	ordered := direction(ctx, order, less, where)
	all := collect(s)
	keys := make(map[int]B, len(all))
	index := make([]int, len(all))
	for i, x := range all {
		keys[i], index[i] = f(ctx, x), i
	}
	sortWith(index, func(i, j int) bool { return ordered(keys[i], keys[j]) })
	out := make([]A, len(all))
	for i, k := range index {
		out[i] = all[k]
	}
	return mk(out)
}

// The With forms of min and max order by a function: sorter(a, b) is true
// when a comes before b, and the result is the element that comes first.
// That is the smallest for <= and the largest for >=, as in Elixir.
func enumMinWith[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], sorter func(*actor.Context, A, A) bool, where string) A {
	return extreme(ctx, s, func(x, y A) bool { return sorter(ctx, x, y) && !sorter(ctx, y, x) }, where)
}

func enumMaxWith[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], sorter func(*actor.Context, A, A) bool, where string) A {
	return enumMinWith(ctx, s, sorter, where)
}

func enumMinWithOr[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], sorter func(*actor.Context, A, A) bool, fallback func(*actor.Context) A) A {
	return extremeOr(ctx, s, fallback, func(x, y A) bool { return sorter(ctx, x, y) && !sorter(ctx, y, x) })
}

func enumMaxWithOr[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], sorter func(*actor.Context, A, A) bool, fallback func(*actor.Context) A) A {
	return enumMinWithOr(ctx, s, sorter, fallback)
}

func enumMinByWith[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, sorter func(*actor.Context, B, B) bool, where string) A {
	return extreme(ctx, s, func(x, y A) bool { a, b := f(ctx, x), f(ctx, y); return sorter(ctx, a, b) && !sorter(ctx, b, a) }, where)
}

func enumMaxByWith[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, sorter func(*actor.Context, B, B) bool, where string) A {
	return enumMinByWith(ctx, s, f, sorter, where)
}

// enumIntoWith transforms each element before adding it to the target.
func enumIntoWith[X, A, C any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[X], target C, f func(*actor.Context, X) A) C {
	return enumInto(func(yield func(A) bool) {
		s(func(x X) bool { return yield(f(ctx, x)) })
	}, target)
}

func enumWithIndexFrom[A any](s iter.Seq[A], offset int64) L[pair[A, int64]] {
	return toList(streamWithIndexFrom(s, offset))
}

// streamZipAll zips enumerables lazily, as lists, stopping at the shortest.
func streamZipAll[A any](sources iter.Seq[iter.Seq[A]]) iter.Seq[L[A]] {
	return func(yield func(L[A]) bool) {
		var pulls []func() (A, bool)
		for source := range sources {
			next, stop := iter.Pull(source)
			defer stop()
			pulls = append(pulls, next)
		}
		if len(pulls) == 0 {
			return
		}
		for {
			row := make([]A, len(pulls))
			for i, next := range pulls {
				value, ok := next()
				if !ok {
					return
				}
				row[i] = value
			}
			if !yield(mk(row)) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamZipLists[A any](lists iter.Seq[L[A]]) iter.Seq[L[A]] {
	return streamZipAll(func(yield func(iter.Seq[A]) bool) {
		lists(func(l L[A]) bool { return yield(seqList(l)) })
	})
}

// listMyersDifference gives the edits that turn one list into another, as
// {:eq | :del | :ins, elements} runs, by Myers' algorithm.
func listMyersDifference[A any](a, b L[A], equal func(A, A) bool) L[pair[actor.Atom, L[A]]] {
	return mk(myers(fwd(a), fwd(b), equal, func(items []A) L[A] { return mk(slices.Clone(items)) }))
}

// myers finds a shortest edit script between two sequences.
func myers[A, G any](a, b []A, equal func(A, A) bool, group func([]A) G) []pair[actor.Atom, G] {
	n, m := len(a), len(b)
	max := n + m
	offset := max + 1
	v := make([]int, 2*max+3)
	var trace [][]int
	found := false
	for d := 0; d <= max && !found; d++ {
		trace = append(trace, slices.Clone(v))
		for k := -d; k <= d; k += 2 {
			var x int
			if k == -d || (k != d && v[offset+k-1] < v[offset+k+1]) {
				x = v[offset+k+1]
			} else {
				x = v[offset+k-1] + 1
			}
			y := x - k
			for x < n && y < m && equal(a[x], b[y]) {
				x, y = x+1, y+1
			}
			v[offset+k] = x
			if x >= n && y >= m {
				found = true
				break
			}
		}
	}
	trace = append(trace, slices.Clone(v))
	// Walk back through the trace, collecting single edits last first.
	type edit struct {
		kind actor.Atom
		item A
	}
	var edits []edit
	x, y := n, m
	for d := len(trace) - 2; d >= 0 && (x > 0 || y > 0); d-- {
		v := trace[d]
		k := x - y
		var prevK int
		if k == -d || (k != d && v[offset+k-1] < v[offset+k+1]) {
			prevK = k + 1
		} else {
			prevK = k - 1
		}
		prevX := v[offset+prevK]
		prevY := prevX - prevK
		for x > prevX && y > prevY {
			edits = append(edits, edit{"eq", a[x-1]})
			x, y = x-1, y-1
		}
		if d > 0 {
			if x == prevX {
				edits = append(edits, edit{"ins", b[y-1]})
			} else {
				edits = append(edits, edit{"del", a[x-1]})
			}
		}
		x, y = prevX, prevY
	}
	for x > 0 && y > 0 {
		edits = append(edits, edit{"eq", a[x-1]})
		x, y = x-1, y-1
	}
	slices.Reverse(edits)
	var out []pair[actor.Atom, G]
	for i := 0; i < len(edits); {
		j := i
		var run []A
		for j < len(edits) && edits[j].kind == edits[i].kind {
			run = append(run, edits[j].item)
			j++
		}
		out = append(out, pair[actor.Atom, G]{edits[i].kind, group(run)})
		i = j
	}
	return out
}

func enumZip[A, B any](a iter.Seq[A], b iter.Seq[B]) L[pair[A, B]] { return toList(streamZip(a, b)) }

// The L variants take two lists as stored, head last, and index them
// instead of pulling one through a coroutine.
func enumZipL[A, B any](a L[A], b L[B]) L[pair[A, B]] {
	n := min(len(a.S), len(b.S))
	out := make([]pair[A, B], n)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		out[n-1-i] = pair[A, B]{a.S[len(a.S)-1-i], b.S[len(b.S)-1-i]}
	}
	return L[pair[A, B]]{S: out}
}

func enumZipWithL[A, B, C any](ctx *actor.Context, a L[A], b L[B], f func(*actor.Context, A, B) C) L[C] {
	n := min(len(a.S), len(b.S))
	out := make([]C, n)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		out[n-1-i] = f(ctx, a.S[len(a.S)-1-i], b.S[len(b.S)-1-i])
	}
	return L[C]{S: out}
}

func enumZipReduceL[A, B, C any](ctx *actor.Context, a L[A], b L[B], acc C, f func(*actor.Context, A, B, C) C) C {
	n := min(len(a.S), len(b.S))
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		acc = f(ctx, a.S[len(a.S)-1-i], b.S[len(b.S)-1-i], acc)
	}
	return acc
}

func enumZipWith[A, B, C any](ctx *actor.Context, a iter.Seq[A], b iter.Seq[B], f func(*actor.Context, A, B) C) L[C] {
	return toList(streamZipWith(ctx, a, b, f))
}

func enumZipReduce[A, B, C any](ctx *actor.Context, a iter.Seq[A], b iter.Seq[B], acc C, f func(*actor.Context, A, B, C) C) C {
	streamZip(a, b)(func(x pair[A, B]) bool { acc = f(ctx, x.E0, x.E1, acc); return true })
	return acc
}

// ---- Stream: lazy functions; nothing runs until the stream is consumed ----

func streamMap[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) iter.Seq[B] {
	return func(yield func(B) bool) {
		s(func(x A) bool { return yield(f(ctx, x)) })
	}
}

func streamFilter[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		s(func(x A) bool { return !f(ctx, x) || yield(x) })
	}
}

func streamReject[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		s(func(x A) bool { return f(ctx, x) || yield(x) })
	}
}

func streamEach[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		s(func(x A) bool { f(ctx, x); return yield(x) })
	}
}

func streamTake[A any](s iter.Seq[A], count int64) iter.Seq[A] {
	if count < 0 {
		// The last elements are only known once the source ends.
		return func(yield func(A) bool) {
			all := collect(s)
			seqSlice(all[max(int64(len(all))+count, 0):])(yield)
		}
	}
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		if count == 0 {
			return
		}
		left := count
		s(func(x A) bool {
			left--
			return yield(x) && left > 0
		})
	}
}

func streamDrop[A any](s iter.Seq[A], count int64) iter.Seq[A] {
	if count < 0 {
		return func(yield func(A) bool) {
			all := collect(s)
			seqSlice(all[:max(int64(len(all))+count, 0)])(yield)
		}
	}
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		skip := count
		s(func(x A) bool {
			if skip > 0 {
				skip--
				return true
			}
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamTakeWhile[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		s(func(x A) bool { return f(ctx, x) && yield(x) })
	}
}

func streamDropWhile[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		dropping := true
		s(func(x A) bool {
			if dropping && f(ctx, x) {
				return true
			}
			dropping = false
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func nthCheck(ctx *actor.Context, nth int64, where string) {
	if nth < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "nth must be a nonnegative integer")
	}
}

func streamTakeEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], nth int64, where string) iter.Seq[A] {
	nthCheck(ctx, nth, where)
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		if nth == 0 {
			return
		}
		index := int64(0)
		s(func(x A) bool {
			index++
			return (index-1)%nth != 0 || yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamDropEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], nth int64, where string) iter.Seq[A] {
	nthCheck(ctx, nth, where)
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		index := int64(0)
		s(func(x A) bool {
			index++
			return (nth != 0 && (index-1)%nth == 0) || yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamMapEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], nth int64, f func(*actor.Context, A) A, where string) iter.Seq[A] {
	nthCheck(ctx, nth, where)
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		index := int64(0)
		s(func(x A) bool {
			index++
			if nth != 0 && (index-1)%nth == 0 {
				x = f(ctx, x)
			}
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

// Chunks of count elements, a new one starting every step elements. The last
// chunk may be shorter; it is dropped only when it holds nothing new.
func streamChunkEveryStep[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], count, step int64, where string) iter.Seq[L[A]] {
	if count <= 0 || step <= 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "chunk_every needs a positive count and step")
	}
	return func(yield func(L[A]) bool) {
		var window []A
		skip := int64(0)
		stopped := false
		s(func(x A) bool {
			if skip > 0 {
				skip--
				return true
			}
			window = append(window, x)
			if int64(len(window)) < count {
				return true
			}
			if !yield(mk(append([]A{}, window...))) {
				stopped = true
				return false
			}
			if step >= count {
				window, skip = nil, step-count
			} else {
				window = append([]A{}, window[step:]...)
			}
			return true
		})
		// What is left is a partial chunk; it is new unless it only repeats
		// the overlap carried over from the chunk before.
		if !stopped && len(window) > 0 && (step >= count || int64(len(window)) > count-step) {
			yield(mk(window))
		}
	}
}

func streamChunkEvery[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], count int64, where string) iter.Seq[L[A]] {
	return streamChunkEveryStep(ctx, s, count, count, where)
}

// chunk_while threads a state through the elements. The step function
// returns the next state with, optionally, a finished chunk to emit; the
// last function may emit one more from the final state.
func streamChunkWhile[A, S, C any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc S, step func(*actor.Context, A, S) pair[Toption[C], S], last func(*actor.Context, S) Toption[C]) iter.Seq[C] {
	return func(yield func(C) bool) {
		state := acc
		stopped := false
		s(func(x A) bool {
			result := step(ctx, x, state)
			state = result.E1
			if chunk, ok := result.E0.(Toption_c1[C]); ok && !yield(chunk.F0) {
				stopped = true
				return false
			}
			return true
		})
		if stopped {
			return
		}
		if chunk, ok := last(ctx, state).(Toption_c1[C]); ok {
			yield(chunk.F0)
		}
	}
}

func streamChunkBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, eq func(B, B) bool) iter.Seq[L[A]] {
	return func(yield func(L[A]) bool) {
		var chunk []A
		var key B
		stopped := false
		s(func(x A) bool {
			next := f(ctx, x)
			if len(chunk) > 0 && !eq(key, next) {
				if !yield(mk(chunk)) {
					stopped = true
					return false
				}
				chunk = nil
			}
			chunk, key = append(chunk, x), next
			return true
		})
		if !stopped && len(chunk) > 0 {
			yield(mk(chunk))
		}
	}
}

func streamConcat[A any](a, b iter.Seq[A]) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		more := true
		a(func(x A) bool { more = yield(x); return more })
		if more {
			b(yield)
		}
	}
}

func streamConcat1[A any](sources iter.Seq[iter.Seq[A]]) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		sources(func(s iter.Seq[A]) bool {
			more := true
			s(func(x A) bool { more = yield(x); return more })
			return more
		})
	}
}

func streamConcatLists[A any](sources iter.Seq[L[A]]) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		sources(func(l L[A]) bool {
			more := true
			seqList(l)(func(x A) bool { more = yield(x); return more })
			return more
		})
	}
}

func streamCycle[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], where string) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		for {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			any, more := false, true
			s(func(x A) bool { any = true; more = yield(x); return more })
			if !any {
				failAt(ctx, where, "cannot cycle over an empty enumerable")
			}
			if !more {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamDedup[A any](s iter.Seq[A], eq func(A, A) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		var last A
		started := false
		s(func(x A) bool {
			if started && eq(last, x) {
				return true
			}
			last, started = x, true
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamDedupBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, eq func(B, B) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		var last B
		started := false
		s(func(x A) bool {
			key := f(ctx, x)
			if started && eq(last, key) {
				return true
			}
			last, started = key, true
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamDuplicate[A any](value A, count int64) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		for i := int64(0); i < count; i++ {
			if !yield(value) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamFlatMap[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) L[B]) iter.Seq[B] {
	return func(yield func(B) bool) {
		s(func(x A) bool {
			more := true
			seqList(f(ctx, x))(func(y B) bool { more = yield(y); return more })
			return more
		})
	}
}

func streamFromIndexAt(ctx *actor.Context, start int64) iter.Seq[int64] {
	return func(yield func(int64) bool) {
		for i := start; ; i++ {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			if !yield(i) || i == maxInt {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamFromIndex(ctx *actor.Context) iter.Seq[int64] { return streamFromIndexAt(ctx, 0) }

func streamIntersperse[A any](s iter.Seq[A], separator A) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		first := true
		s(func(x A) bool {
			if !first && !yield(separator) {
				return false
			}
			first = false
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamInterval(ctx *actor.Context, milliseconds int64, where string) iter.Seq[int64] {
	return func(yield func(int64) bool) {
		for i := int64(0); ; i++ {
			actor.Sleep(ctx, milliseconds, where)
			if !yield(i) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamTimer(ctx *actor.Context, milliseconds int64, where string) iter.Seq[int64] {
	return func(yield func(int64) bool) {
		actor.Sleep(ctx, milliseconds, where)
		yield(0)
	}
}

func streamIterate[A any](ctx *actor.Context, start A, f func(*actor.Context, A) A) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		for x := start; ; x = f(ctx, x) {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			if !yield(x) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamRepeatedly[A any](ctx *actor.Context, f func(*actor.Context) A) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		for {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			if !yield(f(ctx)) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamUnfold[S, A any](ctx *actor.Context, acc S, f func(*actor.Context, S) Toption[pair[A, S]]) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		for state := acc; ; {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			next, ok := f(ctx, state).(Toption_c1[pair[A, S]])
			if !ok || !yield(next.F0.E0) {
				return
			}
			state = next.F0.E1
		}
	}
}

// A resource is opened when the stream starts and closed however it ends:
// exhausted, stopped early by the consumer, or cut short by a failure.
func streamResource[S, A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, start func(*actor.Context) S, next func(*actor.Context, S) Toption[pair[L[A], S]], after func(*actor.Context, S) B) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		state := start(ctx)
		defer func() { after(ctx, state) }()
		for {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			step, ok := next(ctx, state).(Toption_c1[pair[L[A], S]])
			if !ok {
				return
			}
			state = step.F0.E1
			more := true
			seqList(step.F0.E0)(func(x A) bool { more = yield(x); return more })
			if !more {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamTransform[A, S, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc S, f func(*actor.Context, A, S) Toption[pair[L[B], S]]) iter.Seq[B] {
	return func(yield func(B) bool) {
		state := acc
		s(func(x A) bool {
			step, ok := f(ctx, x, state).(Toption_c1[pair[L[B], S]])
			if !ok {
				return false
			}
			state = step.F0.E1
			more := true
			seqList(step.F0.E0)(func(y B) bool { more = yield(y); return more })
			return more
		})
	}
}

func streamRun[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) { s(func(A) bool { return true }) }

func streamScan1[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A, A) A) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		var acc A
		started := false
		s(func(x A) bool {
			if started {
				acc = f(ctx, x, acc)
			} else {
				acc, started = x, true
			}
			return yield(acc)
		})
	}
}

func streamScan[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc B, f func(*actor.Context, A, B) B) iter.Seq[B] {
	return func(yield func(B) bool) {
		state := acc
		s(func(x A) bool {
			state = f(ctx, x, state)
			return yield(state)
		})
	}
}

func streamUniq[A any](s iter.Seq[A], eq func(A, A) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		var seen []A
		s(func(x A) bool {
			for _, y := range seen {
				if eq(x, y) {
					return true
				}
			}
			seen = append(seen, x)
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamUniqC[A comparable](s iter.Seq[A]) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		seen := map[A]bool{}
		s(func(x A) bool {
			if seen[x] {
				return true
			}
			seen[x] = true
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamUniqBy[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, eq func(B, B) bool) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		var seen []B
		s(func(x A) bool {
			key := f(ctx, x)
			for _, other := range seen {
				if eq(key, other) {
					return true
				}
			}
			seen = append(seen, key)
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamUniqByC[A any, B comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		seen := map[B]bool{}
		s(func(x A) bool {
			key := f(ctx, x)
			if seen[key] {
				return true
			}
			seen[key] = true
			return yield(x)
		})
	}
}

func streamWithIndexFrom[A any](s iter.Seq[A], offset int64) iter.Seq[pair[A, int64]] {
	return func(yield func(pair[A, int64]) bool) {
		index := offset
		s(func(x A) bool {
			index++
			return yield(pair[A, int64]{x, index - 1})
		})
	}
}

func streamWithIndex[A any](s iter.Seq[A]) iter.Seq[pair[A, int64]] { return streamWithIndexFrom(s, 0) }

func streamZipWith[A, B, C any](ctx *actor.Context, a iter.Seq[A], b iter.Seq[B], f func(*actor.Context, A, B) C) iter.Seq[C] {
	return func(yield func(C) bool) {
		next, stop := iter.Pull(b)
		defer stop()
		a(func(x A) bool {
			y, ok := next()
			return ok && yield(f(ctx, x, y))
		})
	}
}

func streamZip[A, B any](a iter.Seq[A], b iter.Seq[B]) iter.Seq[pair[A, B]] {
	return streamZipWith(nil, a, b, func(_ *actor.Context, x A, y B) pair[A, B] { return pair[A, B]{x, y} })
}

// ---- Lazy input ----

// fileStream reads a file line by line as the stream is consumed, keeping
// each line's ending, and closes the file when the stream ends.
func fileStream(ctx *actor.Context, path string, where string) iter.Seq[string] {
	return func(yield func(string) bool) {
		file, err := os.Open(path)
		if err != nil {
			failAt(ctx, where, "could not stream "+path+": "+reason(err))
		}
		defer file.Close()
		reader := bufio.NewReaderSize(file, 1<<16)
		for {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			line, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
			if line != "" && !yield(line) {
				return
			}
			if err == io.EOF {
				return
			}
			if err != nil {
				failAt(ctx, where, "could not read "+path+": "+reason(err))
			}
		}
	}
}

// fileStreamBytes reads a file in chunks of at most size bytes. A chunk may
// end in the middle of a multi-byte character.
func fileStreamBytes(ctx *actor.Context, path string, size int64, where string) iter.Seq[string] {
	if size <= 0 || size > 1<<30 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "chunk size must be between 1 and 1073741824 bytes")
	}
	return func(yield func(string) bool) {
		file, err := os.Open(path)
		if err != nil {
			failAt(ctx, where, "could not stream "+path+": "+reason(err))
		}
		defer file.Close()
		buffer := make([]byte, size)
		for {
			actor.Check(ctx)
			n, err := io.ReadFull(file, buffer)
			if n > 0 && !yield(string(buffer[:n])) {
				return
			}
			if err == io.EOF || err == io.ErrUnexpectedEOF {
				return
			}
			if err != nil {
				failAt(ctx, where, "could not read "+path+": "+reason(err))
			}
		}
	}
}

// reason is the operating system's message without the path Go prefixes.
func reason(err error) string {
	if wrapped, ok := err.(*os.PathError); ok {
		return wrapped.Err.Error()
	}
	return err.Error()
}

// ioStream reads standard input line by line as the stream is consumed.
func ioStream(ctx *actor.Context, where string) iter.Seq[string] {
	return func(yield func(string) bool) {
		for {
			line, ok := ioReadLine(ctx, where).(Toption_c1[string])
			if !ok || !yield(line.F0) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

// ---- Kernel ----

func kernelAbs[A int64 | float64](ctx *actor.Context, x A, where string) A {
	if x < 0 {
		if whole, ok := any(x).(int64); ok {
			return any(neg(ctx, whole, where)).(A)
		}
		return -x
	}
	return x
}

func kernelMin[A any](x, y A, less func(A, A) bool) A {
	if less(y, x) {
		return y
	}
	return x
}

func kernelMax[A any](x, y A, less func(A, A) bool) A {
	if less(x, y) {
		return y
	}
	return x
}

func kernelCeil(ctx *actor.Context, x float64, where string) int64 {
	return floatWhole(ctx, math.Ceil(x), where)
}

func kernelFloor(ctx *actor.Context, x float64, where string) int64 {
	return floatWhole(ctx, math.Floor(x), where)
}

func kernelHd[A any](ctx *actor.Context, l L[A], where string) A {
	if len(l.S) == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the list is empty")
	}
	return lat(l, 0)
}

func kernelTl[A any](ctx *actor.Context, l L[A], where string) L[A] {
	if len(l.S) == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the list is empty")
	}
	return lfrom(l, 1)
}

func kernelToString[A any](x A, show func(A) string) string { return show(x) }

func kernelThen[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, x A, f func(*actor.Context, A) B) B { return f(ctx, x) }

func kernelTap[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, x A, f func(*actor.Context, A) B) A {
	f(ctx, x)
	return x
}

func kernelMapSize[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V]) int64 { return int64(actor.MapLen(m)) }
func kernelByteSize(s string) int64                               { return int64(len(s)) }
func kernelIdentity[A any](x A) A                                 { return x }

// ---- List ----

func listFirst[A any](l L[A]) Toption[A] {
	if len(l.S) == 0 {
		return noneOf[A]()
	}
	return someOf(lat(l, 0))
}

func listFirstOr[A any](l L[A], fallback A) A {
	if len(l.S) == 0 {
		return fallback
	}
	return lat(l, 0)
}

func listLast[A any](l L[A]) Toption[A] {
	if len(l.S) == 0 {
		return noneOf[A]()
	}
	return someOf(l.S[0])
}

func listLastOr[A any](l L[A], fallback A) A {
	if len(l.S) == 0 {
		return fallback
	}
	return l.S[0]
}

func listLastBang[A any](ctx *actor.Context, l L[A], where string) A {
	if len(l.S) == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the list is empty")
	}
	return l.S[0]
}

func listFoldr[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], acc B, f func(*actor.Context, A, B) B) B {
	all := collect(s)
	for i := len(all) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		acc = f(ctx, all[i], acc)
	}
	return acc
}

func listDeleteL[A any](l L[A], value A, eq func(A, A) bool) L[A] {
	for at := len(l.S) - 1; at >= 0; at-- {
		if eq(l.S[at], value) {
			out := make([]A, len(l.S)-1)
			copy(out, l.S[:at])
			copy(out[at:], l.S[at+1:])
			return L[A]{S: out}
		}
	}
	return l
}

func listDelete[A any](s iter.Seq[A], value A, eq func(A, A) bool) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	for i, x := range all {
		if eq(x, value) {
			return mk(append(all[:i:i], all[i+1:]...))
		}
	}
	return mk(all)
}

// position resolves an index that may count from the end; ok is false when
// it falls outside the list.
func position(index int64, size int) (int, bool) {
	if index < 0 {
		index += int64(size)
	}
	return int(index), index >= 0 && index < int64(size)
}

func listDeleteAt[A any](s iter.Seq[A], index int64) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	if i, ok := position(index, len(all)); ok {
		all = append(all[:i:i], all[i+1:]...)
	}
	return mk(all)
}

// The L variants edit a copy of the list's storage, where list index i is
// S[len(S)-1-i].
func listDeleteAtL[A any](l L[A], index int64) L[A] {
	i, ok := position(index, len(l.S))
	if !ok {
		return l
	}
	at := len(l.S) - 1 - i
	out := make([]A, len(l.S)-1)
	copy(out, l.S[:at])
	copy(out[at:], l.S[at+1:])
	return L[A]{S: out}
}

func listInsertAtL[A any](l L[A], index int64, value A) L[A] {
	at := index
	if index < 0 {
		at = int64(len(l.S)) + index + 1
	}
	at = max(0, min(at, int64(len(l.S))))
	stored := len(l.S) - int(at)
	out := make([]A, len(l.S)+1)
	copy(out, l.S[:stored])
	out[stored] = value
	copy(out[stored+1:], l.S[stored:])
	return L[A]{S: out}
}

func listReplaceAtL[A any](l L[A], index int64, value A) L[A] {
	i, ok := position(index, len(l.S))
	if !ok {
		return l
	}
	out := append([]A(nil), l.S...)
	out[len(out)-1-i] = value
	return L[A]{S: out}
}

func listUpdateAtL[A any](ctx *actor.Context, l L[A], index int64, f func(*actor.Context, A) A) L[A] {
	i, ok := position(index, len(l.S))
	if !ok {
		return l
	}
	out := append([]A(nil), l.S...)
	out[len(out)-1-i] = f(ctx, out[len(out)-1-i])
	return L[A]{S: out}
}

func listDuplicate[A any](ctx *actor.Context, value A, count int64, where string) L[A] {
	if count < 0 || count > 1<<26 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "duplicate needs a count between 0 and 67108864")
	}
	out := make([]A, count)
	for i := range out {
		out[i] = value
	}
	return L[A]{S: out}
}

// insert_at places the value at the index; past either end it lands at that
// end, and -1 appends.
func listInsertAt[A any](s iter.Seq[A], index int64, value A) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	at := index
	if index < 0 {
		at = int64(len(all)) + index + 1
	}
	at = max(0, min(at, int64(len(all))))
	out := append(append(append([]A{}, all[:at]...), value), all[at:]...)
	return mk(out)
}

func listReplaceAt[A any](s iter.Seq[A], index int64, value A) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	if i, ok := position(index, len(all)); ok {
		all[i] = value
	}
	return mk(all)
}

func listUpdateAt[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], index int64, f func(*actor.Context, A) A) L[A] {
	all := collect(s)
	if i, ok := position(index, len(all)); ok {
		all[i] = f(ctx, all[i])
	}
	return mk(all)
}

func listPopAt[A any](s iter.Seq[A], index int64) pair[Toption[A], L[A]] {
	all := collect(s)
	i, ok := position(index, len(all))
	if !ok {
		return pair[Toption[A], L[A]]{noneOf[A](), mk(all)}
	}
	value := all[i]
	return pair[Toption[A], L[A]]{someOf(value), mk(append(all[:i:i], all[i+1:]...))}
}

func listStartsWith[A any](s, prefix iter.Seq[A], eq func(A, A) bool) bool {
	all, head := collect(s), collect(prefix)
	if len(head) > len(all) {
		return false
	}
	for i, x := range head {
		if !eq(all[i], x) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func listEndsWith[A any](s, suffix iter.Seq[A], eq func(A, A) bool) bool {
	all, tail := collect(s), collect(suffix)
	if len(tail) > len(all) {
		return false
	}
	return listStartsWith(seqSlice(all[len(all)-len(tail):]), seqSlice(tail), eq)
}

// ---- Map ----

func mapGetOption[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], key K) Toption[V] {
	if value, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
		return someOf(value)
	}
	return noneOf[V]()
}

func mapGetLazy[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, f func(*actor.Context) V) V {
	if value, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
		return value
	}
	return f(ctx)
}

func mapPutNew[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], key K, value V) actor.Map[K, V] {
	if _, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
		return m
	}
	return actor.MapPut(m, key, value)
}

func mapPutNewLazy[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, f func(*actor.Context) V) actor.Map[K, V] {
	if _, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
		return m
	}
	return actor.MapPut(m, key, f(ctx))
}

func mapDrop[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], keys iter.Seq[K]) actor.Map[K, V] {
	keys(func(key K) bool { m = actor.MapDelete(m, key); return true })
	return m
}

func mapTake[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], keys iter.Seq[K]) actor.Map[K, V] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, V]{})
	keys(func(key K) bool {
		if value, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
			out.Put(key, value)
		}
		return true
	})
	return out.Map()
}

func mapSplit[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], keys iter.Seq[K]) pair[actor.Map[K, V], actor.Map[K, V]] {
	all := collect(keys)
	return pair[actor.Map[K, V], actor.Map[K, V]]{mapTake(m, seqSlice(all)), mapDrop(m, seqSlice(all))}
}

func mapNew[K comparable, V any](s iter.Seq[pair[K, V]]) actor.Map[K, V] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, V]{})
	s(func(entry pair[K, V]) bool { out.Put(entry.E0, entry.E1); return true })
	return out.Map()
}

func mapNewBy[A any, K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) pair[K, V]) actor.Map[K, V] {
	return mapNew(streamMap(ctx, s, f))
}

func mapFromKeys[K comparable, V any](keys iter.Seq[K], value V) actor.Map[K, V] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, V]{})
	keys(func(key K) bool { out.Put(key, value); return true })
	return out.Map()
}

func mapSplitWith[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], f func(*actor.Context, pair[K, V]) bool, less func(K, K) bool) pair[actor.Map[K, V], actor.Map[K, V]] {
	yes, no := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, V]{}), actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, V]{})
	seqMap(m, less)(func(entry pair[K, V]) bool {
		if f(ctx, entry) {
			yes.Put(entry.E0, entry.E1)
		} else {
			no.Put(entry.E0, entry.E1)
		}
		return true
	})
	return pair[actor.Map[K, V], actor.Map[K, V]]{yes.Map(), no.Map()}
}

func mapFilter[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], f func(*actor.Context, pair[K, V]) bool, less func(K, K) bool) actor.Map[K, V] {
	return mapSplitWith(ctx, m, f, less).E0
}

func mapReject[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], f func(*actor.Context, pair[K, V]) bool, less func(K, K) bool) actor.Map[K, V] {
	return mapSplitWith(ctx, m, f, less).E1
}

func mapUpdate[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, initial V, f func(*actor.Context, V) V) actor.Map[K, V] {
	if value, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
		return actor.MapPut(m, key, f(ctx, value))
	}
	return actor.MapPut(m, key, initial)
}

func mapUpdateBang[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, f func(*actor.Context, V) V, where string) actor.Map[K, V] {
	return actor.MapPut(m, key, f(ctx, lookup(ctx, m, key, where)))
}

func mapReplace[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], key K, value V) actor.Map[K, V] {
	if _, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); !ok {
		return m
	}
	return actor.MapPut(m, key, value)
}

func mapReplaceBang[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, value V, where string) actor.Map[K, V] {
	lookup(ctx, m, key, where)
	return actor.MapPut(m, key, value)
}

// mapGetAndUpdate passes the value under the key, or none, to the function,
// which returns what to give back and the new value, or none to remove it.
func mapGetAndUpdate[K comparable, V, R any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, f func(*actor.Context, Toption[V]) pair[R, Toption[V]]) pair[R, actor.Map[K, V]] {
	result := f(ctx, mapGetOption(m, key))
	if value, ok := result.E1.(Toption_c1[V]); ok {
		return pair[R, actor.Map[K, V]]{result.E0, actor.MapPut(m, key, value.F0)}
	}
	return pair[R, actor.Map[K, V]]{result.E0, actor.MapDelete(m, key)}
}

func mapPopLazy[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, f func(*actor.Context) V) pair[V, actor.Map[K, V]] {
	if value, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
		return pair[V, actor.Map[K, V]]{value, actor.MapDelete(m, key)}
	}
	return pair[V, actor.Map[K, V]]{f(ctx), m}
}

func mapReplaceLazy[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, m actor.Map[K, V], key K, f func(*actor.Context, V) V) actor.Map[K, V] {
	if value, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key); ok {
		return actor.MapPut(m, key, f(ctx, value))
	}
	return m
}

func mapPop[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], key K) pair[Toption[V], actor.Map[K, V]] {
	return pair[Toption[V], actor.Map[K, V]]{mapGetOption(m, key), actor.MapDelete(m, key)}
}

func mapPopOr[K comparable, V any](m actor.Map[K, V], key K, fallback V) pair[V, actor.Map[K, V]] {
	value, ok := actor.MapGet(m, key)
	if !ok {
		value = fallback
	}
	return pair[V, actor.Map[K, V]]{value, actor.MapDelete(m, key)}
}

func mapMergeWith[K comparable, V any](ctx *actor.Context, a, b actor.Map[K, V], f func(*actor.Context, K, V, V) V, less func(K, K) bool) actor.Map[K, V] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(a)
	seqMap(b, less)(func(entry pair[K, V]) bool {
		if existing, ok := out.Get(entry.E0); ok {
			out.Put(entry.E0, f(ctx, entry.E0, existing, entry.E1))
		} else {
			out.Put(entry.E0, entry.E1)
		}
		return true
	})
	return out.Map()
}

// intersect keeps the keys both maps have, with the second map's values.
func mapIntersect[K comparable, V any](a, b actor.Map[K, V]) actor.Map[K, V] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[K, V]{})
	actor.MapEach(b, func(key K, value V) {
		if _, ok := actor.MapGet(a, key); ok {
			out.Put(key, value)
		}
	})
	return out.Map()
}

// ---- MapSet ----

type set[A comparable] = actor.Map[A, struct{}]

func setNew[A comparable]() set[A] { return set[A]{} }

func setFrom[A comparable](s iter.Seq[A]) set[A] {
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(set[A]{})
	s(func(x A) bool { out.Put(x, struct{}{}); return true })
	return out.Map()
}

func setFromBy[A any, B comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) set[B] {
	return setFrom(streamMap(ctx, s, f))
}

func setPut[A comparable](s set[A], x A) set[A]    { return actor.MapPut(s, x, struct{}{}) }
func setDelete[A comparable](s set[A], x A) set[A] { return actor.MapDelete(s, x) }

func setUnion[A comparable](a, b set[A]) set[A] {
	if actor.MapLen(a) < actor.MapLen(b) {
		a, b = b, a
	}
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(a)
	actor.MapEach(b, func(x A, _ struct{}) { out.Put(x, struct{}{}) })
	return out.Map()
}

// setShared counts the elements the two sets have in common, visiting the
// smaller one.
func setShared[A comparable](a, b set[A]) int {
	if actor.MapLen(b) < actor.MapLen(a) {
		a, b = b, a
	}
	shared := 0
	actor.MapEach(a, func(x A, _ struct{}) {
		if mapHas(b, x) {
			shared++
		}
	})
	return shared
}

func setIntersection[A comparable](a, b set[A]) set[A] {
	if actor.MapLen(b) < actor.MapLen(a) {
		a, b = b, a
	}
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(set[A]{})
	actor.MapEach(a, func(x A, _ struct{}) {
		if mapHas(b, x) {
			out.Put(x, struct{}{})
		}
	})
	return out.Map()
}

// difference removes a few elements from a one at a time, which shares most
// of a; when b is large it is cheaper to build the result from what is left.
func setDifference[A comparable](a, b set[A]) set[A] {
	if actor.MapLen(b)*8 < actor.MapLen(a) {
		actor.MapEach(b, func(x A, _ struct{}) { a = actor.MapDelete(a, x) })
		return a
	}
	out := actor.NewMapBuilder(set[A]{})
	actor.MapEach(a, func(x A, _ struct{}) {
		if !mapHas(b, x) {
			out.Put(x, struct{}{})
		}
	})
	return out.Map()
}

func setSymmetricDifference[A comparable](a, b set[A]) set[A] {
	return setUnion(setDifference(a, b), setDifference(b, a))
}

func setSubset[A comparable](a, b set[A]) bool {
	return actor.MapLen(a) <= actor.MapLen(b) && setShared(a, b) == actor.MapLen(a)
}

func setDisjoint[A comparable](a, b set[A]) bool { return setShared(a, b) == 0 }
func setEqual[A comparable](a, b set[A]) bool {
	return actor.MapLen(a) == actor.MapLen(b) && setShared(a, b) == actor.MapLen(a)
}

func setSplitWith[A comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s set[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool, less func(A, A) bool) pair[set[A], set[A]] {
	yes, no := actor.NewMapBuilder(set[A]{}), actor.NewMapBuilder(set[A]{})
	seqSet(s, less)(func(x A) bool {
		if f(ctx, x) {
			yes.Put(x, struct{}{})
		} else {
			no.Put(x, struct{}{})
		}
		return true
	})
	return pair[set[A], set[A]]{yes.Map(), no.Map()}
}

func setFilter[A comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s set[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool, less func(A, A) bool) set[A] {
	return setSplitWith(ctx, s, f, less).E0
}

func setReject[A comparable](ctx *actor.Context, s set[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) bool, less func(A, A) bool) set[A] {
	return setSplitWith(ctx, s, f, less).E1
}

// ---- Graphemes ----

// Grapheme clusters, by the rules of Unicode's UAX #29: what a reader takes
// as one character, such as a letter with its accents, a flag or an emoji
// sequence joined by ZWJ. Indic conjunct rules are not applied.
type graphemeClass uint8

const (
	gcOther graphemeClass = iota
	gcCR
	gcLF
	gcControl
	gcExtend
	gcZWJ
	gcRegional
	gcPrepend
	gcSpacingMark
	gcL
	gcV
	gcT
	gcLV
	gcLVT
	gcPictographic
)

func graphemeClassOf(r rune) graphemeClass {
	switch {
	case r < 0x7f:
		switch {
		case r == '\r':
			return gcCR
		case r == '\n':
			return gcLF
		case r < 0x20:
			return gcControl
		}
		return gcOther
	case r == 0x200d:
		return gcZWJ
	case r == 0x200c, r >= 0x1f3fb && r <= 0x1f3ff, r >= 0xe0020 && r <= 0xe007f, r >= 0xfe00 && r <= 0xfe0f:
		return gcExtend
	case r >= 0x1f1e6 && r <= 0x1f1ff:
		return gcRegional
	case r >= 0x1100 && r <= 0x115f, r >= 0xa960 && r <= 0xa97c:
		return gcL
	case r >= 0x1160 && r <= 0x11a7, r >= 0xd7b0 && r <= 0xd7c6:
		return gcV
	case r >= 0x11a8 && r <= 0x11ff, r >= 0xd7cb && r <= 0xd7fb:
		return gcT
	case r >= 0xac00 && r <= 0xd7a3:
		if (r-0xac00)%28 == 0 {
			return gcLV
		}
		return gcLVT
	case r >= 0x600 && r <= 0x605, r == 0x6dd, r == 0x70f, r == 0x890, r == 0x891, r == 0x8e2, r == 0x110bd, r == 0x110cd:
		return gcPrepend
	case unicode.In(r, unicode.Mn, unicode.Me):
		return gcExtend
	case unicode.Is(unicode.Mc, r):
		return gcSpacingMark
	case unicode.In(r, unicode.Cc, unicode.Zl, unicode.Zp, unicode.Cf):
		return gcControl
	case pictographic(r):
		return gcPictographic
	}
	return gcOther
}

// pictographic approximates Extended_Pictographic with its main blocks.
func pictographic(r rune) bool {
	switch {
	case r == 0xa9, r == 0xae, r == 0x203c, r == 0x2049, r == 0x2122, r == 0x2139, r == 0x3030, r == 0x303d, r == 0x3297, r == 0x3299:
		return true
	case r >= 0x2194 && r <= 0x21aa, r >= 0x231a && r <= 0x23ff, r >= 0x24c2 && r <= 0x24c2, r >= 0x25aa && r <= 0x25fe:
		return true
	case r >= 0x2600 && r <= 0x27bf, r >= 0x2934 && r <= 0x2935, r >= 0x2b05 && r <= 0x2b55:
		return true
	case r >= 0x1f000 && r <= 0x1faff && !(r >= 0x1f1e6 && r <= 0x1f1ff) && !(r >= 0x1f3fb && r <= 0x1f3ff):
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// graphemeLen is the length in bytes of the first grapheme cluster of s.
func graphemeLen(s string) int {
	first, size := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
	if size == len(s) {
		return size
	}
	prev := graphemeClassOf(first)
	regional := 0
	if prev == gcRegional {
		regional = 1
	}
	// emoji is set while the cluster is a pictograph followed by extenders,
	// and joined is set when a ZWJ ends such a run.
	emoji, joined := prev == gcPictographic, false
	i := size
	for i < len(s) {
		r, n := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s[i:])
		next := graphemeClassOf(r)
		join := false
		switch {
		case prev == gcCR && next == gcLF:
			join = true
		case prev == gcCR || prev == gcLF || prev == gcControl || next == gcCR || next == gcLF || next == gcControl:
		case prev == gcL && (next == gcL || next == gcV || next == gcLV || next == gcLVT):
			join = true
		case (prev == gcLV || prev == gcV) && (next == gcV || next == gcT):
			join = true
		case (prev == gcLVT || prev == gcT) && next == gcT:
			join = true
		case next == gcExtend || next == gcZWJ || next == gcSpacingMark || prev == gcPrepend:
			join = true
		case joined && next == gcPictographic:
			join = true
		case prev == gcRegional && next == gcRegional && regional%2 == 1:
			join = true
		}
		if !join {
			break
		}
		switch {
		case next == gcRegional:
			regional++
		case next == gcPictographic:
			emoji, joined = true, false
		case next == gcZWJ:
			joined = emoji
		case next != gcExtend:
			emoji, joined = false, false
		}
		prev = next
		i += n
	}
	return i
}

// simple reports a string whose clusters are its bytes: ASCII without a
// carriage return.
func simple(s string) bool {
	for i := 0; i < len(s); i++ {
		if s[i] >= 0x80 || s[i] == '\r' {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func graphemes(s string) []string {
	var out []string
	for len(s) > 0 {
		n := graphemeLen(s)
		out = append(out, s[:n])
		s = s[n:]
	}
	return out
}

func graphemeCount(s string) int64 {
	if simple(s) {
		return int64(len(s))
	}
	n := int64(0)
	for len(s) > 0 {
		s = s[graphemeLen(s):]
		n++
	}
	return n
}

func strGraphemes(s string) L[string] { return mk(graphemes(s)) }

// strNextGrapheme splits off the first grapheme cluster.
func strNextGrapheme(s string) Toption[pair[string, string]] {
	if s == "" {
		return noneOf[pair[string, string]]()
	}
	n := graphemeLen(s)
	return someOf(pair[string, string]{s[:n], s[n:]})
}

	"230;1e8d0:220;1e8d1:220;1e8d2:220;1e8d3:220;1e8d4:220;1e8d5:220;1e8d6:220;1e944:230;1e945:230;1e946:" +
	"230;1e947:230;1e948:230;1e949:230;1e94a:7"

// normTables are the tables above, read the first time they are needed.
var normTables = sync.OnceValue(func() (t struct {
	decompose map[rune][]rune
	compat    map[rune]bool
	combining map[rune]uint8
	compose   map[[2]rune]rune
}) {
	t.decompose, t.compat, t.combining, t.compose = map[rune][]rune{}, map[rune]bool{}, map[rune]uint8{}, map[[2]rune]rune{}
	hex := func(text string) rune {
		n, _ := strconv.ParseUint(text, 16, 32)
		return rune(n)
	}
	for _, entry := range strings.Split(normDecompositions, ";") {
		key, value, _ := strings.Cut(entry, ":")
		compat, composes := strings.HasPrefix(key, "!"), strings.Contains(key, "*")
		r := hex(strings.TrimLeft(key, "!*"))
		var runes []rune
		for _, part := range strings.Fields(value) {
			runes = append(runes, hex(part))
		}
		t.decompose[r], t.compat[r] = runes, compat
		if composes {
			t.compose[[2]rune{runes[0], runes[1]}] = r
		}
	}
	for _, entry := range strings.Split(normCombining, ";") {
		key, value, _ := strings.Cut(entry, ":")
		class, _ := strconv.Atoi(value)
		t.combining[hex(key)] = uint8(class)
	}
	return t
})

// Hangul syllables decompose and compose by arithmetic.
const (
	hangulBase, hangulL, hangulV, hangulT = 0xac00, 0x1100, 0x1161, 0x11a7
	hangulVCount, hangulTCount            = 21, 28
	hangulCount                           = 19 * hangulVCount * hangulTCount
)

func normDecompose(r rune, compat bool, out []rune) []rune {
	if s := r - hangulBase; s >= 0 && s < hangulCount {
		out = append(out, hangulL+s/(hangulVCount*hangulTCount), hangulV+(s%(hangulVCount*hangulTCount))/hangulTCount)
		if t := s % hangulTCount; t != 0 {
			out = append(out, hangulT+t)
		}
		return out
	}
	tables := normTables()
	if parts, ok := tables.decompose[r]; ok && (compat || !tables.compat[r]) {
		for _, part := range parts {
			out = normDecompose(part, compat, out)
		}
		return out
	}
	return append(out, r)
}

// normCompose combines starters with the marks that follow them, as the
// canonical composition algorithm does.
func normCompose(runes []rune) []rune {
	tables := normTables()
	if len(runes) == 0 {
		return runes
	}
	out := []rune{runes[0]}
	starter, last := 0, uint8(0)
	if tables.combining[runes[0]] != 0 {
		last = 255
	}
	for _, r := range runes[1:] {
		class := tables.combining[r]
		s := out[starter]
		// Hangul L V and LV T.
		if l := s - hangulL; l >= 0 && l < 19 && r >= hangulV && r < hangulV+hangulVCount && starter == len(out)-1 {
			out[starter] = hangulBase + (l*hangulVCount+(r-hangulV))*hangulTCount
			continue
		}
		if lv := s - hangulBase; lv >= 0 && lv < hangulCount && lv%hangulTCount == 0 && r > hangulT && r < hangulT+hangulTCount && starter == len(out)-1 {
			out[starter] = s + (r - hangulT)
			continue
		}
		if composed, ok := tables.compose[[2]rune{s, r}]; ok && (last < class || last == 0 && starter == len(out)-1) && last != 255 {
			out[starter] = composed
			continue
		}
		if class == 0 {
			starter = len(out)
			last = 0
		} else {
			last = class
		}
		out = append(out, r)
	}
	return out
}

func strNormalize(ctx *actor.Context, s string, form actor.Atom, where string) string {
	compat, compose := false, false
	switch form {
	case "nfd":
	case "nfc":
		compose = true
	case "nfkd":
