		compat = true
	case "nfkc":
		compat, compose = true, true
	default:
		failAt(ctx, where, "String.normalize takes :nfc, :nfd, :nfkc or :nfkd")
	}
	if simple(s) {
		return s
	}
	tables := normTables()
	var runes []rune
	for _, r := range s {
		runes = normDecompose(r, compat, runes)
	}
	// Canonical ordering: marks between starters sorted by combining class.
	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
		j := i
		for j < len(runes) && tables.combining[runes[j]] != 0 {
			j++
		}
		if j > i {
			sort.SliceStable(runes[i:j], func(a, b int) bool { return tables.combining[runes[i+a]] < tables.combining[runes[i+b]] })
			i = j
		} else {
			i++
		}
	}
	if compose {
		runes = normCompose(runes)
	}
	return string(runes)
}

// ---- String ----

// strJaro is the Jaro distance of two strings, from 0.0 for nothing in
// common to 1.0 for equal, over their grapheme clusters.
func strJaro(a, b string) float64 {
	x, y := graphemes(a), graphemes(b)
	if len(x) == 0 && len(y) == 0 {
		return 1
	}
	if len(x) == 0 || len(y) == 0 {
		return 0
	}
	window := max(len(x), len(y))/2 - 1
	window = max(window, 0)
	matchedX, matchedY := make([]bool, len(x)), make([]bool, len(y))
	matches := 0
	for i := range x {
		for j := max(0, i-window); j < min(len(y), i+window+1); j++ {
			if !matchedY[j] && x[i] == y[j] {
				matchedX[i], matchedY[j] = true, true
				matches++
				break
			}
		}
	}
	if matches == 0 {
		return 0
	}
	transpositions, j := 0, 0
	for i := range x {
		if !matchedX[i] {
			continue
		}
		for !matchedY[j] {
			j++
		}
		if x[i] != y[j] {
			transpositions++
		}
		j++
	}
	m := float64(matches)
	return (m/float64(len(x)) + m/float64(len(y)) + (m-float64(transpositions/2))/m) / 3
}

// strMyersDifference gives the edits that turn one string into another, as
// {:eq | :del | :ins, text} runs over grapheme clusters.
func strMyersDifference(a, b string) L[pair[actor.Atom, string]] {
	return mk(myers(graphemes(a), graphemes(b), func(x, y string) bool { return x == y }, func(run []string) string { return strings.Join(run, "") }))
}

// strSplitter splits lazily at any of the patterns.
func strSplitter(ctx *actor.Context, s string, patterns iter.Seq[string], where string) iter.Seq[string] {
	var list []string
	for p := range patterns {
		if p == "" {
			failAt(ctx, where, "String.splitter needs patterns that are not empty")
		}
		list = append(list, p)
	}
	return func(yield func(string) bool) {
		rest := s
		for {
			at, size := -1, 0
			for _, p := range list {
				if i := strings.Index(rest, p); i >= 0 && (at < 0 || i < at) {
					at, size = i, len(p)
				}
			}
			if at < 0 {
				yield(rest)
				return
			}
			if !yield(rest[:at]) {
				return
			}
			rest = rest[at+size:]
		}
	}
}

func strSplitterOne(ctx *actor.Context, s, pattern string, where string) iter.Seq[string] {
	return strSplitter(ctx, s, seqSlice([]string{pattern}), where)
}

// caseMode applies a case mapping by mode: :default for Unicode, :ascii for
// the letters a to z alone, :turkic for the dotted and dotless i, and
// :greek for the final sigma.
func caseMode(ctx *actor.Context, s string, mode actor.Atom, upper bool, where string) string {
	switch mode {
	case "default", "greek":
		if upper {
			return strings.ToUpper(s)
		}
		lower := strings.ToLower(s)
		if mode == "greek" {
			lower = finalSigma(lower)
		}
		return lower
	case "ascii":
		return strings.Map(func(r rune) rune {
			switch {
			case upper && r >= 'a' && r <= 'z':
				return r - 32
			case !upper && r >= 'A' && r <= 'Z':
				return r + 32
			}
			return r
		}, s)
	case "turkic":
		if upper {
			return strings.ToUpperSpecial(unicode.TurkishCase, s)
		}
		return strings.ToLowerSpecial(unicode.TurkishCase, s)
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "the case mode is :default, :ascii, :greek or :turkic, not :"+string(mode))
	return s
}

// finalSigma writes σ as ς at the end of a word.
func finalSigma(s string) string {
	runes := []rune(s)
	for i, r := range runes {
		if r == 'σ' && i > 0 && unicode.IsLetter(runes[i-1]) && (i+1 == len(runes) || !unicode.IsLetter(runes[i+1])) {
			runes[i] = 'ς'
		}
	}
	return string(runes)
}

func strUpcaseMode(ctx *actor.Context, s string, mode actor.Atom, where string) string {
	return caseMode(ctx, s, mode, true, where)
}

func strDowncaseMode(ctx *actor.Context, s string, mode actor.Atom, where string) string {
	return caseMode(ctx, s, mode, false, where)
}

func strCapitalizeMode(ctx *actor.Context, s string, mode actor.Atom, where string) string {
	if s == "" {
		return s
	}
	n := graphemeLen(s)
	first := caseMode(ctx, s[:n], mode, true, where)
	if mode == "default" || mode == "greek" {
		first = strings.ToTitle(s[:n])
	}
	return first + caseMode(ctx, s[n:], mode, false, where)
}


func strAt(s string, index int64) Toption[string] {
	clusters := graphemes(s)
	if i, ok := position(index, len(clusters)); ok {
		return someOf(clusters[i])
	}
	return noneOf[string]()
}

func strFirst(s string) Toption[string] { return strAt(s, 0) }
func strLast(s string) Toption[string]  { return strAt(s, -1) }

func strCapitalize(s string) string {
	if s == "" {
		return s
	}
	n := graphemeLen(s)
	return strings.ToTitle(s[:n]) + strings.ToLower(s[n:])
}

func strCodepoints(s string) L[string] {
	var out []string
	for _, r := range s {
		out = append(out, string(r))
	}
	return mk(out)
}

func strDuplicate(ctx *actor.Context, s string, count int64, where string) string {
	if count < 0 || count*int64(len(s)) > 1<<30 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "duplicate needs a nonnegative count and a result under 1 GiB")
	}
	return strings.Repeat(s, int(count))
}

func padding(s string, width int64, with string) string {
	missing := width - graphemeCount(s)
	if missing <= 0 || with == "" {
		return ""
	}
	fill := []rune(strings.Repeat(with, int(missing)/utf8.RuneCountInString(with)+1))
	return string(fill[:missing])
}

func strPadLeadingWith(s string, width int64, with string) string { return padding(s, width, with) + s }
func strPadTrailingWith(s string, width int64, with string) string {
	return s + padding(s, width, with)
}
func strPadLeading(s string, width int64) string  { return strPadLeadingWith(s, width, " ") }
func strPadTrailing(s string, width int64) string { return strPadTrailingWith(s, width, " ") }
func strFields(s string) L[string]                { return mk(strings.Fields(s)) }

// strContainsAny and its kin take a list of patterns, any of which may match.
func strContainsAny(s string, patterns L[string]) bool {
	return slices.ContainsFunc(patterns.S, func(p string) bool { return strings.Contains(s, p) })
}

func strStartsWithAny(s string, prefixes L[string]) bool {
	return slices.ContainsFunc(prefixes.S, func(p string) bool { return strings.HasPrefix(s, p) })
}

func strEndsWithAny(s string, suffixes L[string]) bool {
	return slices.ContainsFunc(suffixes.S, func(p string) bool { return strings.HasSuffix(s, p) })
}

func strSplitAt(s string, index int64) pair[string, string] {
	runes := []rune(s)
	at := index
	if index < 0 {
		at = max(int64(len(runes))+index, 0)
	}
	at = min(at, int64(len(runes)))
	return pair[string, string]{string(runes[:at]), string(runes[at:])}
}

func strToIntegerBase(ctx *actor.Context, s string, base int64, where string) int64 {
	if base < 2 || base > 36 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "base must be between 2 and 36")
	}
	value, err := strconv.ParseInt(s, int(base), 64)
	if err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, strconv.Quote(s)+" is not an integer")
	}
	return value
}

func strToInteger(ctx *actor.Context, s string, where string) int64 {
	return strToIntegerBase(ctx, s, 10, where)
}

func strToFloat(ctx *actor.Context, s string, where string) float64 {
	value, err := strconv.ParseFloat(s, 64)
	if err != nil || math.IsInf(value, 0) || math.IsNaN(value) {
		failAt(ctx, where, strconv.Quote(s)+" is not a float")
	}
	return value
}

func strTrimLeading(s string) string  { return strings.TrimLeftFunc(s, unicode.IsSpace) }
func strTrimTrailing(s string) string { return strings.TrimRightFunc(s, unicode.IsSpace) }

func strTrimLeadingOf(s, remove string) string {
	for remove != "" && strings.HasPrefix(s, remove) {
		s = s[len(remove):]
	}
	return s
}

func strTrimTrailingOf(s, remove string) string {
	for remove != "" && strings.HasSuffix(s, remove) {
		s = s[:len(s)-len(remove)]
	}
	return s
}

func strTrimBoth(s, remove string) string {
	return strTrimTrailingOf(strTrimLeadingOf(s, remove), remove)
}

func strReplacePrefix(s, match, with string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(s, match) {
		return with + s[len(match):]
	}
	return s
}

func strReplaceSuffix(s, match, with string) string {
	if strings.HasSuffix(s, match) {
		return s[:len(s)-len(match)] + with
	}
	return s
}

// replace_leading and replace_trailing replace every repetition of match at
// that end of the string.
func strReplaceLeading(s, match, with string) string {
	rest := strTrimLeadingOf(s, match)
	if match == "" {
		return s
	}
	return strings.Repeat(with, (len(s)-len(rest))/len(match)) + rest
}

func strReplaceTrailing(s, match, with string) string {
	rest := strTrimTrailingOf(s, match)
	if match == "" {
		return s
	}
	return rest + strings.Repeat(with, (len(s)-len(rest))/len(match))
}

func strPrintable(s string) bool {
	if !utf8.ValidString(s) {
		return false
	}
	for _, r := range s {
		if !unicode.IsPrint(r) && r != '\n' && r != '\t' && r != '\r' {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func strCount(s, pattern string) int64 {
	if pattern == "" {
		return 0
	}
	return int64(strings.Count(s, pattern))
}

func strNextCodepoint(s string) Toption[pair[string, string]] {
	if s == "" {
		return noneOf[pair[string, string]]()
	}
	_, size := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
	return someOf(pair[string, string]{s[:size], s[size:]})
}

// ---- Integer and Float ----

func baseCheck(ctx *actor.Context, base int64, where string) {
	if base < 2 || base > 36 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "base must be between 2 and 36")
	}
}

func intToStringBase(ctx *actor.Context, value, base int64, where string) string {
	baseCheck(ctx, base, where)
	// Digits above 9 are written in upper case.
	var space [65]byte
	text := strconv.AppendInt(space[:0], value, int(base))
	for i, c := range text {
		if c >= 'a' {
			text[i] = c - 'a' + 'A'
		}
	}
	return string(text)
}

func intParseBase(ctx *actor.Context, s string, base int64, where string) Tresult[int64, string] {
	baseCheck(ctx, base, where)
	value, err := strconv.ParseInt(s, int(base), 64)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[int64](strconv.Quote(s) + " is not an integer in base " + strconv.FormatInt(base, 10))
	}
	return okOf(value)
}

// digits are most significant first; a negative number has negative digits.
func intDigits(ctx *actor.Context, value, base int64, where string) L[int64] {
	if base < 2 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "base must be at least 2")
	}
	if value == 0 {
		return mk([]int64{0})
	}
	out := make([]int64, 0, 20)
	for ; value != 0; value /= base {
		out = append(out, value%base)
	}
	return L[int64]{S: out}
}

func intDigits10(ctx *actor.Context, value int64, where string) L[int64] {
	return intDigits(ctx, value, 10, where)
}

func intUndigits(ctx *actor.Context, digits iter.Seq[int64], base int64, where string) int64 {
	if base < 2 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "base must be at least 2")
	}
	total := int64(0)
	digits(func(digit int64) bool {
		if digit < 0 || digit >= base {
			failAt(ctx, where, "digit out of range for the base")
		}
		total = add(ctx, mul(ctx, total, base, where), digit, where)
		return true
	})
	return total
}

func intUndigits10(ctx *actor.Context, digits iter.Seq[int64], where string) int64 {
	return intUndigits(ctx, digits, 10, where)
}

func intGcd(ctx *actor.Context, a, b int64, where string) int64 {
	for b != 0 {
		a, b = b, a%b
	}
	return kernelAbs(ctx, a, where)
}

// extended_gcd returns {gcd, m, n} with m*a + n*b == gcd.
func intExtendedGcd(a, b int64) struct{ E0, E1, E2 int64 } {
	oldR, r, oldS, s, oldT, t := a, b, int64(1), int64(0), int64(0), int64(1)
	for r != 0 {
		q := oldR / r
		oldR, r = r, oldR-q*r
		oldS, s = s, oldS-q*s
		oldT, t = t, oldT-q*t
	}
	if oldR < 0 {
		oldR, oldS, oldT = -oldR, -oldS, -oldT
	}
	return struct{ E0, E1, E2 int64 }{oldR, oldS, oldT}
}

// mod and floor_div round toward negative infinity, unlike rem and div.
func intFloorDiv(ctx *actor.Context, a, b int64, where string) int64 {
	q := div(ctx, a, b, where)
	if (a%b != 0) && ((a < 0) != (b < 0)) {
		q--
	}
	return q
}

func intCeilDiv(ctx *actor.Context, a, b int64, where string) int64 {
	q := div(ctx, a, b, where)
	if (a%b != 0) && ((a < 0) == (b < 0)) {
		q++
	}
	return q
}

func intMod(ctx *actor.Context, a, b int64, where string) int64 {
	r := mod(ctx, a, b, where)
	if r != 0 && ((r < 0) != (b < 0)) {
		r += b
	}
	return r
}

func intIsEven(n int64) bool { return n%2 == 0 }
func intIsOdd(n int64) bool  { return n%2 != 0 }

func scale(ctx *actor.Context, precision int64, where string) float64 {
	if precision < 0 || precision > 15 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "precision must be between 0 and 15")
	}
	return powersOfTen[precision]
}

// powersOfTen are exact as floats up to 10**15.
var powersOfTen = [16]float64{1, 1e1, 1e2, 1e3, 1e4, 1e5, 1e6, 1e7, 1e8, 1e9, 1e10, 1e11, 1e12, 1e13, 1e14, 1e15}

// scaled multiplies x by 10**precision and returns the nearest float with
// the exact difference between that float and the true product. Rounding
// works on the true product, which is how a float that prints as 3.7 but is
// slightly more than 3.7 has a ceiling of 3.8 at one decimal place.
func scaled(ctx *actor.Context, x float64, precision int64, where string) (product, rest, by float64) {
	by = scale(ctx, precision, where)
	product = x * by
	return product, math.FMA(x, by, -product), by
}

// exactRounding rounds x at a precision with exact arithmetic. It serves
// values so large that their scaled product is not an exact float; mode is
// -1 for floor, 1 for ceiling and 0 for nearest, halves away from zero.
func exactRounding(x float64, precision int64, mode int) float64 {
	by := new(big.Int).Exp(big.NewInt(10), big.NewInt(precision), nil)
	value := new(big.Rat).SetFloat64(x)
	value.Mul(value, new(big.Rat).SetInt(by))
	// Quo truncates toward zero; rest is what it dropped.
	whole := new(big.Int).Quo(value.Num(), value.Denom())
	rest := new(big.Rat).Sub(value, new(big.Rat).SetInt(whole))
	switch {
	case mode < 0 && rest.Sign() < 0:
		whole.Sub(whole, big.NewInt(1))
	case mode > 0 && rest.Sign() > 0:
		whole.Add(whole, big.NewInt(1))
	case mode == 0 && rest.Abs(rest).Cmp(big.NewRat(1, 2)) >= 0:
		whole.Add(whole, big.NewInt(int64(value.Sign())))
	}
	result, _ := new(big.Rat).SetFrac(whole, by).Float64()
	return result
}

// inexact reports whether the scaled product is too large to be exact.
func inexact(product float64) bool { return math.Abs(product) >= 1<<52 }

func floatRoundTo(ctx *actor.Context, x float64, precision int64, where string) float64 {
	product, rest, by := scaled(ctx, x, precision, where)
	if math.IsInf(x, 0) || math.IsNaN(x) {
		return x
	}
	if inexact(product) {
		return exactRounding(x, precision, 0)
	}
	whole := math.Trunc(product)
	switch part := product - whole; {
	case part == 0.5 && rest >= 0, part > 0.5:
		whole++
	case part == -0.5 && rest <= 0, part < -0.5:
		whole--
	}
	return whole / by
}

func floatFloorTo(ctx *actor.Context, x float64, precision int64, where string) float64 {
	product, rest, by := scaled(ctx, x, precision, where)
	if math.IsInf(x, 0) || math.IsNaN(x) {
		return x
	}
	if inexact(product) {
		return exactRounding(x, precision, -1)
	}
	whole := math.Floor(product)
	if whole == product && rest < 0 {
		whole--
	}
	return whole / by
}

func floatCeilTo(ctx *actor.Context, x float64, precision int64, where string) float64 {
	product, rest, by := scaled(ctx, x, precision, where)
	if math.IsInf(x, 0) || math.IsNaN(x) {
		return x
	}
	if inexact(product) {
		return exactRounding(x, precision, 1)
	}
	whole := math.Ceil(product)
	if whole == product && rest > 0 {
		whole++
	}
	return whole / by
}

func bitAnd(a, b int64) int64 { return a & b }
func bitOr(a, b int64) int64  { return a | b }
func bitXor(a, b int64) int64 { return a ^ b }
func bitNot(a int64) int64    { return ^a }

// Shifts by a negative amount go the other way; shifting everything out
// leaves 0, or -1 for an arithmetic right shift of a negative number.
func bitShiftLeft(a, by int64) int64 {
	if by < 0 {
		return bitShiftRight(a, -by)
	}
	if by >= 64 {
		return 0
	}
	return a << uint(by)
}

func bitShiftRight(a, by int64) int64 {
	if by < 0 {
		return bitShiftLeft(a, -by)
	}
	if by >= 64 {
		by = 63
	}
	return a >> uint(by)
}

// ---- Base ----

func decoded(text []byte, err error) Toption[string] {
	if err != nil {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	return someOf(string(text))
}

func decodedBang(ctx *actor.Context, text []byte, err error, where string) string {
	if err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the encoded text is not valid")
	}
	return string(text)
}

func baseEncode16(s string) string { return strings.ToUpper(hex.EncodeToString([]byte(s))) }
func baseDecode16(s string) Toption[string] {
	text, err := hex.DecodeString(s)
	return decoded(text, err)
}
func baseDecode16Bang(ctx *actor.Context, s string, where string) string {
	text, err := hex.DecodeString(s)
	return decodedBang(ctx, text, err, where)
}
// baseOptions reads case: :upper, :lower or :mixed and padding: true or
// false, given as atoms.
func baseOptions(ctx *actor.Context, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) (string, bool) {
	letters, padding := "upper", true
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		switch {
		case option.E0 == "case" && (option.E1 == "upper" || option.E1 == "lower" || option.E1 == "mixed"):
			letters = string(option.E1)
		case option.E0 == "padding" && (option.E1 == "true" || option.E1 == "false"):
			padding = option.E1 == "true"
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "Base takes case: :upper, :lower or :mixed and padding: true or false")
		}
	}
	return letters, padding
}

// The Base functions also take padding: false as a boolean, as written in
// Elixir.
func atomOptions(options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]]) L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]] {
	var out []pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		out = append(out, pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]{option.E0, actor.Atom(strconv.FormatBool(option.E1))})
	}
	return mk(out)
}

func baseEncode32WithBool(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) string {
	return baseEncode32With(ctx, s, atomOptions(options), where)
}

func baseDecode32WithBool(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) Toption[string] {
	return baseDecode32With(ctx, s, atomOptions(options), where)
}

func baseHexEncode32WithBool(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) string {
	return baseHexEncode32With(ctx, s, atomOptions(options), where)
}

func baseHexDecode32WithBool(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) Toption[string] {
	return baseHexDecode32With(ctx, s, atomOptions(options), where)
}

func baseEncode64WithBool(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) string {
	return baseEncode64With(ctx, s, atomOptions(options), where)
}

func baseDecode64WithBool(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) Toption[string] {
	return baseDecode64With(ctx, s, atomOptions(options), where)
}

func baseURLEncode64WithBool(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) string {
	return baseURLEncode64With(ctx, s, atomOptions(options), where)
}

func lettersIn(text, letters string) string {
	if letters == "lower" {
		return strings.ToLower(text)
	}
	return text
}

func baseEncode16With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) string {
	letters, _ := baseOptions(ctx, options, where)
	return lettersIn(strings.ToUpper(hex.EncodeToString([]byte(s))), letters)
}

func baseDecode16With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Toption[string] {
	letters, _ := baseOptions(ctx, options, where)
	if letters == "upper" && strings.ToUpper(s) != s || letters == "lower" && strings.ToLower(s) != s {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	return baseDecode16(s)
}

// base32Encoding is the standard alphabet, or the extended hex one, with or
// without padding.
func base32Encoding(hexadecimal, padding bool) *base32.Encoding {
	encoding := base32.StdEncoding
	if hexadecimal {
		encoding = base32.HexEncoding
	}
	if !padding {
		encoding = encoding.WithPadding(base32.NoPadding)
	}
	return encoding
}

func baseEncode32With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) string {
	letters, padding := baseOptions(ctx, options, where)
	return lettersIn(base32Encoding(false, padding).EncodeToString([]byte(s)), letters)
}

func baseDecode32With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Toption[string] {
	letters, padding := baseOptions(ctx, options, where)
	if letters != "upper" {
		s = strings.ToUpper(s)
	}
	text, err := base32Encoding(false, padding).DecodeString(s)
	return decoded(text, err)
}

func baseHexEncode32(s string) string { return base32.HexEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s)) }
func baseHexEncode32With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) string {
	letters, padding := baseOptions(ctx, options, where)
	return lettersIn(base32Encoding(true, padding).EncodeToString([]byte(s)), letters)
}
func baseHexDecode32(s string) Toption[string] {
	text, err := base32.HexEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return decoded(text, err)
}
func baseHexDecode32With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Toption[string] {
	letters, padding := baseOptions(ctx, options, where)
	if letters != "upper" {
		s = strings.ToUpper(s)
	}
	text, err := base32Encoding(true, padding).DecodeString(s)
	return decoded(text, err)
}

func baseEncode64With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) string {
	if _, padding := baseOptions(ctx, options, where); !padding {
		return base64.RawStdEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s))
	}
	return baseEncode64(s)
}

func baseDecode64With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Toption[string] {
	if _, padding := baseOptions(ctx, options, where); !padding {
		text, err := base64.RawStdEncoding.DecodeString(s)
		return decoded(text, err)
	}
	return baseDecode64(s)
}

func baseURLEncode64With(ctx *actor.Context, s string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) string {
	if _, padding := baseOptions(ctx, options, where); !padding {
		return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s))
	}
	return base64.URLEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s))
}

// pathType tells an absolute path from a relative one; on Windows a path
// such as C:file, or \\file, is relative to a volume.
func pathType(p string) actor.Atom {
	switch {
	case filepath.IsAbs(p):
		return "absolute"
	case filepath.VolumeName(p) != "" || runtime.GOOS == "windows" && (strings.HasPrefix(p, "\\") || strings.HasPrefix(p, "/")):
		return "volumerelative"
	}
	return "relative"
}

// pathSafeRelative gives the relative path cleaned of . and .. when it
// stays within the directory it is relative to.
func pathSafeRelative(p string) Toption[string] {
	if pathType(p) != "relative" {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	clean := filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Clean(p))
	if clean == ".." || strings.HasPrefix(clean, "../") {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	if clean == "." {
		clean = ""
	}
	return someOf(clean)
}

func pathSafeRelativeTo(p, cwd string) Toption[string] {
	if pathType(p) == "absolute" {
		rel, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, p)
		if err != nil {
			return noneOf[string]()
		}
		p = rel
	}
	return pathSafeRelative(p)
}

func floatRatio(ctx *actor.Context, f float64, where string) pair[int64, int64] {
	if math.IsInf(f, 0) || math.IsNaN(f) {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Float.ratio needs a finite float")
	}
	r := new(big.Rat).SetFloat64(f)
	if !r.Num().IsInt64() || !r.Denom().IsInt64() {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Float.ratio: the fraction does not fit in 64-bit integers")
	}
	return pair[int64, int64]{r.Num().Int64(), r.Denom().Int64()}
}

func floatMaxFinite() float64 { return math.MaxFloat64 }
func floatMinFinite() float64 { return -math.MaxFloat64 }

// binaryPart is the bytes from start, of the length; a negative length
// counts back from start.
func binaryPart(ctx *actor.Context, s string, start, length int64, where string) string {
	if length < 0 {
		start, length = start+length, -length
	}
	if start < 0 || start+length > int64(len(s)) {
		failAt(ctx, where, "binary_part: the part is outside the string")
	}
	return s[start : start+length]
}

func baseEncode32(s string) string { return base32.StdEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s)) }
func baseDecode32(s string) Toption[string] {
	text, err := base32.StdEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return decoded(text, err)
}
func baseDecode32Bang(ctx *actor.Context, s string, where string) string {
	text, err := base32.StdEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return decodedBang(ctx, text, err, where)
}
func baseEncode64(s string) string { return base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s)) }
func baseDecode64(s string) Toption[string] {
	text, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return decoded(text, err)
}
func baseDecode64Bang(ctx *actor.Context, s string, where string) string {
	text, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return decodedBang(ctx, text, err, where)
}
func baseUrlEncode64(s string) string { return base64.URLEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s)) }
func baseUrlDecode64(s string) Toption[string] {
	text, err := base64.URLEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return decoded(text, err)
}
func baseUrlDecode64Bang(ctx *actor.Context, s string, where string) string {
	text, err := base64.URLEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return decodedBang(ctx, text, err, where)
}

// ---- Path ----

func pathJoin(a, b string) string { return filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Join(a, b)) }

func pathJoinAll(ctx *actor.Context, parts iter.Seq[string], where string) string {
	all := collect(parts)
	if len(all) == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "cannot join an empty list of paths")
	}
	return filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Join(all...))
}

func pathBasename(p string) string {
	if p == "" {
		return ""
	}
	return filepath.Base(p)
}

func pathBasenameWithout(p, extension string) string {
	return strings.TrimSuffix(pathBasename(p), extension)
}

func pathDirname(p string) string         { return filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Dir(p)) }
func pathExtname(p string) string         { return filepath.Ext(p) }
func pathRootname(p string) string        { return strings.TrimSuffix(p, filepath.Ext(p)) }
func pathRootnameWithout(p, e string) string { return strings.TrimSuffix(p, e) }

func pathExpand(p string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(p, "~") {
		if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {
			p = home + p[1:]
		}
	}
	if absolute, err := filepath.Abs(p); err == nil {
		p = absolute
	}
	return filepath.ToSlash(p)
}

func pathSplit(p string) L[string] {
	var out []string
	clean := filepath.ToSlash(p)
	if strings.HasPrefix(clean, "/") {
		out = append(out, "/")
	}
	for _, part := range strings.Split(clean, "/") {
		if part != "" {
			out = append(out, part)
		}
	}
	return mk(out)
}

func pathRelativeTo(p, from string) string {
	relative, err := filepath.Rel(from, p)
	if err != nil || strings.HasPrefix(relative, "..") {
		return p
	}
	return filepath.ToSlash(relative)
}

func pathWildcard(ctx *actor.Context, pattern string, where string) L[string] {
	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
	if err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, "malformed wildcard pattern")
	}
	sort.Strings(matches)
	for i := range matches {
		matches[i] = filepath.ToSlash(matches[i])
	}
	return mk(matches)
}

// ---- File, System and IO ----

func must(ctx *actor.Context, outcome Tresult[struct{}, string], where string) {
	if failure, ok := outcome.(Tresult_c1[struct{}, string]); ok {
		failAt(ctx, where, failure.F0)
	}
}

func fileReadBang(ctx *actor.Context, path string, where string) string {
	switch outcome := fileRead(path).(type) {
	case Tresult_c0[string, string]:
		return outcome.F0
	case Tresult_c1[string, string]:
		failAt(ctx, where, "could not read "+path+": "+outcome.F0)
	}
	return ""
}

func fileWriteBang(ctx *actor.Context, path, content string, where string) {
	must(ctx, fileWrite(path, content), where)
}

func fileRemoveBang(ctx *actor.Context, path string, where string) {
	must(ctx, fileRemove(path), where)
}

func fileIsDir(path string) bool {
	info, err := os.Stat(path)
	return err == nil && info.IsDir()
}

func fileIsRegular(path string) bool {
	info, err := os.Stat(path)
	return err == nil && info.Mode().IsRegular()
}

func fileRemoveAll(path string) Tresult[struct{}, string] { return done(os.RemoveAll(path)) }
func fileMkdir(path string) Tresult[struct{}, string]     { return done(os.Mkdir(path, 0755)) }
func fileMkdirAll(path string) Tresult[struct{}, string]  { return done(os.MkdirAll(path, 0755)) }
func fileRename(from, to string) Tresult[struct{}, string] { return done(os.Rename(from, to)) }

func fileListBang(ctx *actor.Context, path string, where string) L[string] {
	switch outcome := fileList(path).(type) {
	case Tresult_c0[L[string], string]:
		return outcome.F0
	case Tresult_c1[L[string], string]:
		failAt(ctx, where, "could not list "+path+": "+outcome.F0)
	}
	return L[string]{}
}

func fileCopy(from, to string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	source, err := os.Open(from)
	if err != nil {
		return done(err)
	}
	defer source.Close()
	target, err := os.Create(to)
	if err != nil {
		return done(err)
	}
	_, err = io.Copy(target, source)
	if closing := target.Close(); err == nil {
		err = closing
	}
	return done(err)
}

func fileTouch(path string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	now := time.Now()
	if err := os.Chtimes(path, now, now); err == nil {
		return done(nil)
	}
	file, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0644)
	if err != nil {
		return done(err)
	}
	return done(file.Close())
}

func fileCwd() Tresult[string, string] {
	dir, err := os.Getwd()
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[string](err.Error())
	}
	return okOf(filepath.ToSlash(dir))
}

func fileCwdBang(ctx *actor.Context, where string) string {
	dir, err := os.Getwd()
	if err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, "could not read the working directory: "+err.Error())
	}
	return filepath.ToSlash(dir)
}

func systemEnvOr(name, fallback string) string {
	if value, ok := os.LookupEnv(name); ok {
		return value
	}
	return fallback
}

func systemPutEnv(ctx *actor.Context, name, value string, where string) {
	if err := os.Setenv(name, value); err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, "could not set "+name+": "+err.Error())
	}
}

func systemUserHome() Toption[string] {
	if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {
		return someOf(filepath.ToSlash(home))
	}
	return noneOf[string]()
}

func systemTmpDir() Toption[string] {
	if dir := os.TempDir(); dir != "" {
		return someOf(filepath.ToSlash(dir))
	}
	return noneOf[string]()
}

func systemTmpDirBang(ctx *actor.Context, where string) string {
	dir := os.TempDir()
	if dir == "" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "no directory for temporary files")
	}
	return filepath.ToSlash(dir)
}

func systemFindExecutable(name string) Toption[string] {
	if path, err := exec.LookPath(name); err == nil {
		return someOf(filepath.ToSlash(path))
	}
	return noneOf[string]()
}

// systemCmd runs a program and returns its combined output with its exit
// status. Failing to start it at all fails the calling process.
// systemCmd runs a program and returns what it wrote to standard output,
// with its exit status; what it writes to standard error goes to the
// program's own.
func systemCmd(ctx *actor.Context, command string, arguments iter.Seq[string], where string) pair[string, int64] {
	return systemCmdWith(ctx, command, arguments, L[pair[actor.Atom, string]]{}, where)
}

// systemCmdWith takes options: cd: a directory, env: "NAME=value" (given as
// often as needed), and stderr_to_stdout: "true".
func systemCmdWith(ctx *actor.Context, command string, arguments iter.Seq[string], options L[pair[actor.Atom, string]], where string) pair[string, int64] {
	cmd := exec.Command(command, collect(arguments)...)
	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		switch option.E0 {
		case "cd":
			cmd.Dir = option.E1
		case "env":
			if !strings.Contains(option.E1, "=") {
				failAt(ctx, where, "System.cmd: env: is \"NAME=value\"")
			}
			if cmd.Env == nil {
				cmd.Env = os.Environ()
			}
			cmd.Env = append(cmd.Env, option.E1)
		case "stderr_to_stdout":
			if option.E1 == "true" {
				cmd.Stderr = nil
			}
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "System.cmd: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; the options are cd:, env: and stderr_to_stdout:")
		}
	}
	var output []byte
	var err error
	if cmd.Stderr == nil {
		output, err = cmd.CombinedOutput()
	} else {
		output, err = cmd.Output()
	}
	if err != nil {
		if exit, ok := err.(*exec.ExitError); ok {
			return pair[string, int64]{string(output), int64(exit.ExitCode())}
		}
		failAt(ctx, where, "could not run "+command+": "+err.Error())
	}
	return pair[string, int64]{string(output), 0}
}

// timeUnit converts nanoseconds to :second, :millisecond, :microsecond,
// :nanosecond or :native, which is nanoseconds.
func timeUnit(ctx *actor.Context, ns int64, unit actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	switch unit {
	case "second":
		return ns / 1e9
	case "millisecond":
		return ns / 1e6
	case "microsecond":
		return ns / 1e3
	case "nanosecond", "native":
		return ns
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "the time unit is :second, :millisecond, :microsecond, :nanosecond or :native, not :"+string(unit))
	return 0
}

var monotonicStart = time.Now()

func systemMonotonicTime() int64 { return int64(time.Since(monotonicStart)) }
func systemMonotonicTimeIn(ctx *actor.Context, unit actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	return timeUnit(ctx, systemMonotonicTime(), unit, where)
}
func systemSystemTime() int64 { return time.Now().UnixNano() }
func systemSystemTimeIn(ctx *actor.Context, unit actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	return timeUnit(ctx, systemSystemTime(), unit, where)
}

func systemConvertTimeUnit(ctx *actor.Context, n int64, from, to actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	per := map[actor.Atom]int64{"second": 1e9, "millisecond": 1e6, "microsecond": 1e3, "nanosecond": 1, "native": 1}
	a, okA := per[from]
	b, okB := per[to]
	if !okA || !okB {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the time units are :second, :millisecond, :microsecond, :nanosecond or :native")
	}
	if a >= b {
		return n * (a / b)
	}
	return n / (b / a)
}

// systemStop ends the program with the status once its processes have been
// asked to stop and have ended, as the end of main does.
func systemStop(ctx *actor.Context, status int64, where string) {
	if status < 0 || status > 255 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "exit status must be between 0 and 255")
	}
	actor.StopProgram(ctx, status)
}

// systemHalt ends the program at once with the given exit status.
func systemHalt(ctx *actor.Context, status int64, where string) {
	if status < 0 || status > 255 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "exit status must be between 0 and 255")
	}
	os.Exit(int(status))
}

func systemPid() string { return strconv.Itoa(os.Getpid()) }

func ioWarn(text string) { os.Stderr.WriteString(text + "\n") }

// ioRead reads from standard input: :line, :eof (all of it), or a count of
// characters; none at the end of the input.
func ioRead(ctx *actor.Context, how actor.Atom, where string) Toption[string] {
	switch how {
	case "line":
		// The line keeps its newline, as Elixir's does.
		stdin.Lock()
		line, err := stdin.reader.ReadString('\n')
		stdin.Unlock()
		if line == "" && err != nil {
			return noneOf[string]()
		}
		return someOf(line)
	case "eof", "all":
		text := ioReadAll(ctx, where)
		if text == "" {
			return noneOf[string]()
		}
		return someOf(text)
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "IO.read takes :line, :eof or a number of characters")
	return noneOf[string]()
}

func ioReadDevice(ctx *actor.Context, device, how actor.Atom, where string) Toption[string] {
	if device != "stdio" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "IO.read reads :stdio, or a file opened with File.open")
	}
	return ioRead(ctx, how, where)
}

func ioReadCount(ctx *actor.Context, count int64, where string) Toption[string] {
	if count < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "IO.read needs a nonnegative count")
	}
	var out []rune
	stdin.Lock()
	for int64(len(out)) < count {
		r, _, err := stdin.reader.ReadRune()
		if err != nil {
			break
		}
		out = append(out, r)
	}
	stdin.Unlock()
	if len(out) == 0 && count > 0 {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	return someOf(string(out))
}

func ioBinread(ctx *actor.Context, count int64, where string) Toption[string] {
	if count < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "IO.binread needs a nonnegative count")
	}
	buffer := make([]byte, count)
	stdin.Lock()
	n, _ := io.ReadFull(stdin.reader, buffer)
	stdin.Unlock()
	if n == 0 && count > 0 {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	return someOf(string(buffer[:n]))
}

// The escape sequences of IO.ANSI, by name.
var ansiCodes = map[string]string{
	"reset": "0", "bright": "1", "faint": "2", "italic": "3", "underline": "4", "blink_slow": "5", "blink_rapid": "6",
	"reverse": "7", "conceal": "8", "crossed_out": "9", "normal": "22", "no_underline": "24", "not_italic": "23",
	"black": "30", "red": "31", "green": "32", "yellow": "33", "blue": "34", "magenta": "35", "cyan": "36", "white": "37", "default_color": "39",
	"black_background": "40", "red_background": "41", "green_background": "42", "yellow_background": "43", "blue_background": "44",
	"magenta_background": "45", "cyan_background": "46", "white_background": "47", "default_background": "49",
	"light_black": "90", "light_red": "91", "light_green": "92", "light_yellow": "93", "light_blue": "94", "light_magenta": "95", "light_cyan": "96", "light_white": "97",
	"light_black_background": "100", "light_red_background": "101", "light_green_background": "102", "light_yellow_background": "103",
	"light_blue_background": "104", "light_magenta_background": "105", "light_cyan_background": "106", "light_white_background": "107",
}

func ansi(name string) string { return "\x1b[" + ansiCodes[name] + "m" }

// ansiCode is the function IO.ANSI.name stands for.
func ansiCode(name string) func() string { return func() string { return ansi(name) } }

func ansiClear() string     { return "\x1b[2J" }
func ansiClearLine() string { return "\x1b[2K" }
func ansiHome() string      { return "\x1b[H" }
func ansiCursor(line, column int64) string {
	return "\x1b[" + strconv.FormatInt(line, 10) + ";" + strconv.FormatInt(column, 10) + "H"
}
func ansiMove(direction string) func(int64) string {
	return func(n int64) string { return "\x1b[" + strconv.FormatInt(n, 10) + direction }
}
func ansiColor(n int64) string           { return "\x1b[38;5;" + strconv.FormatInt(n, 10) + "m" }
func ansiColorBackground(n int64) string { return "\x1b[48;5;" + strconv.FormatInt(n, 10) + "m" }

// ansiEnabled reports whether standard output is a terminal that shows
// escape sequences.
func ansiEnabled() bool {
	info, err := os.Stdout.Stat()
	return err == nil && info.Mode()&os.ModeCharDevice != 0 && os.Getenv("TERM") != "dumb" && os.Getenv("NO_COLOR") == ""
}

// ansiFormat joins the parts, putting the escape sequence of each part that
// names one, such as "red" or "reset", in its place; with enabled false it
// leaves them out. A reset is added at the end, as Elixir's format does.
func ansiFormat(parts L[pair[actor.Atom, string]], enabled bool) string {
	var out strings.Builder
	for _, part := range fwd(parts) {
		if part.E0 == "text" {
			out.WriteString(part.E1)
		} else if code, ok := ansiCodes[string(part.E0)]; ok && enabled {
			out.WriteString("\x1b[" + code + "m")
		}
	}
	if enabled {
		out.WriteString(ansi("reset"))
	}
	return out.String()
}

// ioPutsDevice writes a line to :stdio or :stderr.
func ioPutsDevice(ctx *actor.Context, device actor.Atom, text string, where string) {
	switch device {
	case "stdio":
		ioWrite(text + "\n")
	case "stderr":
		os.Stderr.WriteString(text + "\n")
	default:
		failAt(ctx, where, "IO.puts writes to :stdio or :stderr, not :"+string(device))
	}
}

func ioGets(ctx *actor.Context, prompt string, where string) Toption[string] {
	ioWrite(prompt)
	return ioReadLine(ctx, where)
}

// ---- Regex ----

func regexCompile(source string) Tresult[*regexp.Regexp, string] {
	compiled, err := regexp.Compile(source)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*regexp.Regexp](err.Error())
	}
	return okOf(compiled)
}

func regexCompileBang(ctx *actor.Context, source string, where string) *regexp.Regexp {
	compiled, err := regexp.Compile(source)
	if err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, err.Error())
	}
	return compiled
}

func regexMatch(pattern *regexp.Regexp, text string) bool { return pattern.MatchString(text) }
func strMatch(text string, pattern *regexp.Regexp) bool   { return pattern.MatchString(text) }
func regexSource(pattern *regexp.Regexp) string           { return pattern.String() }

// run returns the whole match followed by each capture group.
func regexRun(pattern *regexp.Regexp, text string) Toption[L[string]] {
	match := pattern.FindStringSubmatch(text)
	if match == nil {
		return noneOf[L[string]]()
	}
	return someOf(mk(match))
}

// scan returns every match: its capture groups, or the whole match when the
// pattern has none.
func regexScan(pattern *regexp.Regexp, text string) L[L[string]] {
	var out []L[string]
	for _, match := range pattern.FindAllStringSubmatch(text, -1) {
		if len(match) > 1 {
			match = match[1:]
		}
		out = append(out, mk(append([]string{}, match...)))
	}
	return mk(out)
}

var backReference = regexp.MustCompile(`\\(\d+)`)

// replace accepts \1 for a capture group, as well as Go's ${1}.
func regexReplace(pattern *regexp.Regexp, text, replacement string) string {
	return pattern.ReplaceAllString(text, backReference.ReplaceAllString(replacement, "${$1}"))
}

func regexSplit(pattern *regexp.Regexp, text string) L[string] { return mk(pattern.Split(text, -1)) }

func regexNamedCaptures(pattern *regexp.Regexp, text string) Toption[actor.Map[string, string]] {
	match := pattern.FindStringSubmatch(text)
	if match == nil {
		return noneOf[actor.Map[string, string]]()
	}
	var out actor.Map[string, string]
	for i, name := range pattern.SubexpNames() {
		if name != "" {
			out = actor.MapPut(out, name, match[i])
		}
	}
	return someOf(out)
}

// ---- Task and Agent ----

// The default deadline, in milliseconds, of calls that wait on a task or agent.
const patience = 5000

func taskAwait[A any](ctx *actor.Context, task *actor.Task[A], where string) A {
	return actor.AwaitTask(ctx, task, patience, where)
}

func taskAwaitManyWithin[A any](ctx *actor.Context, tasks iter.Seq[*actor.Task[A]], milliseconds int64, where string) L[A] {
	started := time.Now()
	var out []A
	tasks(func(task *actor.Task[A]) bool {
		left := milliseconds
		if milliseconds > 0 {
			left = max(milliseconds-time.Since(started).Milliseconds(), 0)
		}
		out = append(out, actor.AwaitTask(ctx, task, left, where))
		return true
	})
	return mk(out)
}

func taskAwaitMany[A any](ctx *actor.Context, tasks iter.Seq[*actor.Task[A]], where string) L[A] {
	return taskAwaitManyWithin(ctx, tasks, patience, where)
}

func taskSupervisedAsync[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s *actor.DynamicSupervisor, f func(*actor.Context) A, where string) *actor.Task[A] {
	task, problem := actor.StartSupervisedTask(ctx, s, f, true, where)
	if problem != "" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Task.Supervisor.async: "+problem)
	}
	return task
}

func taskSupervisedAsyncNolink[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s *actor.DynamicSupervisor, f func(*actor.Context) A, where string) *actor.Task[A] {
	task, problem := actor.StartSupervisedTask(ctx, s, f, false, where)
	if problem != "" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Task.Supervisor.async_nolink: "+problem)
	}
	return task
}

func taskSupervisedStart[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s *actor.DynamicSupervisor, f func(*actor.Context) A, where string) Tresult[*actor.Task[A], string] {
	task, problem := actor.StartSupervisedTask(ctx, s, f, false, where)
	if problem != "" {
		return errOf[*actor.Task[A]](problem)
	}
	return okOf(task)
}

func taskSupervisedTerminate[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s *actor.DynamicSupervisor, task *actor.Task[A], where string) bool {
	return actor.TerminateDynamicChild(ctx, s, actor.TaskID(task), where)
}

func taskYield[A any](ctx *actor.Context, task *actor.Task[A], milliseconds int64, where string) Toption[Tresult[A, string]] {
	outcome := actor.YieldTask(ctx, task, milliseconds, where)
	switch {
	case outcome.Running:
		return noneOf[Tresult[A, string]]()
	case outcome.Reason != "":
		return someOf(errOf[A](outcome.Reason))
	}
	return someOf(okOf(outcome.Value))
}

// async_stream runs f on each element in its own task, at most limit at a
// time, and yields the results in the order of the input.
func taskAsyncStreamLimit[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, limit int64, where string) iter.Seq[B] {
	if limit < 1 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "async_stream needs a concurrency of at least 1")
	}
	return func(yield func(B) bool) {
		var running []*actor.Task[B]
		stop := func() {
			for _, task := range running {
				actor.StopTask(ctx, task, where)
			}
		}
		next, done := iter.Pull(s)
		defer done()
		for {
			for int64(len(running)) < limit {
				x, ok := next()
				if !ok {
					break
				}
				running = append(running, actor.StartTask(ctx, func(child *actor.Context) B { return f(child, x) }, where))
			}
			if len(running) == 0 {
				return
			}
			value := actor.AwaitTask(ctx, running[0], -1, where)
			running = running[1:]
			if !yield(value) {
				stop()
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func taskAsyncStream[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B, where string) iter.Seq[B] {
	return taskAsyncStreamLimit(ctx, s, f, int64(runtime.NumCPU()), where)
}

func agentGet[S, R any](ctx *actor.Context, agent *actor.Agent[S], f func(*actor.Context, S) R, where string) R {
	return actor.AgentGet(ctx, agent, f, patience, where)
}

func agentStartNamed[S any](ctx *actor.Context, initial func(*actor.Context) S, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) *actor.Agent[S] {
	name := ""
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		if option.E0 != "name" {
			failAt(ctx, where, "unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; Agent.start_link takes name:")
		}
		name = string(option.E1)
	}
	agent := actor.StartAgent(ctx, initial, where)
	if name != "" && !actor.RegisterAgent(ctx, agent, name, where) {
		actor.StopAgent(ctx, agent, where)
		failAt(ctx, where, "the name :"+name+" is already taken")
	}
	return agent
}

func agentWhereis[S any](ctx *actor.Context, name actor.Atom) Toption[*actor.Agent[S]] {
	if found, ok := actor.AgentWhereis[S](ctx, string(name)); ok {
		return someOf(found)
	}
	return noneOf[*actor.Agent[S]]()
}

func agentGetNamed[S, R any](ctx *actor.Context, name actor.Atom, f func(*actor.Context, S) R, where string) R {
	return actor.AgentGet(ctx, actor.NamedAgent[S](ctx, string(name), where), f, patience, where)
}

func agentUpdateNamed[S any](ctx *actor.Context, name actor.Atom, f func(*actor.Context, S) S, where string) {
	actor.AgentUpdate(ctx, actor.NamedAgent[S](ctx, string(name), where), f, patience, where)
}

func agentGetAndUpdateNamed[S, R any](ctx *actor.Context, name actor.Atom, f func(*actor.Context, S) pair[R, S], where string) R {
	return actor.AgentGetAndUpdate(ctx, actor.NamedAgent[S](ctx, string(name), where), f, patience, where)
}

func agentCastNamed[S any](ctx *actor.Context, name actor.Atom, f func(*actor.Context, S) S, where string) bool {
	return actor.AgentCast(ctx, actor.NamedAgent[S](ctx, string(name), where), f, where)
}

func agentUpdate[S any](ctx *actor.Context, agent *actor.Agent[S], f func(*actor.Context, S) S, where string) {
	actor.AgentUpdate(ctx, agent, f, patience, where)
}

func agentGetAndUpdate[S, R any](ctx *actor.Context, agent *actor.Agent[S], f func(*actor.Context, S) pair[R, S], where string) R {
	return actor.AgentGetAndUpdate(ctx, agent, f, patience, where)
}

// ---- JSON ----

// jsonValue writes any value as JSON: structs as objects by field name, maps
// as objects with their keys as text, lists, sets and tuples as arrays,
// atoms as strings, none as null and some(x) as x, a variant without
// fields as its name and one with fields as {"name": [fields]}.
func jsonValue(x any) (string, error) {
	var b strings.Builder
	err := jsonOf(&b, reflect.ValueOf(x))
	return b.String(), err
}
func jsonOf(b *strings.Builder, v reflect.Value) error {
	if !v.IsValid() {
		b.WriteString("null")
		return nil
	}
	if j, ok := v.Interface().(TJSON); ok && v.Type().Name() != "" && strings.HasPrefix(v.Type().Name(), "TJSON") {
		jsonWrite(b, j)
		return nil
	}
	switch v.Kind() {
	case reflect.Int64:
		b.WriteString(strconv.FormatInt(v.Int(), 10))
		return nil
	case reflect.Float64:
		if math.IsInf(v.Float(), 0) || math.IsNaN(v.Float()) {
			return fmt.Errorf("%v cannot be written as JSON", v.Float())
		}
		b.WriteString(strconv.FormatFloat(v.Float(), 'g', -1, 64))
		return nil
	case reflect.String:
		q, _ := json.Marshal(v.String())
		b.Write(q)
		return nil
	case reflect.Bool:
		b.WriteString(strconv.FormatBool(v.Bool()))
		return nil
	case reflect.Interface:
		if v.IsNil() {
			b.WriteString("null")
			return nil
		}
		return jsonOf(b, v.Elem())
	case reflect.Func, reflect.Pointer, reflect.Chan:
		return fmt.Errorf("%s cannot be written as JSON", inspectOf(v))
	case reflect.Struct:
		name, _, _ := strings.Cut(v.Type().Name(), "[")
		fields := make([]reflect.Value, v.NumField())
		for i := range fields {
			fields[i] = v.Field(i)
		}
		array := func(items []reflect.Value) error {
			b.WriteByte('[')
			for i, item := range items {
				if i > 0 {
					b.WriteByte(',')
				}
				if err := jsonOf(b, item); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
			b.WriteByte(']')
			return nil
		}
		if pairs, ok := v.Interface().(interface{ Pairs() [][2]any }); ok {
			entries := pairs.Pairs()
			sort.Slice(entries, func(i, j int) bool { return inspectLess(entries[i][0], entries[j][0]) })
			if strings.HasSuffix(v.Type().Name(), ",struct {}]") {
				items := make([]reflect.Value, len(entries))
				for i, e := range entries {
					items[i] = reflect.ValueOf(e[0])
				}
				return array(items)
			}
			b.WriteByte('{')
			for i, entry := range entries {
				if i > 0 {
					b.WriteByte(',')
				}
				key := inspectValue(entry[0])
				if atom, isAtom := entry[0].(actor.Atom); isAtom {
					key = string(atom)
				}
				if s, ok := entry[0].(string); ok {
					key = s
				}
				q, _ := json.Marshal(key)
				b.Write(q)
				b.WriteByte(':')
				if err := jsonOf(b, reflect.ValueOf(entry[1])); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
			b.WriteByte('}')
			return nil
		}
		if name == "L" {
			items := make([]reflect.Value, fields[0].Len())
			for i := range items {
				items[i] = fields[0].Index(len(items) - 1 - i)
			}
			return array(items)
		}
		info, known := typeNames[name]
		if !known {
			return array(fields)
		}
		if info.record {
			b.WriteByte('{')
			for i, field := range fields {
				if i > 0 {
					b.WriteByte(',')
				}
				q, _ := json.Marshal(info.fields[i])
				b.Write(q)
				b.WriteByte(':')
				if err := jsonOf(b, field); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
			b.WriteByte('}')
			return nil
		}
		switch {
		case info.name == "none":
			b.WriteString("null")
			return nil
		case info.name == "some" && len(fields) == 1:
			return jsonOf(b, fields[0])
		}
		variant := info.name[strings.LastIndex(info.name, ".")+1:]
		if len(fields) == 0 {
			q, _ := json.Marshal(variant)
			b.Write(q)
			return nil
		}
		q, _ := json.Marshal(variant)
		b.WriteByte('{')
		b.Write(q)
		b.WriteByte(':')
		if err := array(fields); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		b.WriteByte('}')
		return nil
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("%s cannot be written as JSON", inspectOf(v))
}

// jsonAny writes any value as JSON, failing for one that has no JSON form,
// such as a function.
func jsonAny[A any](ctx *actor.Context, value A, where string) string {
	text, err := jsonValue(value)
	if err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, err.Error())
	}
	return text
}

func jsonAnyResult[A any](value A) Tresult[string, string] {
	text, err := jsonValue(value)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[string](err.Error())
	}
	return okOf(text)
}

// jsonAnyWith takes pretty: true to indent the result by two spaces.
func jsonAnyWith[A any](ctx *actor.Context, value A, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) string {
	text := jsonAny(ctx, value, where)
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		if option.E0 != "pretty" {
			failAt(ctx, where, "JSON.encode! takes pretty:")
		}
		if option.E1 {
			var out bytes.Buffer
			json.Indent(&out, []byte(text), "", "  ")
			text = out.String()
		}
	}
	return text
}

func jsonEncode(value TJSON) string {
	var out strings.Builder
	jsonWrite(&out, value)
	return out.String()
}

// jsonQuote writes a string as JSON. Text made only of printable ASCII that
// needs no escaping, which is most keys and many values, is quoted directly;
// anything else takes the standard encoder, so the escaping is identical.
func jsonQuote(out *strings.Builder, text string) {
	for i := 0; i < len(text); i++ {
		if c := text[i]; c < 0x20 || c >= 0x7f || c == '"' || c == '\\' || c == '<' || c == '>' || c == '&' {
			quoted, _ := json.Marshal(text)
			out.Write(quoted)
			return
		}
	}
	out.WriteByte('"')
	out.WriteString(text)
	out.WriteByte('"')
}

func jsonWrite(out *strings.Builder, value TJSON) {
	switch v := value.(type) {
	case TJSON_c1:
		out.WriteString(strconv.FormatBool(v.F0))
	case TJSON_c2:
		out.WriteString(strconv.FormatInt(v.F0, 10))
	case TJSON_c3:
		out.WriteString(strconv.FormatFloat(v.F0, 'g', -1, 64))
	case TJSON_c4:
		jsonQuote(out, v.F0)
	case TJSON_c5:
		out.WriteByte('[')
		for i, item := range fwd(v.F0) {
			if i > 0 {
				out.WriteByte(',')
			}
			jsonWrite(out, item)
		}
		out.WriteByte(']')
	case TJSON_c6:
		out.WriteByte('{')
		keys, values := actor.MapSorted(v.F0, nil)
		for i, key := range keys {
			if i > 0 {
				out.WriteByte(',')
			}
			jsonQuote(out, key)
			out.WriteByte(':')
			jsonWrite(out, values[i])
		}
		out.WriteByte('}')
	default:
		out.WriteString("null")
	}
}

func jsonRead(value any) TJSON {
	switch v := value.(type) {
	case bool:
		return TJSON(TJSON_c1{v})
	case json.Number:
		if whole, err := strconv.ParseInt(v.String(), 10, 64); err == nil {
			return TJSON(TJSON_c2{whole})
		}
		number, _ := v.Float64()
		return TJSON(TJSON_c3{number})
	case string:
		return TJSON(TJSON_c4{v})
	case []any:
		items := make([]TJSON, len(v))
		for i, item := range v {
			items[i] = jsonRead(item)
		}
		return TJSON(TJSON_c5{mk(items)})
	case map[string]any:
		fields := actor.NewMapBuilder(actor.Map[string, TJSON]{})
		for key, item := range v {
			fields.Put(key, jsonRead(item))
		}
		return TJSON(TJSON_c6{fields.Map()})
	}
	return TJSON(TJSON_c0{})
}

func jsonParse(text string) (TJSON, error) {
	decoder := json.NewDecoder(strings.NewReader(text))
	decoder.UseNumber()
	var value any
	if err := decoder.Decode(&value); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if decoder.More() {
		return nil, errors.New("unexpected text after the JSON value")
	}
	return jsonRead(value), nil
}

func jsonDecode(text string) Tresult[TJSON, string] {
	value, err := jsonParse(text)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[TJSON](err.Error())
	}
	return okOf(value)
}

func jsonDecodeBang(ctx *actor.Context, text string, where string) TJSON {
	value, err := jsonParse(text)
	if err != nil {
		failAt(ctx, where, "invalid JSON: "+err.Error())
	}
	return value
}

// ---- Testing ----

// A test file's entry point runs its tests through runTests. The command
// that started it passes options in the environment and reads the counts
// back from a file, so that it can total several test files.

type testCase struct {
	name, where string
	tags        []string
	run         func(*actor.Context)
}

// tagged reports whether a test carries any of the comma-separated tags.
func tagged(test testCase, list string) bool {
	for _, wanted := range strings.Split(list, ",") {
		for _, tag := range test.tags {
			// A name given alone matches the tag with any value.
			if wanted != "" && (tag == wanted || !strings.Contains(wanted, ":") && strings.HasPrefix(tag, wanted+":")) {
				return true
			}
		}
	}
	return false
}

// tagValue is the value of a test's tag, the last one given.
func tagValue(test testCase, name string) (string, bool) {
	value, found := "", false
	for _, tag := range test.tags {
		if key, given, valued := strings.Cut(tag, ":"); key == name && valued {
			value, found = given, true
		}
	}
	return value, found
}

type coverPoint struct {
	file string
	line int
}

var testExit int

func cover(point int) { coverCounts[point].Add(1) }

// writeCoverage records how often each counted statement ran.
func writeCoverage() {
	path := os.Getenv("ALCHEM_COVER_FILE")
	if path == "" {
		return
	}
	var out strings.Builder
	for i, point := range coverPoints {
		out.WriteString(point.file + "\t" + strconv.Itoa(point.line) + "\t" + strconv.FormatUint(uint64(coverCounts[i].Load()), 10) + "\n")
	}
	os.WriteFile(path, []byte(out.String()), 0600)
}

func testSetting(name string, fallback int64) int64 {
	if value, err := strconv.ParseInt(os.Getenv(name), 10, 64); err == nil {
		return value
	}
	return fallback
}

// runTests runs each test in its own process, so one failure cannot disturb
// the next, in an order shuffled by the seed. It returns the exit status.
func runTests(ctx *actor.Context, module string, tests []testCase) int {
	seed, _ := strconv.ParseUint(os.Getenv("ALCHEM_SEED"), 10, 64)
	only := os.Getenv("ALCHEM_TEST_ONLY")
	trace := os.Getenv("ALCHEM_TEST_TRACE") != ""
	limit := testSetting("ALCHEM_TEST_MAX_FAILURES", 0)
	timeout := testSetting("ALCHEM_TEST_TIMEOUT", 60000)
	var selected []testCase
	// --tag runs only the tests carrying one of the tags; --exclude leaves
	// out those carrying one. A test tagged "skip" runs only when asked for.
	include, exclude := os.Getenv("ALCHEM_TEST_TAGS"), os.Getenv("ALCHEM_TEST_EXCLUDE")
	for _, test := range tests {
		named := only == "" || strings.Contains(test.name, only) || strings.Contains(module, only)
		wanted := include == "" || tagged(test, include)
		unwanted := tagged(test, exclude) || (tagged(test, "skip") && !tagged(test, include))
		if named && wanted && !unwanted {
			selected = append(selected, test)
		}
	}
	excluded := len(tests) - len(selected)
	if seed != 0 {
		order := rand.New(rand.NewPCG(seed, seed^0x9e3779b97f4a7c15))
		order.Shuffle(len(selected), func(i, j int) { selected[i], selected[j] = selected[j], selected[i] })
	}
	if trace {
		fmt.Printf("\n%s\n", module)
	}
	// Positions are shown relative to where the tests were started from.
	here, _ := os.Getwd()
	local := func(text string) string {
		return strings.ReplaceAll(strings.ReplaceAll(text, here+string(os.PathSeparator), ""), filepath.ToSlash(here)+"/", "")
	}
	var failures []string
	passed, skipped := 0, 0
	for i, test := range selected {
		if limit > 0 && int64(len(failures)) >= limit {
			skipped = len(selected) - i
			break
		}
		started := time.Now()
		var running *actor.Context
		// @tag timeout: ms gives one test its own time limit, and
		// timeout: :infinity none.
		allowed := timeout
		if value, ok := tagValue(test, "timeout"); ok {
			if value == "infinity" {
				allowed = -1
			} else if ms, err := strconv.ParseInt(value, 10, 64); err == nil && ms > 0 {
				allowed = ms
			}
		}
		task := actor.StartUnlinkedTask(ctx, func(child *actor.Context) struct{} { running = child; test.run(child); return struct{}{} }, test.where)
		outcome := actor.YieldTask(ctx, task, allowed, test.where)
		problem := ""
		switch {
		case outcome.Running:
			actor.StopTask(ctx, task, test.where)
			problem = "timed out after " + strconv.FormatInt(allowed, 10) + "ms"
		case outcome.Reason != "":
			problem = actor.TaskReport(ctx, task, test.where)
		}
		// Whatever the test registered with on_exit runs now, last first,
		// in a process of its own so that a failing one is reported.
		if hooks := takeExitHooks(running); len(hooks) > 0 {
			cleanup := actor.StartUnlinkedTask(ctx, func(child *actor.Context) struct{} {
				for i := len(hooks) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
					hooks[i](child)
				}
				return struct{}{}
			}, test.where)
			if done := actor.YieldTask(ctx, cleanup, timeout, test.where); problem == "" {
				switch {
				case done.Running:
					actor.StopTask(ctx, cleanup, test.where)
					problem = "on_exit timed out after " + strconv.FormatInt(timeout, 10) + "ms"
				case done.Reason != "":
					problem = "on_exit failed: " + actor.TaskReport(ctx, cleanup, test.where)
				}
			}
		}
		elapsed := float64(time.Since(started).Microseconds()) / 1000
		if problem == "" {
			passed++
			if trace {
				fmt.Printf("  * test %s (%.1fms)\n", test.name, elapsed)
			} else {
				fmt.Print(".")
			}
			continue
		}
		failures = append(failures, fmt.Sprintf("  %d) test %s (%s)\n     %s\n     %s\n", len(failures)+1, test.name, module, local(test.where), strings.ReplaceAll(strings.TrimRight(local(problem), "\n"), "\n", "\n     ")))
		if trace {
			fmt.Printf("  * test %s (%.1fms) [failed]\n", test.name, elapsed)
		} else {
			fmt.Print("F")
		}
	}
	if !trace && len(selected) > 0 {
		fmt.Println()
	}
	for _, failure := range failures {
		fmt.Print("\n" + failure)
	}
	if path := os.Getenv("ALCHEM_TEST_RESULT"); path != "" {
		os.WriteFile(path, []byte(fmt.Sprintf("%d %d %d %d\n", passed, len(failures), excluded, skipped)), 0600)
	}
	if len(failures) > 0 {
		return 1
	}
	return 0
}

// ---- Assertions ----

// The assertions also work in ordinary programs, which have none of the
// coverage counters a test build adds.

// assertRaise passes when the function fails with a reason containing the
// expected text.
func assertRaise[A any](ctx *actor.Context, expected string, f func(*actor.Context) A, where string) {
	task := actor.StartUnlinkedTask(ctx, f, where)
	outcome := actor.YieldTask(ctx, task, -1, where)
	switch {
	case outcome.Reason == "":
		failAt(ctx, where, "expected a failure containing "+strconv.Quote(expected)+", but the function returned")
	case !strings.Contains(outcome.Reason, expected):
		failAt(ctx, where, "expected a failure containing "+strconv.Quote(expected)+", got "+strconv.Quote(outcome.Reason))
	}
}

// exitHooks holds, per running test, the functions registered with on_exit.
var (
	exitHooksLock sync.Mutex
	exitHooks     = map[*actor.Context][]func(*actor.Context){}
)

// onExit registers a function to run when the current test ends, whether
// it passed or failed. Outside a test nothing ever runs it.
func onExit[A any](ctx *actor.Context, f func(*actor.Context) A) {
	exitHooksLock.Lock()
	defer exitHooksLock.Unlock()
	exitHooks[ctx] = append(exitHooks[ctx], func(c *actor.Context) { f(c) })
}

func takeExitHooks(ctx *actor.Context) []func(*actor.Context) {
	exitHooksLock.Lock()
	defer exitHooksLock.Unlock()
	hooks := exitHooks[ctx]
	delete(exitHooks, ctx)
	return hooks
}

// captureIO runs the function and returns what it, and anything else in
// the program meanwhile, wrote to standard output.
func captureIO[A any](ctx *actor.Context, f func(*actor.Context) A) string {
	reader, writer, err := os.Pipe()
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	original := os.Stdout
	os.Stdout = writer
	captured := make(chan string)
	go func() {
		text, _ := io.ReadAll(reader)
		captured <- string(text)
	}()
	restore := func() string {
		os.Stdout = original
		writer.Close()
		text := <-captured
		reader.Close()
		return text
	}
	finished := false
	// A failure inside still restores standard output, and passes on what
	// was written before it.
	defer func() {
		if !finished {
			original.WriteString(restore())
		}
	}()
	f(ctx)
	finished = true
	return restore()
}

func refute(ctx *actor.Context, condition bool, where string) {
	if condition {
		failAt(ctx, where, "refute failed: the expression is true")
	}
}

func assertInDelta(ctx *actor.Context, a, b, delta float64, where string) {
	if math.Abs(a-b) > delta {
		failAt(ctx, where, "assertion failed\n     left:  "+floatString(a)+"\n     right: "+floatString(b)+"\n     delta: "+floatString(delta))
	}
}

func failNow(ctx *actor.Context, message string, where string) { failAt(ctx, where, message) }

// ---- Calendar ----

// Dates and times are plain structs; arithmetic goes through time.Time in UTC.

func calMoment(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, microsecond int64) time.Time {
	return time.Date(int(year), time.Month(month), int(day), int(hour), int(minute), int(second), int(microsecond)*1000, time.UTC)
}

// calValid reports whether the fields name a real date and time of day.
func calValid(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, microsecond int64) bool {
	if year < -9999 || year > 9999 || month < 1 || month > 12 || day < 1 || day > 31 || hour < 0 || hour > 23 || minute < 0 || minute > 59 || second < 0 || second > 59 || microsecond < 0 || microsecond > 999999 {
		return false
	}
	return calMoment(year, month, day, 0, 0, 0, 0).Day() == int(day)
}

func calDateOf(t time.Time) TDate { return TDate{int64(t.Year()), int64(t.Month()), int64(t.Day())} }
func calTimeOf(t time.Time) TTime {
	return TTime{int64(t.Hour()), int64(t.Minute()), int64(t.Second()), int64(t.Nanosecond() / 1000)}
}
func calNaiveOf(t time.Time) TNaiveDateTime {
	return TNaiveDateTime{int64(t.Year()), int64(t.Month()), int64(t.Day()), int64(t.Hour()), int64(t.Minute()), int64(t.Second()), int64(t.Nanosecond() / 1000)}
}
func calDateTimeOf(t time.Time) TDateTime {
	t = t.UTC()
	return TDateTime{int64(t.Year()), int64(t.Month()), int64(t.Day()), int64(t.Hour()), int64(t.Minute()), int64(t.Second()), int64(t.Nanosecond() / 1000), "Etc/UTC", 0}
}

// calZone finds a time zone by its name in the zone database.
func calZone(name string) (*time.Location, bool) {
	if name == "Etc/UTC" || name == "UTC" {
		return time.UTC, true
	}
	if name == "" || name == "Local" {
		return nil, false
	}
	if calLoadZone == nil {
		return nil, false
	}
	return calLoadZone(name)
}

// calLoadZone reads the zone database. It is set only in programs that put
// a DateTime in a zone, so that others do not carry the database.
var calLoadZone func(name string) (*time.Location, bool)

// calIn is a moment as the wall clock of a zone shows it, with the zone's
// name and its offset from UTC at that moment.
func calIn(t time.Time, name string) (TDateTime, bool) {
	zone, ok := calZone(name)
	if !ok {
		return TDateTime{}, false
	}
	if zone == time.UTC {
		return calDateTimeOf(t), true
	}
	t = t.In(zone)
	_, offset := t.Zone()
	return TDateTime{int64(t.Year()), int64(t.Month()), int64(t.Day()), int64(t.Hour()), int64(t.Minute()), int64(t.Second()), int64(t.Nanosecond() / 1000), name, int64(offset)}, true
}

// calKeepZone expresses a moment in the zone another DateTime is in.
func calKeepZone(t time.Time, like TDateTime) TDateTime {
	if moved, ok := calIn(t, like.F7); ok {
		return moved
	}
	return calDateTimeOf(t)
}

func calFromDate(d TDate) time.Time { return calMoment(d.F0, d.F1, d.F2, 0, 0, 0, 0) }
func calFromNaiveTime(n TNaiveDateTime) time.Time {
	return calMoment(n.F0, n.F1, n.F2, n.F3, n.F4, n.F5, n.F6)
}
// calFromDateTime is the moment a DateTime names: its wall clock less its
// offset from UTC.
func calFromDateTime(d TDateTime) time.Time {
	return calMoment(d.F0, d.F1, d.F2, d.F3, d.F4, d.F5, d.F6).Add(-time.Duration(d.F8) * time.Second)
}

// calOffset writes an offset from UTC as +HH:MM, with an optional colon.
func calOffset(seconds int64, colon string) string {
	sign := "+"
	if seconds < 0 {
		sign, seconds = "-", -seconds
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s%02d%s%02d", sign, seconds/3600, colon, seconds%3600/60)
}

// calMicros is a time of day as microseconds since midnight.
func calMicros(t TTime) int64 { return ((t.F0*60+t.F1)*60+t.F2)*1000000 + t.F3 }

const calDay = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000000

func calFraction(microsecond int64) string {
	if microsecond == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	return "." + strings.TrimRight(fmt.Sprintf("%06d", microsecond), "0")
}

func calOrder(a, b time.Time) int64 { return int64(a.Compare(b)) }

func calSeconds(ctx *actor.Context, t time.Time, seconds int64, where string) time.Time {
	if seconds > 1<<40 || seconds < -(1<<40) {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the amount to add is out of range")
	}
	return t.Add(time.Duration(seconds) * time.Second)
}

func calDateIso(d TDate) string    { return fmt.Sprintf("%04d-%02d-%02d", d.F0, d.F1, d.F2) }
func calDateString(d TDate) string { return calDateIso(d) }
func calTimeIso(t TTime) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%02d:%02d:%02d", t.F0, t.F1, t.F2) + calFraction(t.F3)
}
func calTimeString(t TTime) string { return calTimeIso(t) }
func calNaiveDateTimeIso(n TNaiveDateTime) string {
	return calDateIso(TDate{n.F0, n.F1, n.F2}) + "T" + calTimeIso(TTime{n.F3, n.F4, n.F5, n.F6})
}
func calNaiveDateTimeString(n TNaiveDateTime) string {
	return calDateIso(TDate{n.F0, n.F1, n.F2}) + " " + calTimeIso(TTime{n.F3, n.F4, n.F5, n.F6})
}
func calDateTimeIso(d TDateTime) string {
	if d.F8 == 0 && d.F7 == "Etc/UTC" {
		return calNaiveDateTimeIso(calToNaive(d)) + "Z"
	}
	return calNaiveDateTimeIso(calToNaive(d)) + calOffset(d.F8, ":")
}

// A DateTime outside UTC is written with its offset, the zone's
// abbreviation at that moment and the zone's name.
func calDateTimeString(d TDateTime) string {
	if d.F8 == 0 && d.F7 == "Etc/UTC" {
		return calNaiveDateTimeString(calToNaive(d)) + "Z"
	}
	text := calNaiveDateTimeString(calToNaive(d)) + calOffset(d.F8, ":")
	if zone, ok := calZone(d.F7); ok {
		abbreviation, _ := calFromDateTime(d).In(zone).Zone()
		text += " " + abbreviation
	}
	return text + " " + d.F7
}

func calDateCompare(a, b TDate) int64 { return calOrder(calFromDate(a), calFromDate(b)) }
func calDateBefore(a, b TDate) bool   { return calDateCompare(a, b) < 0 }
func calDateAfter(a, b TDate) bool    { return calDateCompare(a, b) > 0 }
func calTimeCompare(a, b TTime) int64 {
	switch x, y := calMicros(a), calMicros(b); {
	case x < y:
		return -1
	case x > y:
		return 1
	}
	return 0
}
func calTimeBefore(a, b TTime) bool { return calTimeCompare(a, b) < 0 }
func calTimeAfter(a, b TTime) bool  { return calTimeCompare(a, b) > 0 }
func calNaiveDateTimeCompare(a, b TNaiveDateTime) int64 {
	return calOrder(calFromNaiveTime(a), calFromNaiveTime(b))
}
func calNaiveDateTimeBefore(a, b TNaiveDateTime) bool { return calNaiveDateTimeCompare(a, b) < 0 }
func calNaiveDateTimeAfter(a, b TNaiveDateTime) bool  { return calNaiveDateTimeCompare(a, b) > 0 }
func calDateTimeCompare(a, b TDateTime) int64 {
	return calOrder(calFromDateTime(a), calFromDateTime(b))
}
func calDateTimeBefore(a, b TDateTime) bool { return calDateTimeCompare(a, b) < 0 }
func calDateTimeAfter(a, b TDateTime) bool  { return calDateTimeCompare(a, b) > 0 }

// Dates add and differ in days; everything else in seconds. A time of day
// wraps around midnight.
func calDateAdd(ctx *actor.Context, d TDate, days int64, where string) TDate {
	if days > 10000000 || days < -10000000 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the number of days to add is out of range")
	}
	return calDateOf(calFromDate(d).AddDate(0, 0, int(days)))
}
func calDateDiff(a, b TDate) int64 {
	return int64(calFromDate(a).Sub(calFromDate(b)).Hours() / 24)
}
func calTimeAdd(ctx *actor.Context, t TTime, seconds int64, where string) TTime {
	micros := ((calMicros(t)+(seconds%86400)*1000000)%calDay + calDay) % calDay
	return TTime{micros / 3600000000, micros / 60000000 % 60, micros / 1000000 % 60, micros % 1000000}
}
func calTimeDiff(a, b TTime) int64 { return (calMicros(a) - calMicros(b)) / 1000000 }
func calNaiveDateTimeAdd(ctx *actor.Context, n TNaiveDateTime, seconds int64, where string) TNaiveDateTime {
	return calNaiveOf(calSeconds(ctx, calFromNaiveTime(n), seconds, where))
}
func calNaiveDateTimeDiff(a, b TNaiveDateTime) int64 {
	return int64(calFromNaiveTime(a).Sub(calFromNaiveTime(b)) / time.Second)
}
func calDateTimeAdd(ctx *actor.Context, d TDateTime, seconds int64, where string) TDateTime {
	return calKeepZone(calSeconds(ctx, calFromDateTime(d), seconds, where), d)
}
func calDateTimeDiff(a, b TDateTime) int64 {
	return int64(calFromDateTime(a).Sub(calFromDateTime(b)) / time.Second)
}

// orderAtom is :lt, :eq or :gt for a comparison's sign.
func orderAtom(n int64) actor.Atom {
	switch {
	case n < 0:
		return "lt"
	case n > 0:
		return "gt"
	}
	return "eq"
}

func calDateCompareAtom(a, b TDate) actor.Atom { return orderAtom(calDateCompare(a, b)) }
func calTimeCompareAtom(a, b TTime) actor.Atom { return orderAtom(calTimeCompare(a, b)) }
func calNaiveDateTimeCompareAtom(a, b TNaiveDateTime) actor.Atom {
	return orderAtom(calNaiveDateTimeCompare(a, b))
}
func calDateTimeCompareAtom(a, b TDateTime) actor.Atom { return orderAtom(calDateTimeCompare(a, b)) }

// calUnit is the number of microseconds in a unit of time.
func calUnit(ctx *actor.Context, unit actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	switch unit {
	case "day":
		return 86400 * 1000000
	case "hour":
		return 3600 * 1000000
	case "minute":
		return 60 * 1000000
	case "second":
		return 1000000
	case "millisecond":
		return 1000
	case "microsecond":
		return 1
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "the unit is :day, :hour, :minute, :second, :millisecond or :microsecond, not :"+string(unit))
	return 1
}

func calMoveMicros(ctx *actor.Context, t time.Time, amount, per int64, where string) time.Time {
	if amount > (1<<62)/per || amount < -(1<<62)/per {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the amount to add is out of range")
	}
	return t.Add(time.Duration(amount*per) * time.Microsecond)
}

func calNaiveDateTimeAddUnit(ctx *actor.Context, n TNaiveDateTime, amount int64, unit actor.Atom, where string) TNaiveDateTime {
	return calNaiveOf(calMoveMicros(ctx, calFromNaiveTime(n), amount, calUnit(ctx, unit, where), where))
}
func calDateTimeAddUnit(ctx *actor.Context, d TDateTime, amount int64, unit actor.Atom, where string) TDateTime {
	return calKeepZone(calMoveMicros(ctx, calFromDateTime(d), amount, calUnit(ctx, unit, where), where), d)
}
func calTimeAddUnit(ctx *actor.Context, t TTime, amount int64, unit actor.Atom, where string) TTime {
	per := calUnit(ctx, unit, where)
	micros := ((calMicros(t)+(amount%(calDay/per+1))*per)%calDay + calDay) % calDay
	return TTime{micros / 3600000000, micros / 60000000 % 60, micros / 1000000 % 60, micros % 1000000}
}

func calNaiveDateTimeDiffUnit(ctx *actor.Context, a, b TNaiveDateTime, unit actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	return int64(calFromNaiveTime(a).Sub(calFromNaiveTime(b))/time.Microsecond) / calUnit(ctx, unit, where)
}
func calDateTimeDiffUnit(ctx *actor.Context, a, b TDateTime, unit actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	return int64(calFromDateTime(a).Sub(calFromDateTime(b))/time.Microsecond) / calUnit(ctx, unit, where)
}
func calTimeDiffUnit(ctx *actor.Context, a, b TTime, unit actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	return (calMicros(a) - calMicros(b)) / calUnit(ctx, unit, where)
}

// calTruncation keeps the microseconds down to a precision: :second keeps
// none, :millisecond thousands of them.
func calTruncation(ctx *actor.Context, micro int64, precision actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	switch precision {
	case "second":
		return 0
	case "millisecond":
		return micro / 1000 * 1000
	case "microsecond":
		return micro
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "truncate takes :second, :millisecond or :microsecond")
	return micro
}

func calTimeTruncate(ctx *actor.Context, t TTime, precision actor.Atom, where string) TTime {
	t.F3 = calTruncation(ctx, t.F3, precision, where)
	return t
}
func calNaiveDateTimeTruncate(ctx *actor.Context, n TNaiveDateTime, precision actor.Atom, where string) TNaiveDateTime {
	n.F6 = calTruncation(ctx, n.F6, precision, where)
	return n
}
func calDateTimeTruncate(ctx *actor.Context, d TDateTime, precision actor.Atom, where string) TDateTime {
	d.F6 = calTruncation(ctx, d.F6, precision, where)
	return d
}

// calDateRange is the dates from first to last, a step of days apart;
// backwards when last is earlier and no step is given.
func calDateRange(first, last TDate) iter.Seq[TDate] {
	step := int64(1)
	if calDateCompare(last, first) < 0 {
		step = -1
	}
	return calDateRangeStep(nil, first, last, step, "")
}

func calDateRangeStep(ctx *actor.Context, first, last TDate, step int64, where string) iter.Seq[TDate] {
	if step == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Date.range needs a step other than 0")
	}
	return func(yield func(TDate) bool) {
		for d := first; step > 0 && calDateCompare(d, last) <= 0 || step < 0 && calDateCompare(d, last) >= 0; d = calDateOf(calFromDate(d).AddDate(0, 0, int(step))) {
			if !yield(d) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

// The week functions take the day a week starts on: :monday by default, or
// :tuesday to :sunday.
func calStartDay(ctx *actor.Context, day actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	for i, name := range []actor.Atom{"monday", "tuesday", "wednesday", "thursday", "friday", "saturday", "sunday"} {
		if name == day {
			return int64(i + 1)
		}
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "a week starts on :monday, :tuesday, :wednesday, :thursday, :friday, :saturday or :sunday")
	return 1
}

func calDayOfWeekFrom(ctx *actor.Context, d TDate, start actor.Atom, where string) int64 {
	return (calDayOfWeek(d)-calStartDay(ctx, start, where)+7)%7 + 1
}
func calWeekStartFrom(ctx *actor.Context, d TDate, start actor.Atom, where string) TDate {
	return calDateOf(calFromDate(d).AddDate(0, 0, -int(calDayOfWeekFrom(ctx, d, start, where)-1)))
}
func calWeekEndFrom(ctx *actor.Context, d TDate, start actor.Atom, where string) TDate {
	return calDateOf(calFromDate(d).AddDate(0, 0, int(7-calDayOfWeekFrom(ctx, d, start, where))))
}

// calWeekOfYear is the ISO 8601 week of the year, from 1 to 53.
func calWeekOfYear(d TDate) int64 {
	_, week := calFromDate(d).ISOWeek()
	return int64(week)
}

// durationNew builds a duration from a keyword list of units.
func durationNew(ctx *actor.Context, units L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) TDuration {
	var d TDuration
	for _, unit := range fwd(units) {
		switch unit.E0 {
		case "year":
			d.F0 += unit.E1
		case "month":
			d.F1 += unit.E1
		case "week":
			d.F2 += unit.E1
		case "day":
			d.F3 += unit.E1
		case "hour":
			d.F4 += unit.E1
		case "minute":
			d.F5 += unit.E1
		case "second":
			d.F6 += unit.E1
		case "microsecond":
			d.F7 += unit.E1
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "Duration.new! takes year:, month:, week:, day:, hour:, minute:, second: and microsecond:")
		}
	}
	return d
}

func calDateNew(year, month, day int64) Tresult[TDate, string] {
	if !calValid(year, month, day, 0, 0, 0, 0) {
		return errOf[TDate]("invalid date")
	}
	return okOf(TDate{year, month, day})
}
func calTimeNewMicro(hour, minute, second, microsecond int64) Tresult[TTime, string] {
	if !calValid(2000, 1, 1, hour, minute, second, microsecond) {
		return errOf[TTime]("invalid time")
	}
	return okOf(TTime{hour, minute, second, microsecond})
}
func calTimeNew(hour, minute, second int64) Tresult[TTime, string] {
	return calTimeNewMicro(hour, minute, second, 0)
}
func calNaiveNew(year, month, day, hour, minute, second int64) Tresult[TNaiveDateTime, string] {
	if !calValid(year, month, day, hour, minute, second, 0) {
		return errOf[TNaiveDateTime]("invalid date or time")
	}
	return okOf(TNaiveDateTime{year, month, day, hour, minute, second, 0})
}
func calNaiveJoin(d TDate, t TTime) Tresult[TNaiveDateTime, string] {
	if !calValid(d.F0, d.F1, d.F2, t.F0, t.F1, t.F2, t.F3) {
		return errOf[TNaiveDateTime]("invalid date or time")
	}
	return okOf(TNaiveDateTime{d.F0, d.F1, d.F2, t.F0, t.F1, t.F2, t.F3})
}

// calOk unwraps a result for the ! forms.
func calOk[T any](ctx *actor.Context, outcome Tresult[T, string], where string) T {
	switch value := outcome.(type) {
	case Tresult_c0[T, string]:
		return value.F0
	case Tresult_c1[T, string]:
