func tcpConnectWith(ctx *actor.Context, address string, ms int64, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Tresult[*socketHandle, string] {
	connecting := tcpConnect(ctx, address, ms)
	if s, ok := connecting.(Tresult_c0[*socketHandle, string]); ok {
		if result := tcpSetopts(ctx, s.F0, options, where); !isOk[struct{}, string](result) {
			s.F0.close()
			return errOf[*socketHandle](errorOf[struct{}, string](result))
		}
	}
	return connecting
}

// tcpOptions checks options given as atoms and returns them.
func tcpOptions(ctx *actor.Context, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], function, where string) L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]] {
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		valid := option.E0 == "active" && (option.E1 == "true" || option.E1 == "once" || option.E1 == "false") ||
			option.E0 == "packet" && (option.E1 == "raw" || option.E1 == "line")
		if !valid {
			failAt(ctx, where, function+": the options are active: true, :once or false, and packet: :raw or :line, not "+string(option.E0)+": :"+string(option.E1))
		}
	}
	return options
}

func isOk[T, E any](r Tresult[T, E]) bool {
	_, ok := r.(Tresult_c0[T, E])
	return ok
}

func errorOf[T, E any](r Tresult[T, E]) E {
	return r.(Tresult_c1[T, E]).F0
}

func tcpShutdown(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, how actor.Atom, where string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	tcp, ok := s.conn.(*net.TCPConn)
	if !ok {
		return errOf[struct{}]("not a connected socket")
	}
	var err error
	switch how {
	case "write":
		err = tcp.CloseWrite()
	case "read":
		err = tcp.CloseRead()
	case "read_write":
		s.close()
	default:
		failAt(ctx, where, "TCP.shutdown takes :read, :write or :read_write")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[struct{}](netProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

func tcpConnect(ctx *actor.Context, address string, ms int64) Tresult[*socketHandle, string] {
	conn, err := netDial(ctx, address, ms)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*socketHandle](netProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(newConnection(ctx, conn))
}

// netDial connects to a TCP address, waiting up to ms milliseconds (negative
// waits as long as the system allows); a process asked to stop while it waits
// stops.
func netDial(ctx *actor.Context, address string, ms int64) (net.Conn, error) {
	dialer := net.Dialer{}
	if ms >= 0 {
		dialer.Timeout = time.Duration(ms) * time.Millisecond
	}
	done := make(chan struct{})
	cancelled := make(chan struct{})
	dialContext, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	go func() {
		select {
		case <-actor.Stopping(ctx):
			close(cancelled)
			cancel()
		case <-done:
		}
	}()
	conn, err := dialer.DialContext(dialContext, "tcp", address)
	close(done)
	select {
	case <-cancelled:
		if conn != nil {
			conn.Close()
		}
		actor.Check(ctx)
	default:
	}
	return conn, err
}

func newConnection(ctx *actor.Context, conn net.Conn) *socketHandle {
	s := &socketHandle{conn: conn, reader: bufio.NewReader(conn)}
	s.own(ctx)
	return s
}

func (s *socketHandle) close() bool {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return false
	}
	s.closed = true
	if s.listener != nil {
		s.listener.Close()
	}
	if s.conn != nil {
		s.conn.Close()
	}
	if s.datagrams != nil {
		s.datagrams.Close()
	}
	return true
}

// packetHeader is the size of a packet's length header, or 0.
func (s *socketHandle) packetHeader() int {
	switch s.packet {
	case "1":
		return 1
	case "2":
		return 2
	case "4":
		return 4
	}
	return 0
}

// tcpPacketLimit bounds a packet with a length header.
const tcpPacketLimit = 64 << 20

// readPacket reads the next packet by the socket's packet mode: whatever has
// arrived, a line with its newline, or the body of a packet with a length
// header. A read cut short by the deadline keeps what it has in partial, so
// that the next call goes on from there.
func (s *socketHandle) readPacket() (string, error) {
	take := func() string {
		out := string(s.partial)
		s.partial = nil
		return out
	}
	switch header := s.packetHeader(); {
	case s.packet == "line":
		for {
			part, err := s.reader.ReadSlice('\n')
			s.partial = append(s.partial, part...)
			switch {
			case err == nil:
				return take(), nil
			case errors.Is(err, bufio.ErrBufferFull):
				if len(s.partial) > tcpPacketLimit {
					return "", errors.New("line too long")
				}
				continue
			}
			return "", err
		}
	case header > 0:
		for {
			need := header
			if len(s.partial) >= header {
				size := 0
				for _, b := range s.partial[:header] {
					size = size<<8 | int(b)
				}
				if size > tcpPacketLimit {
					return "", errors.New("packet too large")
				}
				need += size
				if len(s.partial) == need {
					s.partial = s.partial[header:]
					return take(), nil
				}
			}
			chunk := make([]byte, need-len(s.partial))
			n, err := s.reader.Read(chunk)
			s.partial = append(s.partial, chunk[:n]...)
			if err != nil {
				return "", err
			}
		}
	default:
		if len(s.partial) > 0 {
			return take(), nil
		}
		if _, err := s.reader.Peek(1); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
		chunk := make([]byte, s.reader.Buffered())
		n, err := s.reader.Read(chunk)
		return string(chunk[:n]), err
	}
}

// frame adds the length header a packet mode calls for.
func (s *socketHandle) frame(data string) (string, bool) {
	header := s.packetHeader()
	if header == 0 {
		return data, true
	}
	if header < 4 && len(data) >= 1<<(8*header) || len(data) > tcpPacketLimit {
		return "", false
	}
	prefix := make([]byte, header)
	for i := range prefix {
		prefix[header-1-i] = byte(len(data) >> (8 * i))
	}
	return string(prefix) + data, true
}

// eventReceiver is where an active socket sends its events.
func (s *socketHandle) eventReceiver() (*actor.PID[TTCP_oEvent], bool) {
	if s.receiver != nil {
		p, ok := s.receiver.(*actor.PID[TTCP_oEvent])
		return p, ok
	}
	if s.owner == nil {
		return nil, false
	}
	return actor.MailboxOf[TTCP_oEvent](s.owner)
}

// pump sends packets to the receiver while the socket is active. It reads
// in slices, so that a socket made passive stops between packets.
func (s *socketHandle) pump(receiver *actor.PID[TTCP_oEvent]) {
	send := func(event TTCP_oEvent) {
		for !actor.Deliver(receiver, event, netSlice) {
			select {
			case <-actor.Ended(receiver):
				return
			default:
			}
		}
	}
	for {
		s.mu.Lock()
		if s.closed || s.active == 0 {
			s.reading = false
			s.mu.Unlock()
			return
		}
		s.mu.Unlock()
		s.reads.Lock()
		s.conn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(netSlice))
		data, err := s.readPacket()
		s.reads.Unlock()
		var timeout net.Error
		if errors.As(err, &timeout) && timeout.Timeout() {
			continue
		}
		s.mu.Lock()
		if s.closed {
			// A socket closed here sends nothing about it.
			s.reading = false
			s.mu.Unlock()
			return
		}
		if err != nil {
			s.active, s.reading = 0, false
			s.mu.Unlock()
			if problem := netProblem(err); problem == "closed" {
				send(TTCP_oEvent(TTCP_oEvent_c1{}))
			} else {
				send(TTCP_oEvent(TTCP_oEvent_c2{problem}))
			}
			return
		}
		if s.active > 0 {
			s.active--
		}
		s.mu.Unlock()
		send(TTCP_oEvent(TTCP_oEvent_c0{data}))
	}
}

// tcpSetopts sets the socket options given as atoms: active: true, :once or
// false, and packet: :raw or :line.
func tcpSetopts(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		switch {
		case option.E0 == "active" && (option.E1 == "true" || option.E1 == "once" || option.E1 == "false"):
			count := map[actor.Atom]int64{"true": -1, "once": 1, "false": 0}[option.E1]
			if problem := tcpActivate(s, count); problem != "" {
				return errOf[struct{}](problem)
			}
		case option.E0 == "packet" && (option.E1 == "raw" || option.E1 == "line"):
			s.mu.Lock()
			s.packet = string(option.E1)
			s.mu.Unlock()
		case option.E0 == "active" || option.E0 == "packet":
			failAt(ctx, where, "TCP.setopts: "+string(option.E0)+": cannot be :"+string(option.E1))
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "TCP.setopts: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; the options are active: and packet:")
		}
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

// tcpSetoptsNumbers sets the options given as numbers: active: n packets,
// and packet: 0, 1, 2 or 4 bytes of length header.
func tcpSetoptsNumbers(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		switch {
		case option.E0 == "active" && option.E1 >= 0:
			if problem := tcpActivate(s, option.E1); problem != "" {
				return errOf[struct{}](problem)
			}
		case option.E0 == "packet" && (option.E1 == 0 || option.E1 == 1 || option.E1 == 2 || option.E1 == 4):
			s.mu.Lock()
			s.packet = map[int64]string{0: "raw", 1: "1", 2: "2", 4: "4"}[option.E1]
			s.mu.Unlock()
		case option.E0 == "active" || option.E0 == "packet":
			failAt(ctx, where, "TCP.setopts: "+string(option.E0)+": cannot be "+strconv.FormatInt(option.E1, 10))
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "TCP.setopts: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; the options are active: and packet:")
		}
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

// tcpActivate sets how many packets the socket sends as messages.
func tcpActivate(s *socketHandle, count int64) string {
	if s.conn == nil {
		return "not a connected socket"
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return "closed"
	}
	s.active = count
	if count == 0 || s.reading {
		return ""
	}
	receiver, ok := s.eventReceiver()
	if !ok {
		s.active = 0
		return "the controlling process does not take TCP.Event messages"
	}
	s.reading = true
	go s.pump(receiver)
	return ""
}

// passive reports why a passive read cannot be made now.
func (s *socketHandle) passive() string {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	switch {
	case s.conn == nil:
		return "not a connected socket"
	case s.active != 0 || s.reading:
		return "the socket is active"
	}
	return ""
}

func tcpAccept(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, ms int64) Tresult[*socketHandle, string] {
	if s.listener == nil {
		return errOf[*socketHandle]("not a listening socket")
	}
	tcp, ok := s.listener.(*net.TCPListener)
	if !ok {
		return errOf[*socketHandle]("not a listening socket")
	}
	var conn net.Conn
	err := netWait(ctx, ms, tcp.SetDeadline, func() error {
		var err error
		conn, err = tcp.Accept()
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*socketHandle](netProblem(err))
	}
	accepted := newConnection(ctx, conn)
	if len(s.inherit.S) > 0 {
		// An accepted socket that the acceptor cannot take events from
		// stays passive.
		tcpSetopts(ctx, accepted, s.inherit, "")
	}
	return okOf(accepted)
}

func tcpSend(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, data string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	actor.Check(ctx)
	if s.conn == nil {
		return errOf[struct{}]("not a connected socket")
	}
	framed, fits := s.frame(data)
	if !fits {
		return errOf[struct{}]("packet too large for its length header")
	}
	if _, err := io.WriteString(s.conn, framed); err != nil {
		return errOf[struct{}](netProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

// tcpRecv reads exactly length bytes, or, for a length of 0, whatever is
// available once something is.
func tcpRecv(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, length, ms int64, where string) Tresult[string, string] {
	if length < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "TCP.recv needs a nonnegative length")
	}
	if problem := s.passive(); problem != "" {
		return errOf[string](problem)
	}
	s.reads.Lock()
	defer s.reads.Unlock()
	var out []byte
	if length == 0 {
		// One packet, by the socket's packet mode.
		err := netWait(ctx, ms, s.conn.SetReadDeadline, func() error {
			packet, err := s.readPacket()
			out = []byte(packet)
			return err
		})
		if err != nil {
			return errOf[string](netProblem(err))
		}
		return okOf(string(out))
	}
	if s.packet != "" && s.packet != "raw" {
		return errOf[string]("a length is only given in raw packet mode")
	}
	out, s.partial = s.partial, nil
	if int64(len(out)) > length {
		out, s.partial = out[:length], out[length:]
	}
	err := netWait(ctx, ms, s.conn.SetReadDeadline, func() error {
		if int64(len(out)) == length {
			return nil
		}
		chunk := make([]byte, length-int64(len(out)))
		n, err := io.ReadFull(s.reader, chunk)
		out = append(out, chunk[:n]...)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		if problem := netProblem(err); problem == "timeout" {
			// What arrived waits for the next read.
			s.partial = out
			return errOf[string](problem)
		}
		return errOf[string](netProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(string(out))
}

// tcpRecvLine reads up to and including a newline, which it drops along with
// a carriage return before it.
func tcpRecvLine(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, ms int64) Tresult[string, string] {
	if problem := s.passive(); problem != "" {
		return errOf[string](problem)
	}
	s.reads.Lock()
	line := s.partial
	s.partial = nil
	s.reads.Unlock()
	err := netWait(ctx, ms, s.conn.SetReadDeadline, func() error {
		part, err := s.reader.ReadSlice('\n')
		line = append(line, part...)
		if errors.Is(err, bufio.ErrBufferFull) {
			return nil
		}
		return err
	})
	if err == nil && (len(line) == 0 || line[len(line)-1] != '\n') {
		// A full buffer is not the end of the line.
		rest := tcpRecvLine(ctx, s, ms)
		if text, ok := rest.(Tresult_c0[string, string]); ok {
			return okOf(string(line) + text.F0)
		}
		return rest
	}
	if err != nil {
		if problem := netProblem(err); problem == "timeout" {
			// What arrived of the line waits for the next read.
			s.reads.Lock()
			s.partial = append(line, s.partial...)
			s.reads.Unlock()
			return errOf[string](problem)
		}
		return errOf[string](netProblem(err))
	}
	text := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimSuffix(string(line), "\n"), "\r")
	return okOf(text)
}

func tcpClose(s *socketHandle) bool { return s.close() }

// tcpControllingProcess hands a socket to another process of this program,
// whose end then closes it instead.
func tcpControllingProcess[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, p *actor.PID[A]) bool {
	owner := actor.ContextOf(p)
	if owner == nil {
		return false
	}
	s.own(owner)
	s.mu.Lock()
	s.receiver = p
	s.mu.Unlock()
	return true
}

func tcpAddress(s *socketHandle) string {
	if s.listener != nil {
		return s.listener.Addr().String()
	}
	if s.datagrams != nil {
		return s.datagrams.LocalAddr().String()
	}
	return s.conn.LocalAddr().String()
}

func tcpPeer(s *socketHandle) Toption[string] {
	if s.conn == nil {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	return someOf(s.conn.RemoteAddr().String())
}

// ---- UDP ----

func udpOpen(ctx *actor.Context, address string) Tresult[*socketHandle, string] {
	conn, err := net.ListenPacket("udp", address)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*socketHandle](err.Error())
	}
	s := &socketHandle{datagrams: conn}
	s.own(ctx)
	return okOf(s)
}

// udpLimit is the largest datagram read.
const udpLimit = 65535

func udpSend(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, address, data string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	actor.Check(ctx)
	if s.datagrams == nil {
		return errOf[struct{}]("not a UDP socket")
	}
	to, err := net.ResolveUDPAddr("udp", address)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[struct{}](err.Error())
	}
	if _, err := s.datagrams.WriteTo([]byte(data), to); err != nil {
		return errOf[struct{}](netProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

func udpRecv(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, ms int64) Tresult[pair[string, string], string] {
	if s.datagrams == nil {
		return errOf[pair[string, string]]("not a UDP socket")
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	active := s.active != 0 || s.reading
	s.mu.Unlock()
	if active {
		return errOf[pair[string, string]]("the socket is active")
	}
	s.reads.Lock()
	defer s.reads.Unlock()
	buffer := make([]byte, udpLimit)
	var got pair[string, string]
	err := netWait(ctx, ms, s.datagrams.SetReadDeadline, func() error {
		n, from, err := s.datagrams.ReadFrom(buffer)
		if err == nil {
			got = pair[string, string]{from.String(), string(buffer[:n])}
		}
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[pair[string, string]](netProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(got)
}

func udpSetopts(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		count, valid := map[actor.Atom]int64{"true": -1, "once": 1, "false": 0}[option.E1]
		if option.E0 != "active" || !valid {
			failAt(ctx, where, "UDP.setopts takes active: true, :once or false")
		}
		if problem := udpActivate(s, count); problem != "" {
			return errOf[struct{}](problem)
		}
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

func udpSetoptsNumbers(ctx *actor.Context, s *socketHandle, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		if option.E0 != "active" || option.E1 < 0 {
			failAt(ctx, where, "UDP.setopts takes active: and a number of datagrams")
		}
		if problem := udpActivate(s, option.E1); problem != "" {
			return errOf[struct{}](problem)
		}
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

func udpActivate(s *socketHandle, count int64) string {
	if s.datagrams == nil {
		return "not a UDP socket"
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return "closed"
	}
	s.active = count
	if count == 0 || s.reading {
		return ""
	}
	var receiver *actor.PID[TUDP_oEvent]
	ok := false
	if s.receiver != nil {
		receiver, ok = s.receiver.(*actor.PID[TUDP_oEvent])
	} else if s.owner != nil {
		receiver, ok = actor.MailboxOf[TUDP_oEvent](s.owner)
	}
	if !ok {
		s.active = 0
		return "the controlling process does not take UDP.Event messages"
	}
	s.reading = true
	go s.pumpDatagrams(receiver)
	return ""
}

func (s *socketHandle) pumpDatagrams(receiver *actor.PID[TUDP_oEvent]) {
	buffer := make([]byte, udpLimit)
	for {
		s.mu.Lock()
		if s.closed || s.active == 0 {
			s.reading = false
			s.mu.Unlock()
			return
		}
		s.mu.Unlock()
		s.reads.Lock()
		s.datagrams.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(netSlice))
		n, from, err := s.datagrams.ReadFrom(buffer)
		s.reads.Unlock()
		var timeout net.Error
		if errors.As(err, &timeout) && timeout.Timeout() {
			continue
		}
		s.mu.Lock()
		if s.closed {
			s.reading = false
			s.mu.Unlock()
			return
		}
		var event TUDP_oEvent
		if err != nil {
			event = TUDP_oEvent(TUDP_oEvent_c1{netProblem(err)})
			s.active, s.reading = 0, false
		} else {
			event = TUDP_oEvent(TUDP_oEvent_c0{from.String(), string(buffer[:n])})
			if s.active > 0 {
				s.active--
			}
		}
		s.mu.Unlock()
		for !actor.Deliver(receiver, event, netSlice) {
			select {
			case <-actor.Ended(receiver):
				return
			default:
			}
		}
		if err != nil {
			return
		}
	}
}

// ---- Generators ----

// shrinkTree is a generated value with the smaller values it could shrink
// to, made only when a property fails and they are needed.
type shrinkTree[T any] struct {
	value   T
	shrinks func() []shrinkTree[T]
}

func leaf[T any](value T) shrinkTree[T] {
	return shrinkTree[T]{value, func() []shrinkTree[T] { return nil }}
}

func mapTree[A, B any](t shrinkTree[A], f func(A) B) shrinkTree[B] {
	return shrinkTree[B]{f(t.value), func() []shrinkTree[B] {
		var out []shrinkTree[B]
		for _, s := range t.shrinks() {
			out = append(out, mapTree(s, f))
		}
		return out
	}}
}

// generator makes random values of a type, larger as size grows.
type generator[T any] struct {
	run func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[T]
}

// shrinkInt lists smaller integers to try in place of n, nearest zero first.
func shrinkInt(n, toward int64) []int64 {
	var out []int64
	for gap := n - toward; gap != 0; gap /= 2 {
		if candidate := n - gap; candidate != n {
			out = append(out, candidate)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func intTree(n, toward int64) shrinkTree[int64] {
	return shrinkTree[int64]{n, func() []shrinkTree[int64] {
		var out []shrinkTree[int64]
		for _, candidate := range shrinkInt(n, toward) {
			out = append(out, intTree(candidate, toward))
		}
		return out
	}}
}

func genInteger() *generator[int64] {
	return &generator[int64]{func(_ *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[int64] {
		return intTree(random.Int64N(int64(2*size+1))-int64(size), 0)
	}}
}

func genIntegerBetween(ctx *actor.Context, low, high int64, where string) *generator[int64] {
	if low > high {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Gen.integer needs its low bound no greater than its high one")
	}
	toward := min(max(int64(0), low), high)
	return &generator[int64]{func(_ *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[int64] {
		span := uint64(high - low)
		var n int64
		if span == math.MaxUint64 {
			n = int64(random.Uint64())
		} else {
			n = low + int64(random.Uint64N(span+1))
		}
		return intTree(n, toward)
	}}
}

func genPositiveInteger() *generator[int64] {
	return &generator[int64]{func(_ *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[int64] {
		return intTree(1+random.Int64N(int64(size)+1), 1)
	}}
}

func genFloat() *generator[float64] {
	return &generator[float64]{func(_ *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[float64] {
		var tree func(x float64) shrinkTree[float64]
		tree = func(x float64) shrinkTree[float64] {
			return shrinkTree[float64]{x, func() []shrinkTree[float64] {
				var out []shrinkTree[float64]
				for _, candidate := range []float64{0, math.Trunc(x), x / 2} {
					if candidate != x && math.Abs(candidate) < math.Abs(x) {
						out = append(out, tree(candidate))
					}
				}
				return out
			}}
		}
		return tree((random.Float64()*2 - 1) * float64(size))
	}}
}

func genBoolean() *generator[bool] {
	return &generator[bool]{func(_ *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, _ int) shrinkTree[bool] {
		if random.IntN(2) == 0 {
			return leaf(false)
		}
		return shrinkTree[bool]{true, func() []shrinkTree[bool] { return []shrinkTree[bool]{leaf(false)} }}
	}}
}

func genConstant[A any](value A) *generator[A] {
	return &generator[A]{func(*actor.Context, *rand.Rand, int) shrinkTree[A] { return leaf(value) }}
}

// listTree shrinks a list by leaving out a run of elements, the first half,
// the second, then each one, and then by shrinking an element.
func listTree[A any](items []shrinkTree[A], least int) shrinkTree[[]A] {
	values := make([]A, len(items))
	for i, item := range items {
		values[i] = item.value
	}
	return shrinkTree[[]A]{values, func() []shrinkTree[[]A] {
		var out []shrinkTree[[]A]
		without := func(from, to int) {
			if len(items)-(to-from) >= least {
				rest := append(append([]shrinkTree[A]{}, items[:from]...), items[to:]...)
				out = append(out, listTree(rest, least))
			}
		}
		if len(items) > 1 {
			without(0, len(items)/2)
			without(len(items)/2, len(items))
		}
		for i := range items {
			without(i, i+1)
		}
		for i, item := range items {
			for _, smaller := range item.shrinks() {
				changed := append([]shrinkTree[A]{}, items...)
				changed[i] = smaller
				out = append(out, listTree(changed, least))
			}
		}
		return out
	}}
}

func genListOf[A any](g *generator[A]) *generator[L[A]] {
	return genListBetween(nil, g, 0, -1, "")
}

func genListBetween[A any](ctx *actor.Context, g *generator[A], least, most int64, where string) *generator[L[A]] {
	if least < 0 || most >= 0 && most < least {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Gen.list_of needs 0 <= min <= max")
	}
	return &generator[L[A]]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[L[A]] {
		high := int64(size)
		if most >= 0 {
			high = most
		}
		high = max(high, least)
		n := least + random.Int64N(high-least+1)
		items := make([]shrinkTree[A], n)
		for i := range items {
			items[i] = g.run(ctx, random, size)
		}
		return mapTree(listTree(items, int(least)), func(values []A) L[A] { return mk(slices.Clone(values)) })
	}}
}

// The characters each kind of generated string is made of.
var genAlphabets = map[actor.Atom]string{
	"alphanumeric": "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789",
	"ascii":        " !\"#$%&'()*+,-./0123456789:;<=>?@ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ[\\]^_`abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz{|}~",
	"alpha":        "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ",
	"lower":        "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz",
	"digit":        "0123456789",
	"printable":    "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789 éüñßøλжあ中😀",
}

func genString(ctx *actor.Context, kind actor.Atom, where string) *generator[string] {
	alphabet, ok := genAlphabets[kind]
	if !ok {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Gen.string takes :alphanumeric, :alpha, :lower, :digit, :ascii or :printable")
	}
	letters := []rune(alphabet)
	letter := &generator[rune]{func(_ *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, _ int) shrinkTree[rune] {
		return mapTree(intTree(random.Int64N(int64(len(letters))), 0), func(i int64) rune { return letters[i] })
	}}
	list := genListOf(letter)
	return &generator[string]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[string] {
		return mapTree(list.run(ctx, random, size), func(l L[rune]) string { return string(fwd(l)) })
	}}
}

func genMember[A any](ctx *actor.Context, items iter.Seq[A], where string) *generator[A] {
	choices := slices.Collect(items)
	if len(choices) == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Gen.member_of needs at least one value")
	}
	return &generator[A]{func(_ *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, _ int) shrinkTree[A] {
		return mapTree(intTree(random.Int64N(int64(len(choices))), 0), func(i int64) A { return choices[i] })
	}}
}

func genOneOf[A any](ctx *actor.Context, gens L[*generator[A]], where string) *generator[A] {
	choices := fwd(gens)
	if len(choices) == 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Gen.one_of needs at least one generator")
	}
	return &generator[A]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[A] {
		return choices[random.IntN(len(choices))].run(ctx, random, size)
	}}
}

func genMap[A, B any](g *generator[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) B) *generator[B] {
	return &generator[B]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[B] {
		return mapTree(g.run(ctx, random, size), func(a A) B { return f(ctx, a) })
	}}
}

// filterTree keeps the shrinks that still pass the test.
func filterTree[A any](t shrinkTree[A], keep func(A) bool) shrinkTree[A] {
	return shrinkTree[A]{t.value, func() []shrinkTree[A] {
		var out []shrinkTree[A]
		for _, s := range t.shrinks() {
			if keep(s.value) {
				out = append(out, filterTree(s, keep))
			}
		}
		return out
	}}
}

func genFilter[A any](g *generator[A], keep func(*actor.Context, A) bool, where string) *generator[A] {
	return &generator[A]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[A] {
		for tries := 0; tries < 100; tries++ {
			if t := g.run(ctx, random, size); keep(ctx, t.value) {
				return filterTree(t, func(a A) bool { return keep(ctx, a) })
			}
		}
		failAt(ctx, where, "Gen.filter rejected 100 values in a row; generate more of the values wanted instead")
		panic("unreachable")
	}}
}

// bindTree shrinks the first value, generating the second anew from each,
// then the second.
func bindTree[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, seed uint64, size int, outer shrinkTree[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) *generator[B]) shrinkTree[B] {
	inner := f(ctx, outer.value).run(ctx, rand.New(rand.NewPCG(seed, seed)), size)
	return shrinkTree[B]{inner.value, func() []shrinkTree[B] {
		var out []shrinkTree[B]
		for _, smaller := range outer.shrinks() {
			out = append(out, bindTree(ctx, seed, size, smaller, f))
		}
		return append(out, inner.shrinks()...)
	}}
}

func genBind[A, B any](g *generator[A], f func(*actor.Context, A) *generator[B]) *generator[B] {
	return &generator[B]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[B] {
		outer := g.run(ctx, random, size)
		return bindTree(ctx, random.Uint64(), size, outer, f)
	}}
}

func pairTree[A, B any](a shrinkTree[A], b shrinkTree[B]) shrinkTree[pair[A, B]] {
	return shrinkTree[pair[A, B]]{pair[A, B]{a.value, b.value}, func() []shrinkTree[pair[A, B]] {
		var out []shrinkTree[pair[A, B]]
		for _, s := range a.shrinks() {
			out = append(out, pairTree(s, b))
		}
		for _, s := range b.shrinks() {
			out = append(out, pairTree(a, s))
		}
		return out
	}}
}

func genTuple[A, B any](first *generator[A], second *generator[B]) *generator[pair[A, B]] {
	return &generator[pair[A, B]]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[pair[A, B]] {
		return pairTree(first.run(ctx, random, size), second.run(ctx, random, size))
	}}
}

func genTuple3[A, B, C any](first *generator[A], second *generator[B], third *generator[C]) *generator[triple[A, B, C]] {
	inner := genTuple(genTuple(first, second), third)
	return &generator[triple[A, B, C]]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[triple[A, B, C]] {
		return mapTree(inner.run(ctx, random, size), func(p pair[pair[A, B], C]) triple[A, B, C] {
			return triple[A, B, C]{p.E0.E0, p.E0.E1, p.E1}
		})
	}}
}

func genMapOf[K comparable, V any](keys *generator[K], values *generator[V]) *generator[actor.Map[K, V]] {
	entries := genListOf(genTuple(keys, values))
	return &generator[actor.Map[K, V]]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, size int) shrinkTree[actor.Map[K, V]] {
		return mapTree(entries.run(ctx, random, size), func(l L[pair[K, V]]) actor.Map[K, V] {
			m := actor.Map[K, V]{}
			for _, entry := range fwd(l) {
				m = actor.MapPut(m, entry.E0, entry.E1)
			}
			return m
		})
	}}
}

func genResize[A any](g *generator[A], size int64) *generator[A] {
	return &generator[A]{func(ctx *actor.Context, random *rand.Rand, _ int) shrinkTree[A] {
		return g.run(ctx, random, int(max(size, 0)))
	}}
}

// genRandom is the source a property draws from: from ALCHEM_SEED when the
// test runner set it, so that a failure can be repeated with --seed.
func genRandom() *rand.Rand {
	seed, _ := strconv.ParseUint(os.Getenv("ALCHEM_SEED"), 10, 64)
	if seed == 0 {
		seed = uint64(time.Now().UnixNano())
	}
	return rand.New(rand.NewPCG(seed, seed^0x5851f42d4c957f2d))
}

func genSample[A any](ctx *actor.Context, g *generator[A], count int64) L[A] {
	random := genRandom()
	out := make([]A, 0, max(count, 0))
	for i := int64(0); i < count; i++ {
		out = append(out, g.run(ctx, random, int(min(i+1, 100))).value)
	}
	return mk(out)
}

// genRuns is how many values a property is checked with, unless
// ALCHEM_PROPERTY_RUNS says otherwise.
func genRuns() int {
	if n, err := strconv.Atoi(os.Getenv("ALCHEM_PROPERTY_RUNS")); err == nil && n > 0 {
		return n
	}
	return 100
}

func genCheckDefault[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, g *generator[A], property func(*actor.Context, A) B, where string) {
	genCheck(ctx, g, property, 0, where)
}

// genCheck runs a property on generated values, growing their size; when it
// fails, it shrinks the value to the smallest that still fails and reports
// that one.
func genCheck[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, g *generator[A], property func(*actor.Context, A) B, runs int64, where string) {
	attempt := func(value A) string {
		task := actor.StartUnlinkedTask(ctx, func(child *actor.Context) B { return property(child, value) }, where)
		return actor.YieldTask(ctx, task, -1, where).Reason
	}
	random := genRandom()
	if runs <= 0 {
		runs = int64(genRuns())
	}
	for run := int64(0); run < runs; run++ {
		tree := g.run(ctx, random, int(min(run*2+1, 100)))
		reason := attempt(tree.value)
		if reason == "" {
			continue
		}
		// Shrinking: the first smaller value that still fails becomes the
		// one to shrink, until none does.
		steps := 0
		for steps < 1000 {
			smaller := false
			for _, candidate := range tree.shrinks() {
				steps++
				if why := attempt(candidate.value); why != "" {
					tree, reason, smaller = candidate, why, true
					break
				}
				if steps >= 1000 {
					break
				}
			}
			if !smaller {
				break
			}
		}
		failAt(ctx, where, fmt.Sprintf("property failed after %d of %d runs; smallest failing value, after %d shrinking steps:\n%s\n%s", run+1, runs, steps, inspectValue(tree.value), reason))
	}
}

// ---- Files ----

// fileHandle is a file opened with File.open. It belongs to the process
// that opened it and is closed when that process ends.
type fileHandle struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	file   *os.File
	reader *bufio.Reader
	closed bool
}

func fileOpen(ctx *actor.Context, path string, modes L[actor.Atom], where string) Tresult[*fileHandle, string] {
	flags, write := 0, false
	read := len(modes.S) == 0
	for _, mode := range fwd(modes) {
		switch mode {
		case "read":
			read = true
		case "write":
			write, flags = true, flags|os.O_CREATE|os.O_TRUNC
		case "append":
			write, flags = true, flags|os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND
		case "exclusive":
			flags |= os.O_CREATE | os.O_EXCL
		case "utf8", "binary":
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "File.open takes :read, :write, :append, :exclusive, :utf8 and :binary")
		}
	}
	switch {
	case read && write:
		flags |= os.O_RDWR
		flags &^= os.O_TRUNC
	case write:
		flags |= os.O_WRONLY
	default:
		flags |= os.O_RDONLY
	}
	file, err := os.OpenFile(path, flags, 0o644)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*fileHandle](fileProblem(err))
	}
	h := &fileHandle{file: file, reader: bufio.NewReader(file)}
	actor.OnEnd(ctx, func() { h.close() })
	return okOf(h)
}

// fileOpenWith opens the file, gives it to the function and closes it.
func fileOpenWith[A any](ctx *actor.Context, path string, modes L[actor.Atom], f func(*actor.Context, *fileHandle) A, where string) Tresult[A, string] {
	opened := fileOpen(ctx, path, modes, where)
	h, ok := opened.(Tresult_c0[*fileHandle, string])
	if !ok {
		return errOf[A](errorOf[*fileHandle, string](opened))
	}
	defer h.F0.close()
	return okOf(f(ctx, h.F0))
}

// fileProblem is the reason Elixir gives for a failed file operation.
func fileProblem(err error) string {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist):
		return "enoent"
	case errors.Is(err, os.ErrPermission):
		return "eacces"
	case errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist):
		return "eexist"
	}
	return err.Error()
}

func (h *fileHandle) close() bool {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	if h.closed {
		return false
	}
	h.closed = true
	h.file.Close()
	return true
}

func fileClose(h *fileHandle) bool { return h.close() }

// streamIntoFile writes each element to a file as the stream is consumed,
// passing the elements on; the transform, when given, makes the text.
func streamIntoFile(ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[string], h *fileHandle, where string) iter.Seq[string] {
	return func(yield func(string) bool) {
		for text := range s {
			if result := fileHandleWrite(ctx, h, text); !isOk[struct{}, string](result) {
				failAt(ctx, where, "Stream.into: "+errorOf[struct{}, string](result))
			}
			if !yield(text) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func streamIntoFileWith[A any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], h *fileHandle, f func(*actor.Context, A) string, where string) iter.Seq[A] {
	return func(yield func(A) bool) {
		for x := range s {
			if result := fileHandleWrite(ctx, h, f(ctx, x)); !isOk[struct{}, string](result) {
				failAt(ctx, where, "Stream.into: "+errorOf[struct{}, string](result))
			}
			if !yield(x) {
				return
			}
		}
	}
}

func fileHandleWrite(ctx *actor.Context, h *fileHandle, text string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	if h.closed {
		return errOf[struct{}]("closed")
	}
	if _, err := h.file.WriteString(text); err != nil {
		return errOf[struct{}](fileProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

func fileHandlePuts(ctx *actor.Context, h *fileHandle, text string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	return fileHandleWrite(ctx, h, text+"\n")
}

// fileHandleRead reads :line (with its newline) or :eof (the rest); none at
// the end of the file.
func fileHandleRead(ctx *actor.Context, h *fileHandle, how actor.Atom, where string) Toption[string] {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	if h.closed {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the file is closed")
	}
	switch how {
	case "line":
		line, err := h.reader.ReadString('\n')
		if line == "" && err != nil {
			return noneOf[string]()
		}
		return someOf(line)
	case "eof", "all":
		data, _ := io.ReadAll(h.reader)
		if len(data) == 0 {
			return noneOf[string]()
		}
		return someOf(string(data))
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, "IO.read takes :line, :eof or a number")
	return noneOf[string]()
}

// fileHandleReadCount reads up to count characters, or for binread bytes.
func fileHandleReadCount(ctx *actor.Context, h *fileHandle, count int64, where string) Toption[string] {
	return fileHandleTake(ctx, h, count, false, where)
}

func fileHandleBinread(ctx *actor.Context, h *fileHandle, count int64, where string) Toption[string] {
	return fileHandleTake(ctx, h, count, true, where)
}

func fileHandleTake(ctx *actor.Context, h *fileHandle, count int64, bytes bool, where string) Toption[string] {
	if count < 0 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the count must not be negative")
	}
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	if h.closed {
		failAt(ctx, where, "the file is closed")
	}
	var out []byte
	if bytes {
		out = make([]byte, count)
		n, _ := io.ReadFull(h.reader, out)
		out = out[:n]
	} else {
		for i := int64(0); i < count; i++ {
			r, _, err := h.reader.ReadRune()
			if err != nil {
				break
			}
			out = utf8.AppendRune(out, r)
		}
	}
	if len(out) == 0 && count > 0 {
		return noneOf[string]()
	}
	return someOf(string(out))
}

func fileStatOf(info os.FileInfo, path string) TFile_oStat {
	kind := actor.Atom("other")
	switch {
	case info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0:
		kind = "symlink"
	case info.IsDir():
		kind = "directory"
	case info.Mode().IsRegular():
		kind = "regular"
	}
	access := actor.Atom("none")
	readable := fileAccessible(path, os.O_RDONLY, info.IsDir())
	writable := fileAccessible(path, os.O_WRONLY, info.IsDir())
	switch {
	case readable && writable:
		access = "read_write"
	case readable:
		access = "read"
	case writable:
		access = "write"
	}
	return TFile_oStat{info.Size(), kind, access, info.ModTime().Unix(), int64(info.Mode().Perm())}
}

// fileAccessible tries to open a path as the program would.
func fileAccessible(path string, flag int, dir bool) bool {
	if dir {
		if flag == os.O_RDONLY {
			_, err := os.ReadDir(path)
			return err == nil
		}
		info, err := os.Stat(path)
		return err == nil && info.Mode().Perm()&0o222 != 0
	}
	f, err := os.OpenFile(path, flag, 0)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	f.Close()
	return true
}

func fileStat(path string) Tresult[TFile_oStat, string] {
	info, err := os.Stat(path)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[TFile_oStat](fileProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(fileStatOf(info, path))
}

func fileLstat(path string) Tresult[TFile_oStat, string] {
	info, err := os.Lstat(path)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[TFile_oStat](fileProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(fileStatOf(info, path))
}

func fileDone(err error) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[struct{}](fileProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

func fileLn(existing, link string) Tresult[struct{}, string] { return fileDone(os.Link(existing, link)) }
func fileLnS(existing, link string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	return fileDone(os.Symlink(existing, link))
}
func fileChmod(path string, mode int64) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	return fileDone(os.Chmod(path, os.FileMode(mode)))
}

// fileCpR copies a file or a directory and everything in it, and returns the
// paths it wrote.
func fileCpR(source, destination string) Tresult[L[string], string] {
	var written []string
	err := filepath.WalkDir(source, func(path string, entry os.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rel, _ := filepath.Rel(source, path)
		target := filepath.Join(destination, rel)
		info, err := entry.Info()
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		switch {
		case entry.IsDir():
			err = os.MkdirAll(target, info.Mode().Perm()|0o700)
		case info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0:
			link, linkErr := os.Readlink(path)
			if linkErr != nil {
				return linkErr
			}
			os.Remove(target)
			err = os.Symlink(link, target)
		default:
			data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path)
			if readErr != nil {
				return readErr
			}
			err = os.WriteFile(target, data, info.Mode().Perm())
		}
		if err == nil {
			written = append(written, target)
		}
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[L[string]](fileProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(mk(written))
}

// fileWriteModes writes with modes: :append adds to the end, :exclusive
// fails when the file exists.
func fileWriteModes(ctx *actor.Context, path, content string, modes L[actor.Atom], where string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	flags := os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREATE | os.O_TRUNC
	for _, mode := range fwd(modes) {
		switch mode {
		case "append":
			flags = flags&^os.O_TRUNC | os.O_APPEND
		case "exclusive":
			flags |= os.O_EXCL
		case "write", "utf8", "binary":
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "File.write takes the modes :append, :exclusive, :write, :utf8 and :binary")
		}
	}
	f, err := os.OpenFile(path, flags, 0o644)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[struct{}](fileProblem(err))
	}
	_, err = f.WriteString(content)
	if closeErr := f.Close(); err == nil {
		err = closeErr
	}
	return fileDone(err)
}

// ---- HTTP ----

func httpHeaders(h http.Header) L[pair[string, string]] {
	names := make([]string, 0, len(h))
	for name := range h {
		names = append(names, name)
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	var out []pair[string, string]
	for _, name := range names {
		for _, value := range h[name] {
			out = append(out, pair[string, string]{strings.ToLower(name), value})
		}
	}
	return mk(out)
}

// httpBodyLimit bounds the request and response bodies read into a string.
const httpBodyLimit = 64 << 20

func httpGet(ctx *actor.Context, address string) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	return httpRequestWithin(ctx, "GET", address, L[pair[string, string]]{}, "", 30000)
}

func httpGetWith(ctx *actor.Context, address string, headers L[pair[string, string]]) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	return httpRequestWithin(ctx, "GET", address, headers, "", 30000)
}

func httpPost(ctx *actor.Context, address, body string, headers L[pair[string, string]]) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	return httpRequestWithin(ctx, "POST", address, headers, body, 30000)
}

func httpRequest(ctx *actor.Context, method, address string, headers L[pair[string, string]], body string) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	return httpRequestWithin(ctx, method, address, headers, body, 30000)
}

// httpClientFor is the client a request with these options uses: one that
// trusts the certificates in ca_file, or every certificate when insecure.
func httpClientFor(options httpOptions) (*http.Client, error) {
	if options.caFile == "" && !options.insecure {
		return http.DefaultClient, nil
	}
	config := &tls.Config{InsecureSkipVerify: options.insecure}
	if options.caFile != "" {
		pem, err := os.ReadFile(options.caFile)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		pool := x509.NewCertPool()
		if !pool.AppendCertsFromPEM(pem) {
			return nil, errors.New("no certificates in " + options.caFile)
		}
		config.RootCAs = pool
	}
	transport := http.DefaultTransport.(*http.Transport).Clone()
	transport.TLSClientConfig = config
	return &http.Client{Transport: transport}, nil
}

// httpOptions are a client request's options.
type httpOptions struct {
	caFile   string
	insecure bool
	ms       int64
	stream   bool
}

func httpRequestOptions(ctx *actor.Context, method, address string, headers L[pair[string, string]], body string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, string]], where string) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	chosen := httpOptions{ms: 30000}
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		switch option.E0 {
		case "ca_file":
			chosen.caFile = option.E1
		case "insecure":
			chosen.insecure = option.E1 == "true"
		case "timeout":
			ms, err := strconv.ParseInt(option.E1, 10, 64)
			if err != nil {
				failAt(ctx, where, "HTTP.request: timeout: is a number of milliseconds")
			}
			chosen.ms = ms
		case "stream":
			chosen.stream = option.E1 == "true"
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "HTTP.request: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; the options are ca_file:, insecure:, timeout: and stream:")
		}
	}
	return httpDo(ctx, method, address, headers, body, chosen)
}

func httpStream(ctx *actor.Context, method, address string, headers L[pair[string, string]], body string) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	return httpDo(ctx, method, address, headers, body, httpOptions{ms: -1, stream: true})
}

func httpRequestWithin(ctx *actor.Context, method, address string, headers L[pair[string, string]], body string, ms int64) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	return httpDo(ctx, method, address, headers, body, httpOptions{ms: ms})
}

func httpDo(ctx *actor.Context, method, address string, headers L[pair[string, string]], body string, options httpOptions) Tresult[THTTP_oResponse, string] {
	actor.Check(ctx)
	ms := options.ms
	client, err := httpClientFor(options)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[THTTP_oResponse](err.Error())
	}
	requestContext, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	// The deadline covers the whole response, except that a streamed body,
	// read later, has none once its head has come.
	var timedOut atomic.Bool
	stopDeadline := func() bool { return false }
	if ms >= 0 {
		deadline := time.AfterFunc(time.Duration(ms)*time.Millisecond, func() {
			timedOut.Store(true)
			cancel()
		})
		stopDeadline = deadline.Stop
	}
	defer stopDeadline()
	finished := make(chan struct{})
	streaming := false
	defer func() {
		// A streamed body is read later, so its request stays open.
		if !streaming {
			cancel()
			close(finished)
		}
	}()
	go func() {
		select {
		case <-actor.Stopping(ctx):
			cancel()
		case <-finished:
		}
	}()
	request, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(requestContext, strings.ToUpper(method), address, strings.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[THTTP_oResponse](err.Error())
	}
	for _, header := range fwd(headers) {
		request.Header.Add(header.E0, header.E1)
	}
	response, err := client.Do(request)
	if err != nil {
		actor.Check(ctx)
		if timedOut.Load() || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
			return errOf[THTTP_oResponse]("timeout")
		}
		return errOf[THTTP_oResponse](err.Error())
	}
	if options.stream {
		stopDeadline()
		streaming = true
		chunks := func(yield func(string) bool) {
			defer func() {
				response.Body.Close()
				cancel()
				select {
				case <-finished:
				default:
					close(finished)
				}
			}()
			buffer := make([]byte, 32<<10)
			for {
				actor.Check(ctx)
				n, err := response.Body.Read(buffer)
				if n > 0 && !yield(string(buffer[:n])) {
					return
				}
				if err != nil {
					return
				}
			}
		}
		return okOf(THTTP_oResponse{int64(response.StatusCode), httpHeaders(response.Header), "", someOf[iter.Seq[string]](chunks)})
	}
	defer response.Body.Close()
	data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(response.Body, httpBodyLimit))
	if err != nil {
		actor.Check(ctx)
		if timedOut.Load() {
			return errOf[THTTP_oResponse]("timeout")
		}
		return errOf[THTTP_oResponse](err.Error())
	}
	return okOf(THTTP_oResponse{int64(response.StatusCode), httpHeaders(response.Header), string(data), noneOf[iter.Seq[string]]()})
}

// httpServer is a listening server. Its owner is the process that serves:
// stopping it closes the listener and the connections.
type httpServer struct {
	owner   *actor.PID[struct{}]
	address string
	server  *http.Server
}

func httpServe(ctx *actor.Context, address string, handle func(*actor.Context, THTTP_oRequest) THTTP_oResponse, where string) Tresult[*httpServer, string] {
	return httpServeWith(ctx, address, handle, L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]]{}, where)
}

// httpServeWith serves with limits given in milliseconds and bytes.
func httpServeWith(ctx *actor.Context, address string, handle func(*actor.Context, THTTP_oRequest) THTTP_oResponse, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) Tresult[*httpServer, string] {
	return httpListen(ctx, address, handle, options, nil, where)
}

// httpServeTLS serves HTTPS with a certificate and key read from files.
func httpServeTLS(ctx *actor.Context, address string, handle func(*actor.Context, THTTP_oRequest) THTTP_oResponse, files L[pair[actor.Atom, string]], where string) Tresult[*httpServer, string] {
	return httpServeTLSWith(ctx, address, handle, files, L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]]{}, where)
}

func httpServeTLSWith(ctx *actor.Context, address string, handle func(*actor.Context, THTTP_oRequest) THTTP_oResponse, files L[pair[actor.Atom, string]], options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) Tresult[*httpServer, string] {
	certificate, key := "", ""
	for _, file := range fwd(files) {
		switch file.E0 {
		case "certfile":
			certificate = file.E1
		case "keyfile":
			key = file.E1
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "HTTP.serve_tls: unknown option :"+string(file.E0)+"; it takes certfile: and keyfile:")
		}
	}
	pair, err := tls.LoadX509KeyPair(certificate, key)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*httpServer](err.Error())
	}
	return httpListen(ctx, address, handle, options, &tls.Config{Certificates: []tls.Certificate{pair}}, where)
}

func httpListen(ctx *actor.Context, address string, handle func(*actor.Context, THTTP_oRequest) THTTP_oResponse, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], secure *tls.Config, where string) Tresult[*httpServer, string] {
	// What the server would log, such as a client's failed handshake, is
	// not the program's to print.
	server := &http.Server{ErrorLog: log.New(io.Discard, "", 0)}
	limit := int64(httpBodyLimit)
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		if option.E1 < 0 {
			failAt(ctx, where, "HTTP.serve: "+string(option.E0)+": cannot be negative")
		}
		duration := time.Duration(option.E1) * time.Millisecond
		switch option.E0 {
		case "read_timeout":
			server.ReadTimeout = duration
		case "write_timeout":
			server.WriteTimeout = duration
		case "idle_timeout":
			server.IdleTimeout = duration
		case "header_timeout":
			server.ReadHeaderTimeout = duration
		case "max_header_bytes":
			server.MaxHeaderBytes = int(option.E1)
		case "max_body_bytes":
			limit = option.E1
		case "keep_alive":
			server.SetKeepAlivesEnabled(option.E1 != 0)
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "HTTP.serve: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; the options are read_timeout:, write_timeout:, idle_timeout:, header_timeout:, max_header_bytes:, max_body_bytes: and keep_alive:")
		}
	}
	listener, err := net.Listen("tcp", address)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*httpServer](err.Error())
	}
	if secure != nil {
		listener = tls.NewListener(listener, secure)
	}
	ready := make(chan *actor.Context, 1)
	owner := actor.Spawn(ctx, func(c *actor.Context, _ *actor.PID[struct{}]) int64 {
		ready <- c
		<-actor.Stopping(c)
		return 0
	}, 1, where)
	serving := <-ready
	server.Handler = http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		httpAnswer(serving, handle, w, r, limit, where)
	})
	go server.Serve(listener)
	go func() {
		<-actor.Stopping(serving)
		server.Close()
	}()
	return okOf(&httpServer{owner: owner, address: listener.Addr().String(), server: server})
}

// httpAnswer runs the handler for one request in a process of its own. A
// handler that fails answers 500.
func httpAnswer(serving *actor.Context, handle func(*actor.Context, THTTP_oRequest) THTTP_oResponse, w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, limit int64, where string) {
	data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(r.Body, limit+1))
	if err != nil {
		http.Error(w, "bad request", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}
	if int64(len(data)) > limit {
		http.Error(w, "request body too large", http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge)
		return
	}
	// Go keeps Host out of the header map; a handler sees it as a header,
	// as it arrived.
	headers := r.Header.Clone()
	if r.Host != "" {
		headers.Set("Host", r.Host)
	}
	request := THTTP_oRequest{r.Method, r.URL.Path, r.URL.RawQuery, httpHeaders(headers), string(data)}
	answer := make(chan THTTP_oResponse, 1)
	streaming := make(chan struct{})
	exchange := &httpExchange{w: w, r: r, upgraded: make(chan struct{})}
	var worker *actor.PID[struct{}]
	func() {
		defer func() {
			if recover() != nil {
				worker = nil
			}
		}()
		worker = actor.Spawn(serving, func(c *actor.Context, _ *actor.PID[struct{}]) int64 {
			httpExchanges.Store(c, exchange)
			defer httpExchanges.Delete(c)
			response := handle(c, request)
			if exchange.taken() {
				// The connection became a WebSocket; the response is not sent.
				return 0
			}
			chunks, streamed := response.F3.(Toption_c1[iter.Seq[string]])
			if !streamed {
				answer <- response
				return 0
			}
			// A streamed body is read here, in the process that made it,
			// and each chunk is sent as it comes.
			httpHead(w, response)
			flusher, _ := w.(http.Flusher)
			for chunk := range chunks.F0 {
				if _, err := io.WriteString(w, chunk); err != nil {
					break
				}
				if flusher != nil {
					flusher.Flush()
				}
			}
			close(streaming)
			return 0
		}, 1, where)
	}()
	if worker == nil {
		http.Error(w, "server stopping", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}
	select {
	case response := <-answer:
		httpHead(w, response)
		io.WriteString(w, response.F2)
	case <-streaming:
	case <-exchange.upgraded:
	case <-actor.Ended(worker):
		if exchange.taken() {
			return
		}
		select {
		case response := <-answer:
			httpHead(w, response)
			io.WriteString(w, response.F2)
		case <-streaming:
		default:
			// A worker that failed while streaming has written its head;
			// the client sees the body end early.
			http.Error(w, "internal server error", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		}
	case <-r.Context().Done():
		// The worker may be writing a stream: it is stopped, and the
		// writer is not given back until it has ended.
		actor.Stop(serving, worker, where)
		<-actor.Ended(worker)
	}
}

// httpExchange is the request a handler process is answering, kept so that
// WebSocket.upgrade can take over its connection.
type httpExchange struct {
	w        http.ResponseWriter
	r        *http.Request
	mu       sync.Mutex
	hijacked bool
	upgraded chan struct{}
}

// httpExchanges maps each handler process to its exchange.
var httpExchanges sync.Map

func (e *httpExchange) taken() bool {
	e.mu.Lock()
	defer e.mu.Unlock()
	return e.hijacked
}

// httpHead writes a response's status and headers.
func httpHead(w http.ResponseWriter, response THTTP_oResponse) {
	for _, header := range fwd(response.F1) {
		w.Header().Add(header.E0, header.E1)
	}
	status := int(response.F0)
	if status < 100 || status > 999 {
		status = http.StatusInternalServerError
	}
	w.WriteHeader(status)
}

func httpChunked(status int64, headers L[pair[string, string]], chunks iter.Seq[string]) THTTP_oResponse {
	return THTTP_oResponse{status, headers, "", someOf(chunks)}
}

// httpRead reads a whole response, its body and any chunks.
func httpRead(ctx *actor.Context, response THTTP_oResponse) string {
	chunks, streamed := response.F3.(Toption_c1[iter.Seq[string]])
	if !streamed {
		return response.F2
	}
	var body strings.Builder
	body.WriteString(response.F2)
	for chunk := range chunks.F0 {
		body.WriteString(chunk)
	}
	return body.String()
}

// httpMatch matches a request against a method and a path pattern, whose
// segments may be :name, taking one segment, or *name, taking the rest.
func httpMatch(request THTTP_oRequest, method, pattern string) Toption[actor.Map[string, string]] {
	if method != "*" && !strings.EqualFold(method, request.F0) {
		return noneOf[actor.Map[string, string]]()
	}
	params, ok := httpPath(pattern, request.F1)
	if !ok {
		return noneOf[actor.Map[string, string]]()
	}
	return someOf(params)
}

func httpPath(pattern, path string) (actor.Map[string, string], bool) {
	params := actor.Map[string, string]{}
	want := strings.Split(strings.Trim(pattern, "/"), "/")
	got := strings.Split(strings.Trim(path, "/"), "/")
	for i, segment := range want {
		switch {
		case strings.HasPrefix(segment, "*"):
			params = actor.MapPut(params, segment[1:], strings.Join(got[min(i, len(got)):], "/"))
			return params, true
		case i >= len(got):
			return params, false
		case strings.HasPrefix(segment, ":"):
			text, err := url.PathUnescape(got[i])
			if err != nil {
				return params, false
			}
			params = actor.MapPut(params, segment[1:], text)
		case segment != got[i]:
			return params, false
		}
	}
	return params, len(want) == len(got)
}

// httpRoute answers with the first route whose method and pattern match:
// 404 when no pattern matches the path, and 405 when only other methods do.
func httpRoute(ctx *actor.Context, request THTTP_oRequest, routes L[triple[string, string, func(*actor.Context, THTTP_oRequest, actor.Map[string, string]) THTTP_oResponse]]) THTTP_oResponse {
	var allowed []string
	for _, route := range fwd(routes) {
		params, ok := httpPath(route.E1, request.F1)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if route.E0 == "*" || strings.EqualFold(route.E0, request.F0) {
			return route.E2(ctx, request, params)
		}
		allowed = append(allowed, strings.ToUpper(route.E0))
	}
	if len(allowed) > 0 {
		response := httpText(405, "method not allowed")
		response.F1 = mk(append(fwd(response.F1), pair[string, string]{"allow", strings.Join(allowed, ", ")}))
		return response
	}
	return httpText(404, "not found")
}

func httpAddress(s *httpServer) string { return s.address }

func httpStop(ctx *actor.Context, s *httpServer, where string) bool {
	// The listener is closed before this returns, so no request made
	// afterwards is answered.
	s.server.Close()
	stopped := actor.Stop(ctx, s.owner, where)
	actor.Await(ctx, s.owner, 5000, where)
	return stopped
}

func httpAwait(ctx *actor.Context, s *httpServer, ms int64, where string) bool {
	return actor.Await(ctx, s.owner, ms, where).E0
}

func httpText(status int64, body string) THTTP_oResponse {
	return THTTP_oResponse{status, mk([]pair[string, string]{{"content-type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"}}), body, noneOf[iter.Seq[string]]()}
}

func httpJSON(status int64, body TJSON) THTTP_oResponse {
	return THTTP_oResponse{status, mk([]pair[string, string]{{"content-type", "application/json"}}), jsonEncode(body), noneOf[iter.Seq[string]]()}
}

func httpHeader(headers L[pair[string, string]], name string) Toption[string] {
	name = strings.ToLower(name)
	for _, header := range fwd(headers) {
		if strings.ToLower(header.E0) == name {
			return someOf(header.E1)
		}
	}
	return noneOf[string]()
}

// ---- WebSocket ----

// wsGUID is the constant RFC 6455 joins to a client's key to prove that a
// server understood the handshake.
const wsGUID = "258EAFA5-E914-47DA-95CA-C5AB0DC85B11"

// wsDefaultLimit bounds a message unless max_message_bytes: says otherwise.
const wsDefaultLimit = 16 << 20

// wsWriteTimeout is how long a frame may take to write before the connection
// is given up.
const wsWriteTimeout = 10 * time.Second

// wsConn is one end of a WebSocket. A goroutine reads frames from the start,
// answering pings and the closing handshake itself, and queues whole messages
// in events; recv and an active socket take them from there, so a read that
// times out never loses part of a frame.
type wsConn struct {
	conn   net.Conn
	reader *bufio.Reader
	client bool
	limit  int64
	writes sync.Mutex
	events chan TWebSocket_oEvent
	// done is closed when the reader has ended, quit when the connection has
	// been closed from this end.
	done chan struct{}
	quit chan struct{}
	mu   sync.Mutex
	// closing is set once a close frame has been sent, closed once the
	// connection has been closed from this end.
	closing bool
	closed  bool
	owner   *actor.Context
	// receiver, active and pumping work as they do for TCP sockets.
	receiver any
	active   int64
	pumping  bool
}

// wsFailure is a broken rule of the protocol, with the close code that
// reports it.
type wsFailure struct {
	code int
	text string
}

func (f wsFailure) Error() string { return f.text }

func newWebSocket(ctx *actor.Context, conn net.Conn, reader *bufio.Reader, client bool, limit int64) *wsConn {
	ws := &wsConn{conn: conn, reader: reader, client: client, limit: limit, events: make(chan TWebSocket_oEvent, 16), done: make(chan struct{}), quit: make(chan struct{})}
	ws.own(ctx)
	go ws.read()
	return ws
}

// own makes a process the socket's owner: when it ends, the socket closes
// with 1001, going away.
func (ws *wsConn) own(owner *actor.Context) {
	ws.mu.Lock()
	ws.owner = owner
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	actor.OnEnd(owner, func() {
		ws.mu.Lock()
		current := ws.owner == owner
		ws.mu.Unlock()
		if current {
			ws.shut(1001, "", false)
		}
	})
}

// wsAccept is the Sec-WebSocket-Accept value for a key.
func wsAccept(key string) string {
	sum := sha1.Sum([]byte(key + wsGUID))
	return base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// wsHeaderHas reports whether a comma-separated header holds a token.
func wsHeaderHas(header http.Header, name, token string) bool {
	for _, value := range header.Values(name) {
		for _, part := range strings.Split(value, ",") {
			if strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(part), token) {
				return true
			}
		}
	}
	return false
}

func wsUpgrade(ctx *actor.Context, request THTTP_oRequest, where string) Tresult[*wsConn, string] {
	return wsUpgradeWith(ctx, request, L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]]{}, where)
}

// wsUpgradeWith answers the request its process is handling with the
// WebSocket handshake and takes over the connection.
func wsUpgradeWith(ctx *actor.Context, request THTTP_oRequest, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) Tresult[*wsConn, string] {
	limit := int64(wsDefaultLimit)
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		switch {
		case option.E0 == "max_message_bytes" && option.E1 > 0:
			limit = option.E1
		case option.E0 == "max_message_bytes":
			failAt(ctx, where, "WebSocket.upgrade: max_message_bytes: must be positive")
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "WebSocket.upgrade: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; the option is max_message_bytes:")
		}
	}
	found, ok := httpExchanges.Load(ctx)
	if !ok {
		return errOf[*wsConn]("WebSocket.upgrade must be called by the process handling the request")
	}
	exchange := found.(*httpExchange)
	r := exchange.r
	key := r.Header.Get("Sec-WebSocket-Key")
	decoded, _ := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(key)
	switch {
	case r.Method != http.MethodGet:
		return errOf[*wsConn]("not a WebSocket request: the method is " + r.Method)
	case !wsHeaderHas(r.Header, "Connection", "upgrade") || !wsHeaderHas(r.Header, "Upgrade", "websocket"):
		return errOf[*wsConn]("not a WebSocket request: no upgrade to websocket")
	case r.Header.Get("Sec-WebSocket-Version") != "13":
		return errOf[*wsConn]("not a WebSocket request: the version must be 13")
	case len(decoded) != 16:
		return errOf[*wsConn]("not a WebSocket request: the key is missing or malformed")
	}
	exchange.mu.Lock()
	defer exchange.mu.Unlock()
	if exchange.hijacked {
		return errOf[*wsConn]("the request has already been upgraded")
	}
	hijacker, ok := exchange.w.(http.Hijacker)
	if !ok {
		return errOf[*wsConn]("the connection cannot be taken over")
	}
	conn, buffered, err := hijacker.Hijack()
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*wsConn](err.Error())
	}
	exchange.hijacked = true
	close(exchange.upgraded)
	// The server's own deadlines no longer apply.
	conn.SetDeadline(time.Time{})
	conn.SetWriteDeadline(time.Now().Add(wsWriteTimeout))
	_, err = io.WriteString(conn, "HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Accept: "+wsAccept(key)+"\r\n\r\n")
	if err != nil {
		conn.Close()
		return errOf[*wsConn](netProblem(err))
	}
	conn.SetWriteDeadline(time.Time{})
	return okOf(newWebSocket(ctx, conn, buffered.Reader, false, limit))
}

func wsConnect(ctx *actor.Context, address string, ms int64, where string) Tresult[*wsConn, string] {
	return wsConnectWith(ctx, address, ms, L[pair[actor.Atom, string]]{}, where)
}

// wsConnectWith opens a WebSocket to a ws:// or wss:// address. The options
// are ca_file:, insecure: "true" and origin:, as text.
func wsConnectWith(ctx *actor.Context, address string, ms int64, options L[pair[actor.Atom, string]], where string) Tresult[*wsConn, string] {
	caFile, insecure, origin := "", false, ""
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		switch option.E0 {
		case "ca_file":
			caFile = option.E1
		case "insecure":
			insecure = option.E1 == "true"
		case "origin":
			origin = option.E1
		default:
			failAt(ctx, where, "WebSocket.connect: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; the options are ca_file:, insecure: and origin:")
		}
	}
	target, err := url.Parse(address)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*wsConn](err.Error())
	}
	secure := false
	switch target.Scheme {
	case "ws":
	case "wss":
		secure = true
	default:
		return errOf[*wsConn]("a WebSocket address starts with ws:// or wss://")
	}
	host := target.Host
	if target.Port() == "" {
		host = net.JoinHostPort(target.Hostname(), map[bool]string{false: "80", true: "443"}[secure])
	}
	until, bounded := netDeadline(ms)
	conn, err := netDial(ctx, host, ms)
	if err != nil {
		return errOf[*wsConn](netProblem(err))
	}
	if bounded {
		conn.SetDeadline(until)
	}
	if secure {
		config := &tls.Config{ServerName: target.Hostname(), InsecureSkipVerify: insecure}
		if caFile != "" {
			pem, err := os.ReadFile(caFile)
			if err != nil {
				conn.Close()
				return errOf[*wsConn](err.Error())
			}
			pool := x509.NewCertPool()
			if !pool.AppendCertsFromPEM(pem) {
				conn.Close()
				return errOf[*wsConn]("no certificates in " + caFile)
			}
			config.RootCAs = pool
		}
		encrypted := tls.Client(conn, config)
		if err := encrypted.Handshake(); err != nil {
			conn.Close()
			return errOf[*wsConn](netProblem(err))
		}
		conn = encrypted
	}
	nonce := make([]byte, 16)
	crand.Read(nonce)
	key := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(nonce)
	path := target.RequestURI()
	request := "GET " + path + " HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: " + target.Host + "\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Key: " + key + "\r\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n"
	if origin != "" {
		request += "Origin: " + origin + "\r\n"
	}
	reader := bufio.NewReader(conn)
	if _, err := io.WriteString(conn, request+"\r\n"); err != nil {
		conn.Close()
		return errOf[*wsConn](netProblem(err))
	}
	response, err := http.ReadResponse(reader, nil)
	if err != nil {
		conn.Close()
		return errOf[*wsConn](netProblem(err))
	}
	if response.StatusCode != http.StatusSwitchingProtocols || !wsHeaderHas(response.Header, "Upgrade", "websocket") || response.Header.Get("Sec-WebSocket-Accept") != wsAccept(key) {
		conn.Close()
		return errOf[*wsConn]("the server did not accept the WebSocket: " + response.Status)
	}
	conn.SetDeadline(time.Time{})
	return okOf(newWebSocket(ctx, conn, reader, true, wsDefaultLimit))
}

// frame reads one frame, unmasking its payload.
func (ws *wsConn) frame() (bool, byte, []byte, error) {
	var head [2]byte
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(ws.reader, head[:]); err != nil {
		return false, 0, nil, err
	}
	fin, op := head[0]&0x80 != 0, head[0]&0x0f
	if head[0]&0x70 != 0 {
		return false, 0, nil, wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: reserved bits are set"}
	}
	masked := head[1]&0x80 != 0
	if masked == ws.client {
		if ws.client {
			return false, 0, nil, wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: the server masked a frame"}
		}
		return false, 0, nil, wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: the client sent an unmasked frame"}
	}
	size := uint64(head[1] & 0x7f)
	switch size {
	case 126, 127:
		extended := make([]byte, map[uint64]int{126: 2, 127: 8}[size])
		if _, err := io.ReadFull(ws.reader, extended); err != nil {
			return false, 0, nil, err
		}
		size = 0
		for _, b := range extended {
			size = size<<8 | uint64(b)
		}
	}
	if op >= 8 && (!fin || size > 125) {
		return false, 0, nil, wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: a control frame is fragmented or too long"}
	}
	if size > uint64(ws.limit) {
		return false, 0, nil, wsFailure{1009, "message too large"}
	}
	var mask [4]byte
	if masked {
		if _, err := io.ReadFull(ws.reader, mask[:]); err != nil {
			return false, 0, nil, err
		}
	}
	payload := make([]byte, size)
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(ws.reader, payload); err != nil {
		return false, 0, nil, err
	}
	if masked {
		for i := range payload {
			payload[i] ^= mask[i%4]
		}
	}
	return fin, op, payload, nil
}

// read takes frames until the connection ends, putting whole messages in
// events and ending with a closed or error event.
func (ws *wsConn) read() {
	var message []byte
	var kind byte
	for {
		fin, op, payload, err := ws.frame()
		if err == nil {
			switch op {
			case 0x8:
				ws.peerClosed(payload)
				return
			case 0x9:
				ws.write(0xA, payload)
				continue
			case 0xA:
				continue
			case 0x1, 0x2:
				if kind != 0 {
					err = wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: a message began before the last one ended"}
				}
				kind, message = op, payload
			case 0x0:
				if kind == 0 {
					err = wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: a continuation frame without a message"}
				}
				message = append(message, payload...)
			default:
				err = wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: unknown opcode " + strconv.Itoa(int(op))}
			}
		}
		if err == nil && int64(len(message)) > ws.limit {
			err = wsFailure{1009, "message too large"}
		}
		if err == nil && fin && kind == 0x1 && !utf8.Valid(message) {
			err = wsFailure{1007, "a text message is not valid UTF-8"}
		}
		if err != nil {
			ws.broken(err)
			return
		}
		if !fin {
			continue
		}
		event := TWebSocket_oEvent(TWebSocket_oEvent_c0{string(message)})
		if kind == 0x2 {
			event = TWebSocket_oEvent(TWebSocket_oEvent_c1{string(message)})
		}
		kind, message = 0, nil
		select {
		case ws.events <- event:
		case <-ws.quit:
			ws.finish(nil)
			return
		}
	}
}

// peerClosed answers the other end's close frame and ends the reader with
// its code and reason.
func (ws *wsConn) peerClosed(payload []byte) {
	code, reason := 1005, ""
	switch {
	case len(payload) == 1:
		ws.broken(wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: a close frame with a one-byte body"})
		return
	case len(payload) >= 2:
		code, reason = int(binary.BigEndian.Uint16(payload)), string(payload[2:])
		valid := code >= 1000 && code < 5000 && code != 1004 && code != 1005 && code != 1006 && (code < 1012 || code >= 3000)
		if !valid || !utf8.ValidString(reason) {
			ws.broken(wsFailure{1002, "protocol error: an invalid close code or reason"})
			return
		}
	}
	ws.mu.Lock()
	answered := ws.closing
	ws.closing = true
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	if !answered {
		echo := []byte{}
		if code != 1005 {
			echo = binary.BigEndian.AppendUint16(echo, uint16(code))
		}
		ws.write(0x8, echo)
	}
	ws.conn.Close()
	ws.finish(TWebSocket_oEvent(TWebSocket_oEvent_c2{int64(code), reason}))
}

// broken ends the reader after a failure: a broken rule is reported to the
// other end with its close code, and the connection is dropped.
func (ws *wsConn) broken(err error) {
	var failure wsFailure
	if errors.As(err, &failure) {
		ws.mu.Lock()
		sending := !ws.closing
		ws.closing = true
		ws.mu.Unlock()
		if sending {
			ws.write(0x8, append(binary.BigEndian.AppendUint16(nil, uint16(failure.code)), failure.text...))
		}
		ws.conn.Close()
		ws.finish(TWebSocket_oEvent(TWebSocket_oEvent_c3{failure.text}))
		return
	}
	ws.conn.Close()
	ws.mu.Lock()
	local := ws.closed
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	if local || errors.Is(err, io.EOF) || errors.Is(err, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF) || errors.Is(err, net.ErrClosed) {
		// The connection ended without a closing handshake.
		ws.finish(TWebSocket_oEvent(TWebSocket_oEvent_c2{1006, ""}))
		return
	}
	ws.finish(TWebSocket_oEvent(TWebSocket_oEvent_c3{netProblem(err)}))
}

// finish queues the last event, if any, and ends the event stream.
func (ws *wsConn) finish(last TWebSocket_oEvent) {
	if last != nil {
		select {
		case ws.events <- last:
		case <-ws.quit:
		}
	}
	close(ws.events)
	close(ws.done)
}

// write sends one frame, masked when this end is the client.
func (ws *wsConn) write(op byte, payload []byte) error {
	header := []byte{0x80 | op}
	mask := byte(0)
	if ws.client {
		mask = 0x80
	}
	switch size := len(payload); {
	case size < 126:
		header = append(header, mask|byte(size))
	case size < 1<<16:
		header = binary.BigEndian.AppendUint16(append(header, mask|126), uint16(size))
	default:
		header = binary.BigEndian.AppendUint64(append(header, mask|127), uint64(size))
	}
	body := payload
	if ws.client {
		key := make([]byte, 4)
		crand.Read(key)
		header = append(header, key...)
		body = make([]byte, len(payload))
		for i, b := range payload {
			body[i] = b ^ key[i%4]
		}
	}
	ws.writes.Lock()
	defer ws.writes.Unlock()
	ws.conn.SetWriteDeadline(time.Now().Add(wsWriteTimeout))
	_, err := ws.conn.Write(append(header, body...))
	return err
}

func (ws *wsConn) send(ctx *actor.Context, op byte, data string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	actor.Check(ctx)
	ws.mu.Lock()
	closing := ws.closing
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	if closing {
		return errOf[struct{}]("closed")
	}
	if err := ws.write(op, []byte(data)); err != nil {
		ws.conn.Close()
		return errOf[struct{}](netProblem(err))
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

func wsSendText(ctx *actor.Context, ws *wsConn, text string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	return ws.send(ctx, 0x1, text)
}

func wsSendBinary(ctx *actor.Context, ws *wsConn, data string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	return ws.send(ctx, 0x2, data)
}

// shut starts the closing handshake and drops the connection, after waiting
// up to a second for the other end's answer when wait is set.
func (ws *wsConn) shut(code int, reason string, wait bool) bool {
	ws.mu.Lock()
	if ws.closed {
		ws.mu.Unlock()
		return false
	}
	sending := !ws.closing
	ws.closing = true
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	if sending {
		ws.write(0x8, append(binary.BigEndian.AppendUint16(nil, uint16(code)), reason...))
	}
	if wait {
		select {
		case <-ws.done:
		case <-time.After(time.Second):
		}
	}
	ws.mu.Lock()
	ws.closed = true
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	close(ws.quit)
	ws.conn.Close()
	return true
}

func wsClose(ws *wsConn) bool {
	return ws.shut(1000, "", true)
}

// wsCloseWith closes with a code a program may send, 1000 or 3000 to 4999,
// and a reason of at most 123 bytes.
func wsCloseWith(ctx *actor.Context, ws *wsConn, code int64, reason string, where string) bool {
	if code != 1000 && (code < 3000 || code > 4999) {
		failAt(ctx, where, "WebSocket.close: the code must be 1000 or 3000 to 4999, not "+strconv.FormatInt(code, 10))
	}
	if len(reason) > 123 || !utf8.ValidString(reason) {
		failAt(ctx, where, "WebSocket.close: the reason must be valid text of at most 123 bytes")
	}
	return ws.shut(int(code), reason, true)
}

// wsRecv waits up to ms milliseconds for the next event.
func wsRecv(ctx *actor.Context, ws *wsConn, ms int64) Tresult[TWebSocket_oEvent, string] {
	ws.mu.Lock()
	active := ws.active != 0 || ws.pumping
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	if active {
		return errOf[TWebSocket_oEvent]("the socket is active")
	}
	until, bounded := netDeadline(ms)
	for {
		actor.Check(ctx)
		slice := netSlice
		if bounded {
			if left := time.Until(until); left < slice {
				slice = max(left, 0)
			}
		}
		timer := time.NewTimer(slice)
		select {
		case event, ok := <-ws.events:
			timer.Stop()
			if !ok {
				return errOf[TWebSocket_oEvent]("closed")
			}
			return okOf(event)
		case <-timer.C:
			if bounded && !time.Now().Before(until) {
				return errOf[TWebSocket_oEvent]("timeout")
			}
		}
	}
}

func wsControllingProcess[A any](ctx *actor.Context, ws *wsConn, p *actor.PID[A]) bool {
	owner := actor.ContextOf(p)
	if owner == nil {
		return false
	}
	ws.own(owner)
	ws.mu.Lock()
	ws.receiver = p
	ws.mu.Unlock()
	return true
}

// wsSetopts sets active: true, :once or false.
func wsSetopts(ctx *actor.Context, ws *wsConn, options L[pair[actor.Atom, actor.Atom]], where string) Tresult[struct{}, string] {
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		count, valid := map[actor.Atom]int64{"true": -1, "once": 1, "false": 0}[option.E1]
		if option.E0 != "active" || !valid {
			failAt(ctx, where, "WebSocket.setopts: the option is active: true, :once or false")
		}
		ws.mu.Lock()
		ws.active = count
		if count == 0 || ws.pumping {
			ws.mu.Unlock()
			continue
		}
		var receiver *actor.PID[TWebSocket_oEvent]
		ok := false
		if ws.receiver != nil {
			receiver, ok = ws.receiver.(*actor.PID[TWebSocket_oEvent])
		} else if ws.owner != nil {
			receiver, ok = actor.MailboxOf[TWebSocket_oEvent](ws.owner)
		}
		if !ok {
			ws.active = 0
			ws.mu.Unlock()
			return errOf[struct{}]("the controlling process does not take WebSocket.Event messages")
		}
		ws.pumping = true
		ws.mu.Unlock()
		go ws.pump(receiver)
	}
	return okOf(struct{}{})
}

// pump sends events to the receiver while the socket is active.
func (ws *wsConn) pump(receiver *actor.PID[TWebSocket_oEvent]) {
	for {
		ws.mu.Lock()
		if ws.active == 0 {
			ws.pumping = false
			ws.mu.Unlock()
			return
		}
		ws.mu.Unlock()
		timer := time.NewTimer(netSlice)
		select {
		case event, ok := <-ws.events:
			timer.Stop()
			if !ok {
				ws.mu.Lock()
				ws.active, ws.pumping = 0, false
				ws.mu.Unlock()
				return
			}
			// The count is settled before the event is delivered, so that a
			// receiver given the last one can read passively at once.
			ws.mu.Lock()
			if ws.active > 0 {
				ws.active--
			}
			last := ws.active == 0
			if last {
				ws.pumping = false
			}
			ws.mu.Unlock()
			for !actor.Deliver(receiver, event, netSlice) {
				select {
				case <-actor.Ended(receiver):
					return
				default:
				}
			}
			if last {
				return
			}
		case <-timer.C:
		}
	}
}

func wsPeer(ws *wsConn) string {
	return ws.conn.RemoteAddr().String()
}

// ---- Crypto ----

func cryptoRandBytes(ctx *actor.Context, n int64, where string) string {
	if n < 0 || n > 1<<20 {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Crypto.strong_rand_bytes takes a count from 0 to 1048576")
	}
	out := make([]byte, n)
	crand.Read(out)
	return string(out)
}

// cryptoDigest is the hash function an algorithm names.
func cryptoDigest(ctx *actor.Context, algorithm actor.Atom, function, where string) func() hash.Hash {
	switch algorithm {
	case "md5":
		return md5.New
	case "sha1":
		return sha1.New
	case "sha224":
		return sha256.New224
	case "sha256":
		return sha256.New
	case "sha384":
		return sha512.New384
	case "sha512":
		return sha512.New
	}
	failAt(ctx, where, function+": the algorithms are :md5, :sha1, :sha224, :sha256, :sha384 and :sha512, not :"+string(algorithm))
	return nil
}

func cryptoHash(ctx *actor.Context, algorithm actor.Atom, data string, where string) string {
	h := cryptoDigest(ctx, algorithm, "Crypto.hash", where)()
	h.Write([]byte(data))
	return string(h.Sum(nil))
}

func cryptoHmac(ctx *actor.Context, algorithm actor.Atom, key, data string, where string) string {
	mac := hmac.New(cryptoDigest(ctx, algorithm, "Crypto.hmac", where), []byte(key))
	mac.Write([]byte(data))
	return string(mac.Sum(nil))
}

// cryptoSecureCompare compares in time that depends only on the lengths, so
// that checking a signature does not reveal how much of it was right.
func cryptoSecureCompare(a, b string) bool {
	return subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(a), []byte(b)) == 1
}

// ---- Markup ----

// markupEscape makes text safe inside HTML elements and quoted attribute
// values: &, <, >, " and ' become character references.
func markupEscape(text string) string { return html.EscapeString(text) }

func markupRaw(text string) TMarkup { return TMarkup{text} }

func markupString(m TMarkup) string { return m.F0 }

func markupOf(m TMarkup) TMarkup { return m }

func markupOfString(text string) TMarkup { return TMarkup{markupEscape(text)} }

func markupOfInteger(n int64) TMarkup { return TMarkup{strconv.FormatInt(n, 10)} }

func markupOfFloat(f float64) TMarkup { return TMarkup{floatString(f)} }

func markupOfAtom(a actor.Atom) TMarkup { return TMarkup{markupEscape(string(a))} }

// markupJoin puts pieces of markup one after another.
func markupJoin(pieces L[TMarkup]) TMarkup {
	var b strings.Builder
	for _, piece := range fwd(pieces) {
		b.WriteString(piece.F0)
	}
	return TMarkup{b.String()}
}

// ---- Split options ----

// splitParts reads [parts: n]: at most n pieces, the last holding the rest.
func splitParts(ctx *actor.Context, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) int {
	parts := -1
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		if option.E0 != "parts" {
			failAt(ctx, where, "String.split: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; it takes parts: n or trim: true")
		}
		if option.E1 < 1 {
			failAt(ctx, where, "String.split: parts: must be at least 1")
		}
		parts = int(option.E1)
	}
	return parts
}
