// splitTrim reads [trim: true]: empty pieces are left out.
func splitTrim(ctx *actor.Context, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) bool {
	trim := false
	for _, option := range fwd(options) {
		if option.E0 != "trim" {
			failAt(ctx, where, "String.split: unknown option :"+string(option.E0)+"; it takes parts: n or trim: true")
		}
		trim = option.E1
	}
	return trim
}

func withoutEmpty(pieces []string) L[string] {
	kept := make([]string, 0, len(pieces))
	for _, piece := range pieces {
		if piece != "" {
			kept = append(kept, piece)
		}
	}
	return mk(kept)
}

func strSplitParts(ctx *actor.Context, s, separator string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) L[string] {
	if separator == "" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "separator must not be empty")
	}
	return mk(strings.SplitN(s, separator, splitParts(ctx, options, where)))
}

func strSplitTrim(ctx *actor.Context, s, separator string, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) L[string] {
	if separator == "" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "separator must not be empty")
	}
	pieces := strings.Split(s, separator)
	if splitTrim(ctx, options, where) {
		return withoutEmpty(pieces)
	}
	return mk(pieces)
}

func regexSplitParts(ctx *actor.Context, s string, pattern *regexp.Regexp, options L[pair[actor.Atom, int64]], where string) L[string] {
	return mk(pattern.Split(s, splitParts(ctx, options, where)))
}

func regexSplitTrim(ctx *actor.Context, s string, pattern *regexp.Regexp, options L[pair[actor.Atom, bool]], where string) L[string] {
	pieces := pattern.Split(s, -1)
	if splitTrim(ctx, options, where) {
		return withoutEmpty(pieces)
	}
	return mk(pieces)
}

// ---- Key lists ----

// The List.key functions treat a list of pairs as a table keyed by the
// first element of each pair, as Elixir's do at position 0.

func keyIndex[K, V any](pairs []pair[K, V], key K, eq func(K, K) bool) int {
	for i, p := range pairs {
		if eq(p.E0, key) {
			return i
		}
	}
	return -1
}

func listKeyFind[K, V any](l L[pair[K, V]], key K, eq func(K, K) bool) Toption[pair[K, V]] {
	pairs := fwd(l)
	if i := keyIndex(pairs, key, eq); i >= 0 {
		return someOf(pairs[i])
	}
	return noneOf[pair[K, V]]()
}

func listKeyMember[K, V any](l L[pair[K, V]], key K, eq func(K, K) bool) bool {
	return keyIndex(fwd(l), key, eq) >= 0
}

func listKeyDelete[K, V any](l L[pair[K, V]], key K, eq func(K, K) bool) L[pair[K, V]] {
	pairs := fwd(l)
	i := keyIndex(pairs, key, eq)
	if i < 0 {
		return l
	}
	return mk(append(append([]pair[K, V]{}, pairs[:i]...), pairs[i+1:]...))
}

func listKeyReplace[K, V any](l L[pair[K, V]], key K, replacement pair[K, V], eq func(K, K) bool) L[pair[K, V]] {
	pairs := fwd(l)
	i := keyIndex(pairs, key, eq)
	if i < 0 {
		return l
	}
	out := append([]pair[K, V]{}, pairs...)
	out[i] = replacement
	return mk(out)
}

func listKeyStore[K, V any](l L[pair[K, V]], key K, replacement pair[K, V], eq func(K, K) bool) L[pair[K, V]] {
	pairs := fwd(l)
	if keyIndex(pairs, key, eq) < 0 {
		return mk(append(append([]pair[K, V]{}, pairs...), replacement))
	}
	return listKeyReplace(l, key, replacement, eq)
}

func listKeyTake[K, V any](l L[pair[K, V]], key K, eq func(K, K) bool) Toption[pair[pair[K, V], L[pair[K, V]]]] {
	pairs := fwd(l)
	i := keyIndex(pairs, key, eq)
	if i < 0 {
		return noneOf[pair[pair[K, V], L[pair[K, V]]]]()
	}
	rest := append(append([]pair[K, V]{}, pairs[:i]...), pairs[i+1:]...)
	return someOf(pair[pair[K, V], L[pair[K, V]]]{pairs[i], mk(rest)})
}

// listKeySort sorts by key, keeping pairs with equal keys in order.
func listKeySort[K, V any](l L[pair[K, V]], less func(K, K) bool) L[pair[K, V]] {
	pairs := append([]pair[K, V]{}, fwd(l)...)
	sort.SliceStable(pairs, func(i, j int) bool { return less(pairs[i].E0, pairs[j].E0) })
	return mk(pairs)
}

// ---- Find value with a default ----

func enumFindValueOr[A, B any](ctx *actor.Context, s iter.Seq[A], fallback B, f func(*actor.Context, A) Toption[B]) B {
	for x := range s {
		if found, ok := f(ctx, x).(Toption_c1[B]); ok {
			return found.F0
		}
	}
	return fallback
}

// ---- Supervised servers ----

// supervisorServer is the child specification of a server that a user
// supervisor runs under a name; see actor.ServerChild.
func supervisorServer[S, Q, R any](ctx *actor.Context, name actor.Atom, policy string, state S, handle func(*actor.Context, S, Q) struct {
	E0 S
	E1 R
}, where string) triple[string, string, func(*actor.Context, *actor.PID[struct{}]) int64] {
	if policy != "permanent" && policy != "transient" && policy != "temporary" {
		failAt(ctx, where, "Supervisor.server: the restart policy is \"permanent\", \"transient\" or \"temporary\", not "+strconv.Quote(policy))
	}
	return triple[string, string, func(*actor.Context, *actor.PID[struct{}]) int64]{string(name), policy, actor.ServerChild[S, Q, R, struct{}](string(name), state, handle, where)}
}
